<script>
"use strict";
/* FlipTrace — one file, full stack: IndexedDB backend, internal REST router,
   blob-URL worker for the heavy analysis, and a hand-rolled UI on top.
   Written to be read: everything below is plain algorithms and DOM. */

/* The worker gets its own copy of the engine as a Blob-URL string; the main
   thread keeps the live copy right below it. Same source, two homes. */
const WORKER_SOURCE = "// FlipTrace analysis engine.\n// Pure algorithms only — no DOM, no network. The same source string runs inside\n// the worker thread and (as a fallback when workers are unavailable) on the main\n// thread, which is why everything lives in one closure.\nconst Engine = (() => {\n  const DAY = 864e5;\n\n  // ------------------------------------------------------------------ lexicons\n\n  const DEFAULT_LEXICON = {\n    commit: [\n      \"let's do\",\"let's go with\",\"let's use\",\"let's pick\",\"let's choose\",\"let's move\",\"let's shift\",\"decided\",\n      \"final answer\",\"final decision\",\"we're going with\",\"we're using\",\"we'll use\",\"we'll go with\",\n      \"i'll go with\",\"i'll take\",\"i'm choosing\",\"i'm going with\",\"it's settled\",\"settled on\",\"settling on\",\n      \"confirmed\",\"confirming\",\"locked in\",\"locking in\",\"locked in on\",\"booked\",\"booking\",\"signed up\",\n      \"signing up\",\"committed to\",\"committing to\",\"subscribed to\",\"subscribing to\",\"ordered\",\"ordering\",\n      \"choosing\",\"chose\",\"chosen\",\"picked\",\"picking\",\"going with\",\"gone with\",\"went with\",\"sticking with\",\n      \"sticking to\",\"rolling with\",\"decided on\",\"deciding on\",\"starting\",\"move to\",\"moving to\",\"shift to\",\n      \"shifting to\",\"rescheduling\",\"rescheduled\"\n    ],\n    reversal: [\n      \"actually\",\"changed my mind\",\"change of mind\",\"on second thought\",\"scratch that\",\"never mind\",\n      \"nevermind\",\"i take it back\",\"take it back\",\"back to\",\"switching to\",\"switch to\",\"switching\",\n      \"switched\",\"let's not\",\"cancel that\",\"cancelled\",\"canceling\",\"cancelling\",\"revert\",\"reverting\",\n      \"rolled back\",\"rolling back\",\"undo\",\"instead\",\"no wait\",\"new plan\",\"back on\",\"returned to\",\n      \"returning to\",\"switch it\"\n    ],\n    reject: [\n      \"let's not\",\"no to\",\"not doing\",\"pass on\",\"passing on\",\"veto\",\"scrapping\",\"scrap\",\"dropping\",\n      \"drop the\",\"ditch\",\"ditching\",\"killing\",\"kill the\",\"shelve\",\"shelving\",\"cancel\",\"forget it\",\n      \"forgetting\",\"against\",\"rejecting\",\"backing out\",\"abort\",\"aborting\",\"deleted\",\"delete\"\n    ],\n    hedge: [\n      \"maybe\",\"probably\",\"i think\",\"might\",\"possibly\",\"kinda\",\"sort of\",\"not sure\",\"unsure\",\"i guess\",\n      \"we could\",\"could\",\"leaning\",\"tempted\",\"on the fence\",\"torn\",\"hmm\",\"not convinced\",\"wondering if\",\n      \"should we\",\"or maybe\",\"i feel like\"\n    ]\n  };\n\n  // Reversal words that on their own (no option, no commit verb) are too weak to\n  // count as a decision — \"actually, let me check the docs\" is not a reversal.\n  const WEAK_REVERSALS = new Set([\"actually\",\"instead\",\"no wait\",\"new plan\",\"switch it\",\"back on\",\n    \"on second thought\",\"second thought\",\"changed my mind\",\"change of mind\",\"hold on\"]);\n\n  const STRONG_COMMITS = new Set([\"final answer\",\"final decision\",\"locked in\",\"it's settled\",\"signed up\",\"confirmed\"]);\n\n  // Seeded option vocabulary. Options that live in the same family strongly hint\n  // that two decisions belong to the same topic (React/Vue/Svelte etc). The\n  // vocabulary also grows at runtime from options that end up grouped together.\n  const FAMILIES = [\n    { label: \"framework choice\",  words: \"react vue svelte angular solid ember jquery htmx backbone knockout preact\" },\n    { label: \"diet plan\",         words: \"keto vegan paleo mediterranean carnivore vegetarian pescatarian fasting\" },\n    { label: \"meeting time\",      words: \"monday tuesday wednesday thursday friday saturday sunday morning afternoon evening 9am 10am 11am 12pm 1pm 2pm 3pm 4pm 5pm\" },\n    { label: \"logo color\",        words: \"teal magenta indigo blue green orange purple red navy black white gold pink lime violet\" },\n    { label: \"pricing plan\",      words: \"$9 $19 $29 $39 $49 $99 free basic standard premium starter pro tier\" },\n    { label: \"job offer\",         words: \"accept decline sign pass negotiate quit counter stay leave\" },\n    { label: \"hosting provider\",  words: \"aws gcp azure heroku vercel netlify fly fly.io digitalocean linode render\" },\n    { label: \"database\",          words: \"postgres postgresql mysql sqlite mongodb dynamo aurora\" },\n    { label: \"vendor\",            words: \"stripe paypal braintree mercury chase amex\" }\n  ];\n\n  const ALIASES = {\n    \"reactjs\":\"react\",\"react.js\":\"react\",\"react js\":\"react\",\"vuejs\":\"vue\",\"vue.js\":\"vue\",\"vue js\":\"vue\",\n    \"sveltekit\":\"svelte\",\"pg\":\"postgresql\",\"postgres\":\"postgresql\",\"tue\":\"tuesday\",\"tues\":\"tuesday\",\n    \"mon\":\"monday\",\"wed\":\"wednesday\",\"thu\":\"thursday\",\"fri\":\"friday\",\"sat\":\"saturday\",\"sun\":\"sunday\",\n    \"$19/mo\":\"$19\",\"$19mo\":\"$19\",\"$19.00\":\"$19\",\"$29/mo\":\"$29\",\"$29.00\":\"$29\",\"$39/mo\":\"$39\",\n    \"$39.00\":\"$39\",\"fly.io\":\"fly\",\"flyio\":\"fly\",\"accepting\":\"accept\",\"declining\":\"decline\",\n    \"declined\":\"decline\",\"signing\":\"sign\",\"signed\":\"sign\",\"passing\":\"pass\",\"staying\":\"stay\",\"leaving\":\"leave\"\n  };\n\n  const FAMILY_INDEX = (() => {\n    const map = new Map();\n    FAMILIES.forEach((f, i) => f.words.split(/\\s+/).forEach(w => {\n      const canon = ALIASES[w] || w;\n      if (!map.has(canon)) map.set(canon, i);\n    }));\n    return map;\n  })();\n  const familyOf = opt => {\n    if (opt == null) return null;\n    const c = canonOption(opt);\n    if (FAMILY_INDEX.has(c)) return FAMILY_INDEX.get(c);\n    const first = c.split(\" \")[0];\n    return FAMILY_INDEX.has(first) ? FAMILY_INDEX.get(first) : null;\n  };\n\n  const STOPWORDS = new Set((\"a,an,the,and,or,but,if,then,else,when,while,of,at,by,for,with,about,to,from,in,out,on,off,\" +\n    \"over,under,again,further,once,here,there,all,any,both,each,few,more,most,other,some,such,no,nor,not,only,own,same,\" +\n    \"so,than,too,very,can,will,just,should,now,is,are,was,were,be,been,being,have,has,had,having,do,does,did,doing,\" +\n    \"i,me,my,we,our,you,your,he,him,his,she,her,it,its,they,them,their,this,that,these,those,am,im,ive,id,youre,youve,\" +\n    \"dont,doesnt,didnt,wont,cant,couldnt,wouldnt,lets,us,ok,okay,yeah,yes,nope,nah,hey,hi,please,thanks,thank,thx,\" +\n    \"really,pretty,very,much,lot,bit,thing,things,stuff,kind,sort,like,would,could,also,still,even,back,go,going,gone,\" +\n    \"get,gets,got,make,makes,made,need,needs,want,wants,think,thinks,thought,say,says,said,tell,told,one,two,new,good,\" +\n    \"great,better,best,nice,cool,hmm,huh,well,got,gonna,wanna,lets,heres,whats,thats,im,ive,were,well,theyre,youve,\" +\n    \"team,guys,everyone,folks,man,dude,time,week,weeks,day,days,today,tomorrow,yesterday,month,months,year\").split(\",\"));\n\n  // Task board states that represent normal forward progress, not flip-flopping.\n  const STATUS_ORDER = [\"backlog\",\"to do\",\"todo\",\"in progress\",\"review\",\"done\",\"completed\",\"closed\",\"shipped\"];\n  const TERMINAL_STATES = new Set([\"done\",\"completed\",\"closed\",\"shipped\",\"cancelled\",\"deleted\"]);\n\n  // ------------------------------------------------------------------ text utils\n\n  // Lowercase, straighten quotes/dashes, drop apostrophes so \"let's\" == \"lets\".\n  // Keeps a map back to original character positions so matched phrases can be\n  // highlighted in the UI on the untouched sentence.\n  function normWithMap(text) {\n    let s = \"\", map = [];\n    const src = text.replace(/[’‘`]/g, \"'\").replace(/[–—]/g, \"-\").replace(/\\s+/g, \" \");\n    for (let i = 0; i < src.length; i++) {\n      const ch = src[i];\n      if (ch === \"'\") continue;\n      s += ch.toLowerCase();\n      map.push(i);\n    }\n    return { s, map };\n  }\n  const norm = t => normWithMap(t).s;\n\n  function splitSentences(text) {\n    return text.replace(/\\s+/g, \" \").trim()\n      .split(/(?<=[.!?…])\\s+(?=[A-Z0-9$\"'“(])|\\n+/)\n      .map(s => s.trim()).filter(Boolean);\n  }\n\n  // Gently chop common English suffixes. Not a real stemmer — just enough to make\n  // \"switching\"/\"switched\"/\"switches\" collide for the similarity math.\n  function stem(w) {\n    if (w.length > 4 && w.endsWith(\"ies\")) return w.slice(0, -3) + \"y\";\n    if (w.length > 5 && w.endsWith(\"sses\")) return w.slice(0, -2);\n    if (w.length > 3 && /[^sui]s$/.test(w) && !w.endsWith(\"ss\")) return w.slice(0, -1);\n    if (w.length > 5 && w.endsWith(\"ing\")) return w.slice(0, -3);\n    if (w.length > 4 && w.endsWith(\"ed\")) return w.slice(0, -2);\n    return w;\n  }\n\n  function tokenize(text) {\n    const out = [];\n    for (let tok of norm(text).split(/[^a-z0-9$+#.'-]+/)) {\n      tok = tok.replace(/^[.'-]+|[.'-]+$/g, \"\");\n      if (tok.length < 2 || STOPWORDS.has(tok)) continue;\n      out.push(stem(tok));\n    }\n    return out;\n  }\n\n  function hash32(str) {\n    let h = 2166136261 >>> 0;\n    for (let i = 0; i < str.length; i++) { h ^= str.charCodeAt(i); h = Math.imul(h, 16777619) >>> 0; }\n    return h.toString(36);\n  }\n\n  function canonOption(o) {\n    if (!o) return null;\n    let v = norm(o).replace(/^(the|a|an|our|my)\\s+/, \"\").trim();\n    v = v.replace(/[.!]+$/, \"\").trim();\n    return ALIASES[v] || v;\n  }\n\n  const canonPerson = p => (p || \"Unknown\").replace(/\\s+/g, \" \").trim();\n\n  // ------------------------------------------------------------------ date utils\n\n  const MONTHS = [\"jan\",\"feb\",\"mar\",\"apr\",\"may\",\"jun\",\"jul\",\"aug\",\"sep\",\"oct\",\"nov\",\"dec\"];\n  function parseDateAny(v, fallback) {\n    if (v == null || v === \"\") return fallback;\n    if (typeof v === \"number\") return v > 1e12 ? v : v * 1000;\n    const s = String(v).trim();\n    if (/^\\d+(\\.\\d+)?$/.test(s)) return +s > 1e12 ? +s : +s * 1000;\n    let m = s.match(/^(\\d{4})-(\\d{2})-(\\d{2})(?:[T ](\\d{1,2}):(\\d{2}))?/);\n    if (m) return new Date(+m[1], +m[2] - 1, +m[3], +(m[4] || 12), +(m[5] || 0)).getTime();\n    m = s.match(/^(\\d{1,2})\\/(\\d{1,2})\\/(\\d{4})$/);\n    if (m) return new Date(+m[3], +m[1] - 1, +m[2], 12).getTime();\n    const t = Date.parse(s);\n    return isNaN(t) ? fallback : t;\n  }\n  function humanGap(ms) {\n    if (ms == null || !isFinite(ms)) return \"unknown\";\n    if (ms < 36e5) return Math.max(1, Math.round(ms / 6e4)) + \" min\";\n    if (ms < DAY) return Math.round(ms / 36e5) + \"h\";\n    const d = Math.round(ms / DAY);\n    return d === 1 ? \"a day\" : d + \" days\";\n  }\n\n  // ------------------------------------------------------------------ option extraction\n\n  // Shared lazy capture: an option phrase, terminated by sentence end or by a\n  // common conjunction/preposition, so \"react for the frontend\" yields \"react\".\n  const TAIL_WORDS = \"but|because|since|instead|so|and|or|as|if|while|which|that|it's|its|it is|again|tho|though|on|in|at|after|before|during|until|with|from|to|this|next|last|for|into|onto|over|today|tonight|tomorrow\";\n  const CAP = \"([a-z0-9$][a-z0-9$.+#' -]*?)(?=\\\\s+(?:\" + TAIL_WORDS + \")\\\\b|[,.;!?…]|$)\";\n  // Order matters: the first pattern that yields a clean option wins.\n  const OPT_PATTERNS = [\n    [new RegExp(\"\\\\bback (?:to|on|with) \" + CAP), \"revert\"],\n    [new RegExp(\"\\\\breturn(?:ing|ed)? to \" + CAP), \"revert\"],\n    [new RegExp(\"\\\\brevert(?:ing)? (?:to|back to) \" + CAP), \"revert\"],\n    [new RegExp(\"\\\\b(?:switch(?:ing|ed)?|chang(?:e|ing|ed)|mov(?:e|ing|ed)|migrat(?:e|ing|ed)|pivot(?:ing|ed)?) (?:to|over to|back to) \" + CAP), \"switch\"],\n    [new RegExp(\"\\\\b(?:switch|move|shift|push)(?:ing|ed)? [a-z0-9 -]{2,30}? to \" + CAP), \"switch\"],\n    [new RegExp(\"\\\\b(?:lets|let us) (?:do|go with|use|pick|choose|try|roll with|move to|switch to) \" + CAP), \"commit\"],\n    [new RegExp(\"\\\\b(?:were|we are|well|we will|im|i will|ive|i have) (?:going with|using|doing|choosing|picking|settling on|switching to|moving to|locking in|committing to|rolling with|sticking with|going to try) \" + CAP), \"commit\"],\n    [new RegExp(\"\\\\b(?:go|goes|going|gone|went) with \" + CAP), \"commit\"],\n    // yes/no style options (offer decisions) before the generic \"going X\" form,\n    // but never swallow \"sign up for\" / \"pass on\" phrases\n    [new RegExp(\"\\\\b(?:(?:decided|going|planning|plan|want|wanted|prefer|preferring)? ?to )?(accept|accepting|decline|declining|sign(?!\\\\s+up)|pass(?!\\\\s+on)|quit|staying|stay|leaving|leave|counter)\\\\b\"), \"yesno\"],\n    [new RegExp(\"\\\\b(?:starting|adopting|trying|going|moving|switching) (?:to )?\" + CAP), \"switch\"],\n    [new RegExp(\"\\\\b(?:use|using|used) \" + CAP), \"commit\"],\n    [new RegExp(\"\\\\b(?:choose|choosing|chose|chosen|pick|picking|picked|decided on|deciding on|settled on|settling on|locked in on|locking in on|locked in|locking in|committing to|committed to|booked|booking|ordered|ordering|signed up for|subscribed to|subscribing to|rolling with|sticking with) \" + CAP), \"commit\"],\n    // \"X it is\" — capture cannot cross dashes, so \"Revert — Tuesday 10am it is\"\n    // yields \"tuesday 10am\" rather than the whole clause\n    [new RegExp(\"(?<![a-z0-9$-])([a-z0-9$][a-z0-9$.+#' ]*?) it is\\\\b\"), \"commit\"],\n    [new RegExp(\"\\\\bfinal (?:answer|decision)[:\\\\s]+\" + CAP), \"commit\"],\n    [new RegExp(\"\\\\b(?:no to|not doing|pass(?:ing)? on|drop(?:ping)?|scrap(?:ping)?|cancel(?:ling|ing)?|shelv(?:e|ing)|ditch(?:ing)?|kill(?:ing)?) (?:the |a |an |our |my )?\" + CAP), \"reject\"]\n  ];\n  // \"Keto again.\" — a bare option standing alone after a flip is its own signal.\n  const AGAIN_ONLY = /^([a-z0-9$][a-z0-9$.+#'-]*(?: [a-z0-9$.+#'-]+){0,2}) again\\b/;\n\n  const TAIL_NOISE = /\\s+(it is|it's|for now|for good|for real|again|today|tomorrow|yesterday|now|this week|next week|this time|this cycle|guys|team|everyone|folks|instead|as well|too|then|on (?:mon|tues|wednes|thurs|fri|satur|sun)day)$/i;\n  // Leading verb/article noise from loose captures, e.g. \"going with react\".\n  const HEAD_NOISE = /^(going with|gone with|went with|use|using|used|lets|let us|move to|moving to|switch to|switching to|shift to|shifting to|do|doing|try|trying|take|taking|pick|picking|choose|choosing|chose|chosen|decided on|deciding on|settled on|settling on|the|a|an|our|my|i|we|to|into|onto|for)\\s+/i;\n  // Captures that are pure filler are not options at all.\n  const STOP_OPTIONS = new Set([\"it\",\"that\",\"this\",\"them\",\"us\",\"him\",\"her\",\"one\",\"now\",\"then\",\"there\",\"here\",\n    \"something\",\"anything\",\"everything\",\"nothing\",\"too\",\"also\",\"well\",\"good\",\"fine\",\"great\",\"ok\",\"okay\",\"real\",\"due\",\"still\",\"already\"]);\n\n  function cleanOption(raw) {\n    if (!raw) return null;\n    let v = raw.toLowerCase().replace(/^[.'-]+|[.'-]+$/g, \"\").trim();\n    v = v.replace(/^(the|a|an|our|my)\\s+/, \"\");\n    for (let i = 0; i < 3 && HEAD_NOISE.test(v); i++) v = v.replace(HEAD_NOISE, \"\").trim();\n    for (let i = 0; i < 4 && TAIL_NOISE.test(v); i++) v = v.replace(TAIL_NOISE, \"\").trim();\n    if (!v || v.length < 2 || v.length > 26) return null;\n    if (STOP_OPTIONS.has(v)) return null;\n    const words = v.split(\" \");\n    if (words.length > 3) v = words.slice(0, 3).join(\" \");\n    return canonOption(v) || v;\n  }\n\n  function extractOption(n) {\n    for (const [re, kind] of OPT_PATTERNS) {\n      const m = n.match(re);\n      if (m) {\n        const opt = cleanOption(m[1]);\n        if (opt) return { option: opt, kind };\n      }\n    }\n    const m = n.match(AGAIN_ONLY);\n    if (m) {\n      const opt = cleanOption(m[1]);\n      if (opt) return { option: opt, kind: \"revert\" };\n    }\n    return null;\n  }\n\n  const CUE_WORDS = /\\b(framework|diet|logo|standup|meeting|pricing|offer|hosting|database|vendor|plan|color|tier|subscription)\\b/;\n\n  function extractSubject(n) {\n    let m = n.match(/\\bfor (?:the |our |my |a )?([a-z][a-z0-9 -]{1,26}?)(?=$|[,.;!?]|\\s(?:but|and|or|so|because|instead|to|with)\\b)/);\n    if (m) return m[1].trim();\n    m = n.match(CUE_WORDS);\n    return m ? m[1] : null;\n  }\n\n  // ------------------------------------------------------------------ decision extraction\n\n  function lexHits(n, lexicon) {\n    const hits = { commit: [], reversal: [], reject: [], hedge: [] };\n    for (const phrase of lexicon.commit) if (hasPhrase(n, phrase)) hits.commit.push(phrase);\n    for (const phrase of lexicon.reversal) if (hasPhrase(n, phrase)) hits.reversal.push(phrase);\n    for (const phrase of lexicon.reject) if (hasPhrase(n, phrase)) hits.reject.push(phrase);\n    for (const phrase of lexicon.hedge) if (hasPhrase(n, phrase)) hits.hedge.push(phrase);\n    return hits;\n  }\n\n  function hasPhrase(n, phrase) {\n    const p = norm(phrase);\n    let i = n.indexOf(p);\n    while (i !== -1) {\n      const before = n[i - 1], after = n[i + p.length];\n      const okBefore = !before || !/[a-z0-9$]/.test(before);\n      const okAfter = !after || !/[a-z0-9$]/.test(after);\n      if (okBefore && okAfter) return true;\n      i = n.indexOf(p, i + 1);\n    }\n    return false;\n  }\n\n  const REVERT_PHRASES = new Set([\"back to\",\"returned to\",\"returning to\",\"revert\",\"reverting\",\"rolled back\",\"rolling back\",\"i take it back\",\"take it back\",\"back on\"].map(norm));\n\n  function extractSentence(sentence, lexicon) {\n    const { s: n } = normWithMap(sentence);\n    const hits = lexHits(n, lexicon);\n    const signal = hits.commit.length + hits.reversal.length + hits.reject.length;\n    const bareAgain = AGAIN_ONLY.test(n);\n    if (!signal && !bareAgain) return null;\n\n    const opt = extractOption(n);\n    const revertish = opt?.kind === \"revert\" || hits.reversal.some(p => REVERT_PHRASES.has(norm(p))) || /\\bagain\\b/.test(n);\n\n    let polarity = null;\n    if (revertish && opt) polarity = \"revert\";\n    else if (opt?.kind === \"reject\") polarity = \"reject\";\n    else if ((hits.reversal.length > 0 || opt?.kind === \"switch\") && opt) polarity = \"switch\";\n    else if (hits.commit.length > 0 && opt) polarity = \"commit\";\n    else if (hits.reject.length > 0) polarity = \"reject\";\n    else if (hits.commit.length > 0) polarity = \"commit\"; // reinforcement, no new option\n    else if (opt && signal > 0) polarity = \"commit\";\n    else if (hits.reversal.length > 0 && hits.reversal.some(p => !WEAK_REVERSALS.has(norm(p)))) polarity = \"reject\";\n    if (!polarity) return null;\n\n    let conf = 0.42;\n    conf += 0.18 * Math.min(hits.commit.length, 2);\n    if (hits.commit.some(p => STRONG_COMMITS.has(norm(p)))) conf += 0.1;\n    conf += 0.1 * Math.min(hits.reversal.length, 2);\n    conf -= 0.11 * Math.min(hits.hedge.length, 3);\n    if (polarity === \"commit\" && !opt) conf -= 0.08;\n    if (bareAgain) conf = Math.max(conf, 0.65);\n    conf = Math.max(0.05, Math.min(0.98, conf));\n\n    return {\n      text: sentence,\n      subject: extractSubject(n),\n      option: opt ? opt.option : null,\n      rejectedOption: polarity === \"reject\" ? opt?.option : null,\n      polarity,\n      reinforce: polarity === \"commit\" && !opt,\n      confidence: Math.round(conf * 100) / 100,\n      matched: [...hits.commit.slice(0, 3), ...hits.reversal.slice(0, 3), ...hits.reject.slice(0, 2)].filter(Boolean),\n      hedges: hits.hedge.slice(0, 2)\n    };\n  }\n\n  function buildDecisions(messages, manualDecisions, lexicon) {\n    const out = [];\n    for (const m of messages) {\n      if (m.kind === \"task\") continue; // task history events arrive pre-extracted\n      splitSentences(m.text).forEach((sentence, si) => {\n        const d = extractSentence(sentence, lexicon);\n        if (!d) return;\n        out.push({\n          ...d,\n          id: \"d\" + hash32(m.sourceId + \"#\" + m.id + \"#\" + si),\n          ts: m.ts, person: canonPerson(m.person),\n          sourceId: m.sourceId, sourceName: m.sourceName || \"\", messageId: m.id\n        });\n      });\n    }\n    for (const d of manualDecisions || []) {\n      out.push({\n        id: d.id,\n        text: d.text, subject: d.subject || null,\n        option: d.option ? canonOption(d.option) : null,\n        rejectedOption: null, polarity: d.polarity || \"commit\",\n        confidence: d.confidence ?? 0.8, matched: d.matched || [\"manual entry\"],\n        hedges: [], ts: d.ts, person: canonPerson(d.person),\n        sourceId: d.sourceId || \"manual\", sourceName: d.sourceName || \"Manual entry\",\n        messageId: null, manual: d.origin !== \"task\", origin: d.origin || \"manual\"\n      });\n    }\n    return out.sort((a, b) => a.ts - b.ts);\n  }\n\n  // ------------------------------------------------------------------ parsers\n\n  function parseWhatsApp(raw, sourceId) {\n    const msgs = [];\n    const re = /^(\\d{1,2})[\\/.-](\\d{1,2})[\\/.-](\\d{2,4}),?\\s+(\\d{1,2}):(\\d{2})(?::(\\d{2}))?\\s*([ap]\\.?m\\.?)?\\s*[-–—]\\s*([^:]{1,48}?):\\s?(.*)$/gim;\n    // Guess day-first vs month-first from the lines that make it unambiguous.\n    let dayFirst = true, m, samples = [];\n    while ((m = re.exec(raw))) samples.push(m);\n    if (samples.some(s => +s[1] > 12) ) dayFirst = true;\n    else if (samples.some(s => +s[2] > 12)) dayFirst = false;\n    samples.forEach((s, i) => {\n      const d = dayFirst ? +s[1] : +s[2];\n      const mo = dayFirst ? +s[2] : +s[1];\n      let hh = +s[4];\n      const ap = (s[7] || \"\").replace(/\\./g, \"\");\n      if (ap === \"pm\" && hh < 12) hh += 12;\n      if (ap === \"am\" && hh === 12) hh = 0;\n      const year = s[3].length === 2 ? 2000 + +s[3] : +s[3];\n      const ts = new Date(year, mo - 1, d, hh, +s[5], +(s[6] || 0)).getTime();\n      msgs.push(mkMsg(sourceId, i, s[8].trim(), s[9].trim(), ts, \"chat\"));\n    });\n    return msgs;\n  }\n\n  function parseIsoChat(raw, sourceId) {\n    const msgs = [];\n    let i = 0;\n    for (const line of raw.split(/\\n/)) {\n      const m = line.match(/^[\\[(]?(\\d{4})-(\\d{1,2})-(\\d{1,2})[,\\s]+(\\d{1,2}):(\\d{2})(?::(\\d{2}))?[\\])]?\\s+([^:]{1,48}?):\\s?(.*)$/);\n      if (m) {\n        const ts = new Date(+m[1], +m[2] - 1, +m[3], +m[4], +m[5], +(m[6] || 0)).getTime();\n        msgs.push(mkMsg(sourceId, i++, m[7].trim(), m[8].trim(), ts, \"chat\"));\n      } else if (line.trim() && msgs.length && /^[^:]{1,48}:/.test(line) === false) {\n        // continuation line — append to previous message\n        msgs[msgs.length - 1].text += \" \" + line.trim();\n      }\n    }\n    return msgs;\n  }\n\n  // Slack-style copy/paste: a name + time header line, message on the next line(s).\n  function parseSlackCopy(raw, sourceId) {\n    const msgs = [];\n    const lines = raw.split(/\\n/);\n    let cur = null, i = 0;\n    const head = /^([A-Za-z][\\w .'-]{1,32}?)\\s+(\\d{1,2}:\\d{2})\\s*([ap]m)?\\s*(\\[([^\\]]+)\\])?$/i;\n    const dateIn = raw.match(/(jan|feb|mar|apr|may|jun|jul|aug|sep|oct|nov|dec)[a-z]*\\.?\\s+\\d{1,2},?\\.?\\s+(\\d{4})/i);\n    const baseY = dateIn ? +dateIn[2] : new Date().getFullYear();\n    const baseM = dateIn ? MONTHS.indexOf(dateIn[1].toLowerCase()) : new Date().getMonth();\n    const baseD = dateIn ? +raw.match(new RegExp(dateIn[1] + \"[a-z]*\\\\.?\\\\s+(\\\\d{1,2})\", \"i\"))[1] : 1;\n    for (const line of lines) {\n      const m = line.trim().match(head);\n      if (m) {\n        let [_, hh, mm] = m[2].split(\":\");\n        let h = +hh;\n        if ((m[3] || \"\").toLowerCase() === \"pm\" && h < 12) h += 12;\n        const ts = new Date(baseY, baseM, baseD, h, +mm).getTime() + (msgs.length * 60000);\n        cur = { person: m[1].trim(), ts };\n      } else if (cur && line.trim()) {\n        msgs.push(mkMsg(sourceId, i++, cur.person, line.trim(), cur.ts, \"chat\"));\n        cur.ts += 60000;\n      }\n    }\n    return msgs;\n  }\n\n  function parseGenericChat(raw, sourceId) {\n    const msgs = [];\n    let lastTs = Date.now() - 3600e3;\n    let i = 0;\n    for (const line of raw.split(/\\n/)) {\n      const m = line.match(/^([^:]{1,40}):\\s?(.+)$/);\n      if (!m) continue;\n      lastTs += 6e4;\n      msgs.push(mkMsg(sourceId, i++, m[1].trim(), m[2].trim(), lastTs, \"chat\"));\n    }\n    return msgs;\n  }\n\n  function parseChatJson(data, sourceId) {\n    const arr = Array.isArray(data) ? data : (data.messages || data.history || []);\n    return arr.map((m, i) => {\n      // prefer human names over slack user ids when an export carries both\n      const person = canonPerson(m.person || m.real_name || m.user_profile?.real_name || m.name || m.username || m.user || \"Unknown\");\n      const ts = parseDateAny(m.ts || m.time || m.date || m.timestamp, Date.now());\n      return mkMsg(sourceId, i, person, String(m.text || m.message || \"\"), ts, \"chat\", m.subject);\n    }).filter(m => m.text);\n  }\n\n  function parseEmails(raw, sourceId) {\n    const chunks = /^From /m.test(raw) ? raw.split(/\\n(?=From )/) : raw.split(/\\n(?=From: )/);\n    const msgs = [];\n    chunks.forEach((chunk, i) => {\n      if (!chunk.trim()) return;\n      const sep = chunk.match(/\\n\\s*\\n/);\n      const head = sep ? chunk.slice(0, sep.index) : chunk;\n      const body = sep ? chunk.slice(sep.index + sep[0].length) : \"\";\n      const grab = re => (head.match(re) || [])[1];\n      const from = grab(/^From:\\s*(.+)$/im) || \"Unknown\";\n      const dateStr = grab(/^Date:\\s*(.+)$/im);\n      const subject = (grab(/^Subject:\\s*(.+)$/im) || \"\").replace(/^re:\\s*/i, \"\");\n      let person = from;\n      const nm = from.match(/\"?([^\"<]+?)\"?\\s*<[^>]+>/);\n      if (nm) person = nm[1].trim();\n      else if (from.includes(\"@\")) person = from.split(\"@\")[0].replace(/[._]/g, \" \");\n      let text = body\n        .split(\"\\n\")\n        .filter(l => !/^\\s*>/.test(l))                       // strip quoted lines\n        .filter(l => !/^\\s*on .{3,120} wrote:\\s*$/i.test(l))  // strip \"On ... wrote:\" header\n        .join(\"\\n\");\n      text = text.replace(/\\n--\\s*\\n[\\s\\S]*$/, \"\");           // classic signature marker\n      text = text.replace(/\\n(sent from|get outlook|best regards,)[\\s\\S]*$/i, \"\");\n      text = text.trim();\n      if (!text) return;\n      msgs.push(mkMsg(sourceId, i, canonPerson(person), text, parseDateAny(dateStr, Date.now()), \"email\", subject));\n    });\n    return msgs;\n  }\n\n  // Tiny CSV reader — quotes, escaped quotes, newlines inside quotes.\n  function parseCSV(text) {\n    const rows = [];\n    let row = [], cell = \"\", inQ = false;\n    for (let i = 0; i < text.length; i++) {\n      const c = text[i];\n      if (inQ) {\n        if (c === '\"') { if (text[i + 1] === '\"') { cell += '\"'; i++; } else inQ = false; }\n        else cell += c;\n      } else if (c === '\"') inQ = true;\n      else if (c === \",\") { row.push(cell); cell = \"\"; }\n      else if (c === \"\\n\" || c === \"\\r\") {\n        if (c === \"\\r\" && text[i + 1] === \"\\n\") i++;\n        row.push(cell); cell = \"\";\n        if (row.some(x => x !== \"\")) rows.push(row);\n        row = [];\n      } else cell += c;\n    }\n    row.push(cell);\n    if (row.some(x => x !== \"\")) rows.push(row);\n    return rows;\n  }\n\n  const pick = (obj, ...keys) => {\n    for (const k of keys) {\n      const hit = Object.keys(obj).find(ok => ok.toLowerCase().replace(/[\\s_]/g, \"\") === k);\n      if (hit != null && obj[hit] !== \"\" && obj[hit] != null) return obj[hit];\n    }\n    return undefined;\n  };\n\n  function taskEventDecision(sourceId, n, task, h, assignee) {\n    const field = String(pick(h, \"field\", \"type\", \"action\") || \"\").toLowerCase();\n    const from = String(pick(h, \"from\", \"oldvalue\", \"old\") ?? \"\");\n    const to = String(pick(h, \"to\", \"value\", \"newvalue\", \"new\") ?? \"\");\n    const at = parseDateAny(pick(h, \"at\", \"date\", \"ts\", \"when\", \"time\"), parseDateAny(pick(task, \"updated\", \"updatedat\"), Date.now()));\n    const title = String(pick(task, \"title\", \"name\", \"task\", \"summary\") || \"Untitled task\");\n    let option = null, polarity = \"commit\", text = \"\", matched = [\"task change: \" + (field || \"update\")];\n    if (/due/.test(field)) {\n      option = canonOption(to); polarity = \"commit\";\n      text = `Moved due date of “${title}” from ${from || \"unplanned\"} to ${to}`;\n    } else if (/status/.test(field)) {\n      option = canonOption(to); polarity = \"commit\";\n      text = `Changed status of “${title}” from ${from} to ${to}`;\n    } else if (/priority/.test(field)) {\n      option = canonOption(to); polarity = \"commit\";\n      text = `Changed priority of “${title}” from ${from} to ${to}`;\n    } else if (/delete|remove/.test(field)) {\n      polarity = \"reject\"; option = null;\n      text = `Deleted task “${title}”`;\n      matched = [\"task deleted\"];\n    } else if (/recreat|restor|reopen/.test(field)) {\n      polarity = \"revert\"; option = canonOption(title);\n      text = `Recreated task “${title}” after deletion`;\n      matched = [\"task recreated\"];\n    } else if (to) {\n      option = canonOption(to); polarity = \"commit\";\n      text = `Changed ${field || \"field\"} of “${title}” from ${from} to ${to}`;\n    } else return null;\n    return {\n      id: \"d\" + hash32(sourceId + \"#\" + n), ts: at, person: canonPerson(assignee),\n      text, subject: canonOption(title), option, rejectedOption: null,\n      polarity, confidence: 0.75, matched, hedges: [],\n      sourceId, sourceName: \"\", messageId: null, manual: false, origin: \"task\"\n    };\n  }\n\n  function parseTasks(rawOrData, sourceId) {\n    let tasks = null;\n    if (typeof rawOrData === \"string\") {\n      const t = rawOrData.trim();\n      if (t[0] === \"[\" || t[0] === \"{\") {\n        try { const j = JSON.parse(t); tasks = Array.isArray(j) ? j : (j.tasks || [j]); } catch { tasks = null; }\n      }\n      if (!tasks) {\n        const rows = parseCSV(t);\n        if (rows.length < 2) return { messages: [], taskDecisions: [] };\n        const header = rows[0].map(h => h.trim());\n        tasks = rows.slice(1).map(r => Object.fromEntries(header.map((h, i) => [h, r[i] ?? \"\"])));\n      }\n    } else tasks = Array.isArray(rawOrData) ? rawOrData : (rawOrData?.tasks || [rawOrData]);\n\n    const messages = [], taskDecisions = [];\n    let n = 0, mi = 0;\n    for (const task of tasks) {\n      const title = String(pick(task, \"title\", \"name\", \"task\", \"summary\") || \"Untitled task\");\n      const assignee = pick(task, \"assignee\", \"owner\", \"person\", \"user\") || \"Task tracker\";\n      const created = parseDateAny(pick(task, \"created\", \"createdat\", \"createdat\", \"datecreated\"), Date.now());\n      messages.push(mkMsg(sourceId, mi++, assignee, `Created task “${title}”`, created, \"task\"));\n      let history = pick(task, \"history\", \"changes\", \"changelog\", \"activity\");\n      if (typeof history === \"string\") { try { history = JSON.parse(history); } catch { history = []; } }\n      for (const h of Array.isArray(history) ? history : []) {\n        const d = taskEventDecision(sourceId, n++, task, h, assignee);\n        if (!d) continue;\n        d.sourceName = \"\";\n        taskDecisions.push(d);\n        messages.push(mkMsg(sourceId, mi++, assignee, d.text, d.ts, \"task\"));\n      }\n    }\n    return { messages, taskDecisions };\n  }\n\n  const mkMsg = (sourceId, i, person, text, ts, kind, subject) => ({\n    id: \"m\" + hash32(sourceId + \"#\" + i),\n    sourceId, person: canonPerson(person), text, ts, kind,\n    subject: subject || null\n  });\n\n  function detectFormat(raw) {\n    const t = (typeof raw === \"string\" ? raw : \"\").trim();\n    if (/^[[{]/.test(t)) {\n      try {\n        const j = JSON.parse(t);\n        const arr = Array.isArray(j) ? j : (j.tasks || j.messages || j.history || []);\n        const first = arr[0] || {};\n        if (Array.isArray(arr) && (first.title || first.status || first.history || first.changes)) return \"tasks\";\n        if (Array.isArray(arr) && (first.text || first.message)) return \"chat-json\";\n      } catch { /* fall through */ }\n    }\n    if (/^(From |From:|Date:|Subject:)/m.test(t) && /(wrote:|@|To:)/i.test(t)) return \"email\";\n    if (/^title[ ,]|^name[ ,]|,status,|^task,/i.test(t) && /,/.test(t) && /\\n/.test(t)) return \"tasks\";\n    if (/\\d{1,2}[\\/.-]\\d{1,2}[\\/.-]\\d{2,4},?\\s+\\d{1,2}:\\d{2}/.test(t)) return \"whatsapp\";\n    if (/^[\\[(]?\\d{4}-\\d{1,2}-\\d{1,2}[\\])?,\\s]/m.test(t)) return \"iso-chat\";\n    if (/^[A-Za-z][\\w .'-]{1,32}?\\s+\\d{1,2}:\\d{2}\\s*[ap]m?\\b/im.test(t)) return \"slack-copy\";\n    if (/^[^:\\n]{1,40}:\\s*\\S/m.test(t)) return \"generic-chat\";\n    return \"unknown\";\n  }\n\n  function parseRaw(raw, opts) {\n    const sourceId = opts.sourceId;\n    const hint = opts.kindHint;\n    let format = detectFormat(raw);\n    if (hint === \"tasks\" && (format === \"unknown\" || format === \"chat-json\")) format = \"tasks\";\n    if (hint === \"chat\" && format === \"unknown\") format = \"generic-chat\";\n    if (hint === \"email\" && format === \"unknown\") format = \"email\";\n\n    if (format === \"tasks\") {\n      let data = raw;\n      if (typeof raw === \"string\" && /^[[{]/.test(raw.trim())) { try { data = JSON.parse(raw); } catch { /* keep string */ } }\n      const { messages, taskDecisions } = parseTasks(data, sourceId);\n      return { format, kind: \"tasks\", messages, taskDecisions };\n    }\n    if (format === \"chat-json\") {\n      let messages = [];\n      try { messages = parseChatJson(JSON.parse(raw), sourceId); } catch { /* bad json */ }\n      return { format, kind: \"chat\", messages, taskDecisions: [] };\n    }\n    if (format === \"email\") return { format, kind: \"email\", messages: parseEmails(raw, sourceId), taskDecisions: [] };\n    if (format === \"whatsapp\") return { format, kind: \"chat\", messages: parseWhatsApp(raw, sourceId), taskDecisions: [] };\n    if (format === \"iso-chat\") return { format, kind: \"chat\", messages: parseIsoChat(raw, sourceId), taskDecisions: [] };\n    if (format === \"slack-copy\") return { format, kind: \"chat\", messages: parseSlackCopy(raw, sourceId), taskDecisions: [] };\n    if (format === \"generic-chat\") return { format, kind: \"chat\", messages: parseGenericChat(raw, sourceId), taskDecisions: [] };\n    // last resort: treat the whole blob as one message from an unknown speaker\n    return { format: \"unknown\", kind: \"chat\", messages: raw.trim() ? [mkMsg(sourceId, 0, \"Unknown\", raw.trim(), Date.now(), \"chat\")] : [], taskDecisions: [] };\n  }\n\n  // ------------------------------------------------------------------ topic grouping\n\n  function tfMap(tokens, boost = {}) {\n    const tf = {};\n    for (const t of tokens) tf[t] = (tf[t] || 0) + 1;\n    for (const [t, w] of Object.entries(boost)) tf[t] = (tf[t] || 0) + w;\n    return tf;\n  }\n\n  function decisionTokens(d) {\n    const tokens = tokenize(d.text);\n    const boost = {};\n    if (d.subject) tokenize(d.subject).forEach(t => boost[t] = (boost[t] || 0) + 2);\n    const fam = familyOf(d.option);\n    if (fam != null) tokenize(FAMILIES[fam].label).forEach(t => boost[t] = (boost[t] || 0) + 2);\n    return { tokens, boost };\n  }\n\n  function buildVectors(decisions) {\n    const docs = decisions.map(d => {\n      const { tokens, boost } = decisionTokens(d);\n      return { id: d.id, tokens, tf: tfMap(tokens, boost) };\n    });\n    const df = {};\n    docs.forEach(doc => Object.keys(doc.tf).forEach(w => df[w] = (df[w] || 0) + 1));\n    const N = Math.max(1, docs.length);\n    docs.forEach(doc => {\n      const v = {};\n      for (const w in doc.tf) v[w] = doc.tf[w] * Math.log(1 + N / (1 + (df[w] || 0)));\n      doc.vec = v;\n      doc.top = Object.keys(v).sort((a, b) => v[b] - v[a]).slice(0, 12);\n    });\n    return docs;\n  }\n\n  function cosine(a, b) {\n    let dot = 0, na = 0, nb = 0;\n    for (const w in a) { na += a[w] * a[w]; if (b[w]) dot += a[w] * b[w]; }\n    for (const w in b) nb += b[w] * b[w];\n    return (na && nb) ? dot / Math.sqrt(na * nb) : 0;\n  }\n\n  function jaccard(A, B) {\n    let inter = 0;\n    for (const x of A) if (B.includes(x)) inter++;\n    return inter / Math.max(1, A.length + B.length - inter);\n  }\n\n  function optionAffinity(option, vocab) {\n    if (!option) return 0.4;\n    const fam = familyOf(option);\n    for (const v of vocab) {\n      if (v === option) return 0.95;\n      if (fam != null && familyOf(v) === fam) return 1;\n    }\n    return vocab.length ? 0.05 : 0.2;\n  }\n\n  function topicNameFor(d, doc) {\n    const fam = familyOf(d.option);\n    if (fam != null) return FAMILIES[fam].label;\n    if (d.subject && d.subject.length <= 24) return d.subject;\n    const tops = (doc?.top || []).filter(t => !/^\\d/.test(t)).slice(0, 2);\n    return tops.length ? tops.join(\" \") : \"untitled topic\";\n  }\n\n  function assignTopics(decisions, existingTopics, threshold, fullRecluster) {\n    const docs = buildVectors(decisions);\n    const byId = new Map(docs.map(d => [d.id, d]));\n    let topics;\n    if (fullRecluster || !existingTopics?.length) {\n      topics = [];\n    } else {\n      topics = existingTopics.map(t => ({\n        id: t.id, name: t.name, kws: { ...(t.kws || {}) },\n        optionVocab: [...(t.optionVocab || [])],\n        top: Object.keys(t.kws || {}).slice(0, 12),\n        created: t.created || Date.now(), count: 0\n      }));\n    }\n    const assignments = {};\n    const findTopic = (doc, d) => {\n      let best = null, bestS = 0;\n      for (const t of topics) {\n        const cos = cosine(doc.vec, t.kws);\n        const jac = jaccard(doc.top, t.top);\n        const aff = optionAffinity(d.option, t.optionVocab);\n        const s = 0.55 * cos + 0.2 * jac + 0.25 * aff;\n        if (s > bestS) { bestS = s; best = t; }\n      }\n      return bestS >= threshold ? best : null;\n    };\n    for (let i = 0; i < decisions.length; i++) {\n      const d = decisions[i], doc = byId.get(d.id);\n      const known = !fullRecluster && existingTopics?.length && existingAssignments.get?.(d.id);\n      if (known) { assignments[d.id] = known; const t = topics.find(t => t.id === known); if (t) { t.count++; } continue; }\n      const t = findTopic(doc, d);\n      if (t) {\n        assignments[d.id] = t.id; t.count++;\n        for (const w in doc.vec) t.kws[w] = (t.kws[w] || 0) * 0.985 + doc.vec[w];\n        if (d.option && !t.optionVocab.includes(d.option)) t.optionVocab.push(d.option);\n        t.top = Object.keys(t.kws).sort((a, b) => t.kws[b] - t.kws[a]).slice(0, 12);\n      } else if (!d.option && !d.manual) {\n        // reinforcement or contextless cancellation — only meaningful in context\n        assignments[d.id] = null;\n        continue;\n      } else {\n        const id = \"t\" + hash32(topicNameFor(d, doc) + \"#\" + d.id);\n        const nt = { id, name: topicNameFor(d, doc), kws: { ...doc.vec }, optionVocab: d.option ? [d.option] : [], top: doc.top, created: d.ts, count: 1 };\n        topics.push(nt);\n        assignments[d.id] = id;\n      }\n    }\n    return { topics, assignments };\n  }\n\n  // holder so assignTopics can respect stored assignments on incremental runs\n  let existingAssignments = new Map();\n  function assignTopicsIncremental(decisions, existingTopics, assignmentsIn, threshold) {\n    existingAssignments = new Map(Object.entries(assignmentsIn || {}));\n    const res = assignTopics(decisions, existingTopics, threshold, false);\n    existingAssignments = new Map();\n    return res;\n  }\n  function clusterFull(decisions, threshold) {\n    return assignTopics(decisions, [], threshold, true);\n  }\n\n  // Used by merge/split on the main thread to keep keyword weights honest.\n  function kwsOf(decisions) {\n    const docs = buildVectors(decisions);\n    const kws = {};\n    docs.forEach(doc => { for (const w in doc.vec) kws[w] = (kws[w] || 0) + doc.vec[w]; });\n    return kws;\n  }\n\n  // ------------------------------------------------------------------ reversal detection\n\n  function detectReversals(decisions, windowMs) {\n    const revs = [];\n    const seen = [];\n    let cur = null, lastCommitTs = null, lastCommitId = null;\n    let lastRejectTs = null;\n    for (const d of decisions) {\n      const o = d.option ? canonOption(d.option) : null;\n      if (d.polarity === \"commit\" || d.polarity === \"switch\") {\n        if (o) {\n          if (!cur && seen.includes(o)) {\n            revs.push(rev(d, null, o, \"boomerang\", d.ts - lastRejectTs, `Re-committed ${o} after cancelling it`));\n          } else if (cur && o !== cur) {\n            const gap = d.ts - lastCommitTs;\n            if (gap <= windowMs) {\n              const boom = seen.includes(o);\n              revs.push(rev(d, cur, o, boom ? \"boomerang\" : \"switch\", gap,\n                boom ? `Boomerang — back to ${o} after ${cur}` : `Option changed: ${cur} → ${o}`));\n            }\n          }\n          if (!seen.includes(o)) seen.push(o);\n          cur = o; lastCommitTs = d.ts; lastCommitId = d.id; lastRejectTs = null;\n        }\n      } else if (d.polarity === \"reject\") {\n        const target = d.rejectedOption ? canonOption(d.rejectedOption) : cur;\n        if (cur && (!target || target === cur)) {\n          const gap = d.ts - lastCommitTs;\n          if (gap <= windowMs) revs.push(rev(d, cur, null, \"reject\", gap, `Cancelled ${cur} committed ${humanGap(gap)} earlier`));\n          cur = null; lastRejectTs = d.ts;\n        }\n      } else if (d.polarity === \"revert\") {\n        if (o) {\n          if (cur && o !== cur) {\n            const gap = d.ts - lastCommitTs;\n            if (gap <= windowMs) {\n              const boom = seen.includes(o);\n              revs.push(rev(d, cur, o, boom ? \"boomerang\" : \"revert\", gap,\n                boom ? `Boomerang — back to ${o} after ${cur}` : `Reverted from ${cur} back to ${o}`));\n            }\n          } else if (!cur && lastRejectTs != null) {\n            revs.push(rev(d, null, o, \"boomerang\", d.ts - lastRejectTs, `Restored ${o} after it was cancelled`));\n          }\n          if (!seen.includes(o)) seen.push(o);\n          cur = o; lastCommitTs = d.ts; lastCommitId = d.id; lastRejectTs = null;\n        }\n      }\n    }\n    return revs;\n\n    function rev(d, from, to, type, gapMs, rule) {\n      const fwd = isTaskProgression(from, to);\n      if (fwd) return null;\n      return {\n        id: \"r\" + hash32(d.topicId + \"#\" + d.id + \"#\" + type),\n        topicId: d.topicId, type, from, to,\n        fromId: lastCommitId && type !== \"boomerang\" ? lastCommitId : d.id,\n        toId: d.id, person: d.person, ts: d.ts,\n        gapMs: isFinite(gapMs) ? Math.max(0, gapMs) : null, rule\n      };\n    }\n  }\n\n  // A task moving backlog → in progress → done is progress, not a flip.\n  function isTaskProgression(from, to) {\n    if (!from || !to) return false;\n    const a = STATUS_ORDER.indexOf(from), b = STATUS_ORDER.indexOf(to);\n    return a !== -1 && b !== -1 && b > a && TERMINAL_STATES.has(to);\n  }\n\n  // ------------------------------------------------------------------ scoring\n\n  const LEVELS = [\n    { max: 20, label: \"Stable\", idx: 0 },\n    { max: 40, label: \"Wobbly\", idx: 1 },\n    { max: 60, label: \"Restless\", idx: 2 },\n    { max: 80, label: \"Chaotic\", idx: 3 },\n    { max: 101, label: \"Full Pendulum\", idx: 4 }\n  ];\n  const levelOf = score => LEVELS.find(l => score < l.max);\n\n  function normEntropy(counts) {\n    const total = counts.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0);\n    if (!total || counts.length < 2) return 0;\n    let H = 0;\n    for (const c of counts) { const p = c / total; H -= p * Math.log(p); }\n    return H / Math.log(counts.length);\n  }\n\n  function scoreTopic(decisions, reversals) {\n    const flips = reversals.length;\n    const boomerangs = reversals.filter(r => r.type === \"boomerang\").length;\n    const gaps = reversals.map(r => r.gapMs).filter(g => g != null && isFinite(g) && g >= 0);\n    const avgGapMs = gaps.length ? gaps.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / gaps.length : null;\n    const minGapMs = gaps.length ? Math.min(...gaps) : null;\n    const minDays = minGapMs != null ? minGapMs / DAY : 90;\n\n    const optCounts = {};\n    decisions.forEach(d => { if (d.option) optCounts[d.option] = (optCounts[d.option] || 0) + 1; });\n    const entropy = normEntropy(Object.values(optCounts));\n\n    // alternation: of all the flips, how many swung back to something tried before\n    const oscillation = flips ? boomerangs / flips : 0;\n\n    // confidence drift: early third vs late third of the topic's decisions\n    let drift = 0;\n    if (decisions.length >= 4) {\n      const sorted = [...decisions].sort((a, b) => a.ts - b.ts);\n      const k = Math.max(1, Math.floor(sorted.length / 3));\n      const early = avg(sorted.slice(0, k).map(d => d.confidence));\n      const late = avg(sorted.slice(-k).map(d => d.confidence));\n      drift = Math.abs(late - early);\n    }\n    const hedgeRatio = decisions.length ? decisions.filter(d => d.hedges?.length).length / decisions.length : 0;\n\n    const flipF = Math.min(1, flips / 5);\n    const boomF = Math.min(1, boomerangs / 2.5);\n    const speedF = Math.min(1, 10 / (minDays + 4));\n    const entropyF = entropy;\n    const driftF = Math.min(1, drift * 2.2);\n    const score = Math.round(100 * Math.min(1, Math.max(0,\n      0.30 * flipF + 0.25 * boomF + 0.15 * speedF + 0.15 * oscillation + 0.10 * entropyF + 0.05 * driftF\n    )));\n    return {\n      flips, boomerangs, avgGapMs, minGapMs, oscillation, entropy,\n      confidenceDrift: Math.round(drift * 100) / 100, hedgeRatio: Math.round(hedgeRatio * 100) / 100,\n      score, level: levelOf(score).label, levelIdx: levelOf(score).idx,\n      factors: { flipF, boomF, speedF, oscillation, entropyF, driftF }\n    };\n  }\n\n  function personStats(decisions, reversals, topicScores) {\n    const people = {};\n    const topicOf = new Map((topicScores || []).map(t => [t.id, t]));\n    for (const d of decisions) {\n      const p = people[d.person] || (people[d.person] = {\n        person: d.person, decisions: 0, flips: 0, boomerangs: 0,\n        topics: new Map(), hedges: 0, confs: [], firstTs: Infinity, lastTs: 0,\n        flipHours: new Array(24).fill(0), flipDays: new Array(7).fill(0), gaps: []\n      });\n      p.decisions++; p.firstTs = Math.min(p.firstTs, d.ts); p.lastTs = Math.max(p.lastTs, d.ts);\n      if (d.hedges?.length) p.hedges++;\n      p.confs.push(d.confidence);\n      const tid = d.topicId;\n      if (tid) p.topics.set(tid, (p.topics.get(tid) || 0) + 1);\n    }\n    for (const r of reversals) {\n      const p = people[r.person];\n      if (!p) continue;\n      p.flips++;\n      if (r.type === \"boomerang\") p.boomerangs++;\n      const dt = new Date(r.ts);\n      p.flipHours[dt.getHours()]++;\n      p.flipDays[dt.getDay()]++;\n      if (r.gapMs != null && isFinite(r.gapMs)) p.gaps.push(r.gapMs);\n    }\n    const out = [];\n    for (const p of Object.values(people)) {\n      const minGap = p.gaps.length ? Math.min(...p.gaps) : null;\n      const flipRate = p.decisions ? p.flips / p.decisions : 0;\n      // weighted average of the topic scores this person drives\n      let wsum = 0, wtot = 0;\n      p.topics.forEach((cnt, tid) => { const t = topicOf.get(tid); if (t) { wsum += t.score * cnt; wtot += cnt; } });\n      const topicScore = wtot ? wsum / wtot : 0;\n      const score = Math.round(Math.min(100, Math.max(0,\n        0.6 * topicScore + 25 * Math.min(1, flipRate * 2) + 20 * Math.min(1, p.boomerangs / 2.5)\n      )));\n      out.push({\n        person: p.person, decisions: p.decisions, flips: p.flips, boomerangs: p.boomerangs,\n        topics: [...p.topics.keys()], topicShare: [...p.topics.entries()].map(([id, n]) => ({ id, n })),\n        flipRate: Math.round(flipRate * 100) / 100,\n        minGapMs: minGap, avgConf: Math.round(avg(p.confs) * 100) / 100,\n        hedgeRatio: p.decisions ? Math.round(p.hedges / p.decisions * 100) / 100 : 0,\n        score, level: levelOf(score).label, levelIdx: levelOf(score).idx,\n        flipHours: p.flipHours, flipDays: p.flipDays, firstTs: p.firstTs, lastTs: p.lastTs,\n        tags: behaviorTags(p, minGap, flipRate)\n      });\n    }\n    return out.sort((a, b) => b.score - a.score);\n  }\n\n  function behaviorTags(p, minGap, flipRate) {\n    const tags = [];\n    const night = p.flipHours.slice(21).concat(p.flipHours.slice(0, 5)).reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0);\n    if (night >= Math.max(2, p.flips * 0.4) && p.flips >= 2) tags.push({ id: \"night\", label: \"Night-time flipper\" });\n    if (p.flipDays[1] >= Math.max(2, p.flips * 0.4) && p.flips >= 2) tags.push({ id: \"monday\", label: \"Monday reverser\" });\n    if (minGap != null && minGap < 2 * DAY && p.flips >= 3) tags.push({ id: \"fast\", label: \"Fast flipper\" });\n    if (p.boomerangs >= 2) tags.push({ id: \"boom\", label: \"Boomerang brain\" });\n    if (flipRate >= 0.5 && p.decisions >= 4) tags.push({ id: \"serial\", label: \"Serial second-guesser\" });\n    if (p.hedges / Math.max(1, p.decisions) > 0.4) tags.push({ id: \"hedge\", label: \"Hedge lord\" });\n    if (p.flips === 0 && p.decisions >= 3) tags.push({ id: \"steady\", label: \"Steady hands\" });\n    if (!tags.length) tags.push({ id: \"balanced\", label: \"Balanced decider\" });\n    return tags.slice(0, 3);\n  }\n\n  const avg = arr => arr.length ? arr.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / arr.length : 0;\n\n  // ------------------------------------------------------------------ alerts\n\n  const ORDINALS = { 1: \"first\", 2: \"second\", 3: \"third\", 4: \"fourth\", 5: \"fifth\" };\n  const ordinal = n => ORDINALS[n] || n + \"th\";\n\n  function suggestAlerts(topicScores, reversals, existingKeys, settings) {\n    const alerts = [];\n    const byTopic = {};\n    reversals.forEach(r => { (byTopic[r.topicId] = byTopic[r.topicId] || []).push(r); });\n    for (const t of topicScores) {\n      const revs = (byTopic[t.id] || []).sort((a, b) => a.ts - b.ts);\n      if (revs.length < (settings.alertMinReversals ?? 2)) continue;\n      const latest = revs[revs.length - 1];\n      const key = t.id + \":\" + latest.id;\n      if (existingKeys.has(key)) continue;\n      const severity = (t.score >= (settings.alertCriticalScore ?? 70) || t.boomerangs >= 3) ? \"critical\" : \"warn\";\n      alerts.push({\n        id: \"a\" + hash32(key), key, topicId: t.id, reversalId: latest.id,\n        severity, ts: latest.ts,\n        title: `${t.name}: ${ordinal(revs.length)} flip detected`,\n        body: `${latest.person} — ${latest.rule}. ${t.name} is now ${t.level} (score ${t.score}).`,\n        read: false\n      });\n    }\n    return alerts;\n  }\n\n  // ------------------------------------------------------------------ orchestrator\n\n  // One call that walks the whole pipeline. progress(stage, pct) lets the worker\n  // stream status back to the UI while the heavy loops churn.\n  function analyze(payload, progress = () => {}) {\n    const { messages = [], manualDecisions = [], existingTopics = [], assignments = {}, settings = {}, mode = \"incremental\" } = payload;\n    const lex = { ...DEFAULT_LEXICON, ...(settings.lexicon || {}) };\n    for (const k of Object.keys(lex)) if (!Array.isArray(lex[k])) delete lex[k];\n    const threshold = settings.similarity ?? 0.3;\n    const windowMs = (settings.reversalWindowDays ?? 45) * DAY;\n\n    progress(\"extract\", 8, \"Scanning messages for decision language\");\n    const decisions = buildDecisions(messages, manualDecisions, lex);\n    progress(\"extract\", 34, `${decisions.length} decision sentences found`);\n\n    progress(\"group\", 40, \"Grouping decisions into topics\");\n    let grouped;\n    if (mode === \"full\" || !existingTopics.length) grouped = clusterFull(decisions, threshold);\n    else grouped = assignTopicsIncremental(decisions, existingTopics, assignments, threshold);\n    const { topics, assignments: outAssign } = grouped;\n    decisions.forEach(d => { d.topicId = outAssign[d.id] || null; });\n    // A bare \"scratch that\" with no option of its own almost certainly refers to\n    // whatever the same person last committed to — attach it to that topic so the\n    // cancellation lands in the right chain.\n    const lastByPerson = {};\n    for (const d of decisions) {\n      const prev = lastByPerson[d.person];\n      if (!d.option && !d.subject && d.polarity === \"reject\" && prev && prev.topicId && d.ts - prev.ts < 14 * DAY) {\n        d.topicId = prev.topicId;\n        outAssign[d.id] = prev.topicId;\n      }\n      lastByPerson[d.person] = d;\n    }\n    progress(\"group\", 62, `${topics.length} topics`);\n\n    progress(\"detect\", 66, \"Detecting reversals and boomerangs\");\n    const reversals = [];\n    const byTopic = new Map();\n    decisions.forEach(d => { if (d.topicId) { if (!byTopic.has(d.topicId)) byTopic.set(d.topicId, []); byTopic.get(d.topicId).push(d); } });\n    for (const [tid, ds] of byTopic) {\n      ds.sort((a, b) => a.ts - b.ts);\n      reversals.push(...detectReversals(ds, windowMs));\n    }\n    progress(\"detect\", 76, `${reversals.length} reversals`);\n\n    progress(\"score\", 80, \"Scoring instability\");\n    const topicScores = topics.map(t => {\n      const ds = byTopic.get(t.id) || [];\n      const rv = reversals.filter(r => r.topicId === t.id);\n      const s = scoreTopic(ds, rv);\n      return { id: t.id, name: t.name, decisionCount: ds.length, ...s, optionVocab: t.optionVocab };\n    });\n    const people = personStats(decisions, reversals, topicScores);\n    progress(\"score\", 92, \"Scores ready\");\n\n    progress(\"alerts\", 96, \"Checking alert thresholds\");\n    const existingKeys = new Set((payload.existingAlertKeys || []).map(a => a.key || a));\n    const alerts = suggestAlerts(topicScores, reversals, existingKeys, settings);\n    progress(\"done\", 100, \"Analysis complete\");\n\n    return {\n      decisions, topics: topics.map(t => ({\n        id: t.id, name: t.name, kws: t.kws, top: t.top,\n        optionVocab: t.optionVocab, created: t.created\n      })),\n      reversals, topicScores, people, alerts\n    };\n  }\n\n  return {\n    DAY, FAMILIES, DEFAULT_LEXICON, LEVELS, ALIASES,\n    norm, normWithMap, splitSentences, tokenize, stem, hash32,\n    canonOption, canonPerson, parseDateAny, humanGap, parseCSV,\n    detectFormat, parseRaw, buildDecisions, extractSentence,\n    clusterFull, assignTopicsIncremental, kwsOf, topicNameFor,\n    detectReversals, scoreTopic, personStats, suggestAlerts, analyze,\n    levelOf\n  };\n})();\n\n// Let the worker wrapper (and node tests) grab this without window shenanigans.\nif (typeof module !== \"undefined\" && module.exports) module.exports = Engine;\n\n/* Worker glue — the engine source gets prepended to this file inside the Blob.\n   runOp holds the logic so the main-thread fallback can execute the exact same\n   code when blob workers aren't available. */\nfunction runOp(op, payload, progress) {\n  payload = payload || {};\n  if (op === \"ingest\") {\n    progress(\"parse\", 3, \"Detecting source format\");\n    const parsed = Engine.parseRaw(payload.raw, { sourceId: payload.sourceId, kindHint: payload.kindHint });\n    progress(\"parse\", 8, parsed.format + \" — \" + parsed.messages.length + \" messages\");\n    const existing = payload.existing || {};\n    const messages = (existing.messages || []).concat(parsed.messages);\n    const preset = (existing.presetDecisions || []).concat(parsed.taskDecisions || []);\n    const result = Engine.analyze({\n      messages: messages,\n      manualDecisions: preset,\n      existingTopics: existing.topics || [],\n      assignments: existing.assignments || {},\n      settings: payload.settings || {},\n      mode: \"incremental\",\n      existingAlertKeys: existing.alertKeys || []\n    }, progress);\n    return { type: \"result\", parsed: { format: parsed.format, kind: parsed.kind, messages: parsed.messages, taskDecisions: parsed.taskDecisions }, result: result };\n  }\n  if (op === \"reanalyze\") {\n    const result = Engine.analyze(payload, progress);\n    return { type: \"result\", result: result };\n  }\n  if (op === \"rescore\") {\n    // grouping is untouched (manual merges survive); only downstream stages re-run\n    const decisions = payload.decisions || [];\n    const settings = payload.settings || {};\n    const windowMs = (settings.reversalWindowDays ?? 45) * Engine.DAY;\n    progress(\"detect\", 25, \"Re-detecting reversals\");\n    const byTopic = new Map();\n    decisions.forEach(function (d) {\n      if (!d.topicId) return;\n      if (!byTopic.has(d.topicId)) byTopic.set(d.topicId, []);\n      byTopic.get(d.topicId).push(d);\n    });\n    const reversals = [];\n    byTopic.forEach(function (ds) {\n      ds.sort(function (a, b) { return a.ts - b.ts; });\n      Engine.detectReversals(ds, windowMs).forEach(function (r) { reversals.push(r); });\n    });\n    progress(\"score\", 65, \"Scoring topics\");\n    const topicScores = (payload.topics || []).map(function (t) {\n      const ds = byTopic.get(t.id) || [];\n      const rv = reversals.filter(function (r) { return r.topicId === t.id; });\n      const s = Engine.scoreTopic(ds, rv);\n      return Object.assign({ id: t.id, name: t.name, decisionCount: ds.length, optionVocab: t.optionVocab || [] }, s);\n    });\n    const people = Engine.personStats(decisions, reversals, topicScores);\n    progress(\"alerts\", 90, \"Checking thresholds\");\n    const alerts = Engine.suggestAlerts(topicScores, reversals, new Set(payload.alertKeys || []), settings);\n    progress(\"done\", 100, \"Rescored\");\n    return { type: \"result\", result: { decisions: decisions, topics: payload.topics || [], reversals: reversals, topicScores: topicScores, people: people, alerts: alerts } };\n  }\n  throw new Error(\"Unknown op: \" + op);\n}\n\n// only wire the message handler when actually running as a worker\nif (typeof WorkerGlobalScope !== \"undefined\" && self instanceof WorkerGlobalScope) {\n  self.onmessage = function (e) {\n    const msg = e.data || {};\n    const post = function (type, extra) { self.postMessage(Object.assign({ id: msg.id, type: type }, extra || {})); };\n    const progress = function (stage, pct, label) { post(\"progress\", { stage: stage, pct: pct, label: label }); };\n    try {\n      post(\"result\", runOp(msg.op, msg.payload, progress));\n    } catch (err) {\n      post(\"error\", { error: String((err && err.message) || err) });\n    }\n  };\n}\n";

// FlipTrace analysis engine.
// Pure algorithms only — no DOM, no network. The same source string runs inside
// the worker thread and (as a fallback when workers are unavailable) on the main
// thread, which is why everything lives in one closure.
const Engine = (() => {
  const DAY = 864e5;

  // ------------------------------------------------------------------ lexicons

  const DEFAULT_LEXICON = {
    commit: [
      "let's do","let's go with","let's use","let's pick","let's choose","let's move","let's shift","decided",
      "final answer","final decision","we're going with","we're using","we'll use","we'll go with",
      "i'll go with","i'll take","i'm choosing","i'm going with","it's settled","settled on","settling on",
      "confirmed","confirming","locked in","locking in","locked in on","booked","booking","signed up",
      "signing up","committed to","committing to","subscribed to","subscribing to","ordered","ordering",
      "choosing","chose","chosen","picked","picking","going with","gone with","went with","sticking with",
      "sticking to","rolling with","decided on","deciding on","starting","move to","moving to","shift to",
      "shifting to","rescheduling","rescheduled"
    ],
    reversal: [
      "actually","changed my mind","change of mind","on second thought","scratch that","never mind",
      "nevermind","i take it back","take it back","back to","switching to","switch to","switching",
      "switched","let's not","cancel that","cancelled","canceling","cancelling","revert","reverting",
      "rolled back","rolling back","undo","instead","no wait","new plan","back on","returned to",
      "returning to","switch it"
    ],
    reject: [
      "let's not","no to","not doing","pass on","passing on","veto","scrapping","scrap","dropping",
      "drop the","ditch","ditching","killing","kill the","shelve","shelving","cancel","forget it",
      "forgetting","against","rejecting","backing out","abort","aborting","deleted","delete"
    ],
    hedge: [
      "maybe","probably","i think","might","possibly","kinda","sort of","not sure","unsure","i guess",
      "we could","could","leaning","tempted","on the fence","torn","hmm","not convinced","wondering if",
      "should we","or maybe","i feel like"
    ]
  };

  // Reversal words that on their own (no option, no commit verb) are too weak to
  // count as a decision — "actually, let me check the docs" is not a reversal.
  const WEAK_REVERSALS = new Set(["actually","instead","no wait","new plan","switch it","back on",
    "on second thought","second thought","changed my mind","change of mind","hold on"]);

  const STRONG_COMMITS = new Set(["final answer","final decision","locked in","it's settled","signed up","confirmed"]);

  // Seeded option vocabulary. Options that live in the same family strongly hint
  // that two decisions belong to the same topic (React/Vue/Svelte etc). The
  // vocabulary also grows at runtime from options that end up grouped together.
  const FAMILIES = [
    { label: "framework choice",  words: "react vue svelte angular solid ember jquery htmx backbone knockout preact" },
    { label: "diet plan",         words: "keto vegan paleo mediterranean carnivore vegetarian pescatarian fasting" },
    { label: "meeting time",      words: "monday tuesday wednesday thursday friday saturday sunday morning afternoon evening 9am 10am 11am 12pm 1pm 2pm 3pm 4pm 5pm" },
    { label: "logo color",        words: "teal magenta indigo blue green orange purple red navy black white gold pink lime violet" },
    { label: "pricing plan",      words: "$9 $19 $29 $39 $49 $99 free basic standard premium starter pro tier" },
    { label: "job offer",         words: "accept decline sign pass negotiate quit counter stay leave" },
    { label: "hosting provider",  words: "aws gcp azure heroku vercel netlify fly fly.io digitalocean linode render" },
    { label: "database",          words: "postgres postgresql mysql sqlite mongodb dynamo aurora" },
    { label: "vendor",            words: "stripe paypal braintree mercury chase amex" }
  ];

  const ALIASES = {
    "reactjs":"react","react.js":"react","react js":"react","vuejs":"vue","vue.js":"vue","vue js":"vue",
    "sveltekit":"svelte","pg":"postgresql","postgres":"postgresql","tue":"tuesday","tues":"tuesday",
    "mon":"monday","wed":"wednesday","thu":"thursday","fri":"friday","sat":"saturday","sun":"sunday",
    "$19/mo":"$19","$19mo":"$19","$19.00":"$19","$29/mo":"$29","$29.00":"$29","$39/mo":"$39",
    "$39.00":"$39","fly.io":"fly","flyio":"fly","accepting":"accept","declining":"decline",
    "declined":"decline","signing":"sign","signed":"sign","passing":"pass","staying":"stay","leaving":"leave"
  };

  const FAMILY_INDEX = (() => {
    const map = new Map();
    FAMILIES.forEach((f, i) => f.words.split(/\s+/).forEach(w => {
      const canon = ALIASES[w] || w;
      if (!map.has(canon)) map.set(canon, i);
    }));
    return map;
  })();
  const familyOf = opt => {
    if (opt == null) return null;
    const c = canonOption(opt);
    if (FAMILY_INDEX.has(c)) return FAMILY_INDEX.get(c);
    const first = c.split(" ")[0];
    return FAMILY_INDEX.has(first) ? FAMILY_INDEX.get(first) : null;
  };

  const STOPWORDS = new Set(("a,an,the,and,or,but,if,then,else,when,while,of,at,by,for,with,about,to,from,in,out,on,off," +
    "over,under,again,further,once,here,there,all,any,both,each,few,more,most,other,some,such,no,nor,not,only,own,same," +
    "so,than,too,very,can,will,just,should,now,is,are,was,were,be,been,being,have,has,had,having,do,does,did,doing," +
    "i,me,my,we,our,you,your,he,him,his,she,her,it,its,they,them,their,this,that,these,those,am,im,ive,id,youre,youve," +
    "dont,doesnt,didnt,wont,cant,couldnt,wouldnt,lets,us,ok,okay,yeah,yes,nope,nah,hey,hi,please,thanks,thank,thx," +
    "really,pretty,very,much,lot,bit,thing,things,stuff,kind,sort,like,would,could,also,still,even,back,go,going,gone," +
    "get,gets,got,make,makes,made,need,needs,want,wants,think,thinks,thought,say,says,said,tell,told,one,two,new,good," +
    "great,better,best,nice,cool,hmm,huh,well,got,gonna,wanna,lets,heres,whats,thats,im,ive,were,well,theyre,youve," +
    "team,guys,everyone,folks,man,dude,time,week,weeks,day,days,today,tomorrow,yesterday,month,months,year").split(","));

  // Task board states that represent normal forward progress, not flip-flopping.
  const STATUS_ORDER = ["backlog","to do","todo","in progress","review","done","completed","closed","shipped"];
  const TERMINAL_STATES = new Set(["done","completed","closed","shipped","cancelled","deleted"]);

  // ------------------------------------------------------------------ text utils

  // Lowercase, straighten quotes/dashes, drop apostrophes so "let's" == "lets".
  // Keeps a map back to original character positions so matched phrases can be
  // highlighted in the UI on the untouched sentence.
  function normWithMap(text) {
    let s = "", map = [];
    const src = text.replace(/[’‘`]/g, "'").replace(/[–—]/g, "-").replace(/\s+/g, " ");
    for (let i = 0; i < src.length; i++) {
      const ch = src[i];
      if (ch === "'") continue;
      s += ch.toLowerCase();
      map.push(i);
    }
    return { s, map };
  }
  const norm = t => normWithMap(t).s;

  function splitSentences(text) {
    return text.replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim()
      .split(/(?<=[.!?…])\s+(?=[A-Z0-9$"'“(])|\n+/)
      .map(s => s.trim()).filter(Boolean);
  }

  // Gently chop common English suffixes. Not a real stemmer — just enough to make
  // "switching"/"switched"/"switches" collide for the similarity math.
  function stem(w) {
    if (w.length > 4 && w.endsWith("ies")) return w.slice(0, -3) + "y";
    if (w.length > 5 && w.endsWith("sses")) return w.slice(0, -2);
    if (w.length > 3 && /[^sui]s$/.test(w) && !w.endsWith("ss")) return w.slice(0, -1);
    if (w.length > 5 && w.endsWith("ing")) return w.slice(0, -3);
    if (w.length > 4 && w.endsWith("ed")) return w.slice(0, -2);
    return w;
  }

  function tokenize(text) {
    const out = [];
    for (let tok of norm(text).split(/[^a-z0-9$+#.'-]+/)) {
      tok = tok.replace(/^[.'-]+|[.'-]+$/g, "");
      if (tok.length < 2 || STOPWORDS.has(tok)) continue;
      out.push(stem(tok));
    }
    return out;
  }

  function hash32(str) {
    let h = 2166136261 >>> 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < str.length; i++) { h ^= str.charCodeAt(i); h = Math.imul(h, 16777619) >>> 0; }
    return h.toString(36);
  }

  function canonOption(o) {
    if (!o) return null;
    let v = norm(o).replace(/^(the|a|an|our|my)\s+/, "").trim();
    v = v.replace(/[.!]+$/, "").trim();
    return ALIASES[v] || v;
  }

  const canonPerson = p => (p || "Unknown").replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();

  // ------------------------------------------------------------------ date utils

  const MONTHS = ["jan","feb","mar","apr","may","jun","jul","aug","sep","oct","nov","dec"];
  function parseDateAny(v, fallback) {
    if (v == null || v === "") return fallback;
    if (typeof v === "number") return v > 1e12 ? v : v * 1000;
    const s = String(v).trim();
    if (/^\d+(\.\d+)?$/.test(s)) return +s > 1e12 ? +s : +s * 1000;
    let m = s.match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})(?:[T ](\d{1,2}):(\d{2}))?/);
    if (m) return new Date(+m[1], +m[2] - 1, +m[3], +(m[4] || 12), +(m[5] || 0)).getTime();
    m = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})\/(\d{1,2})\/(\d{4})$/);
    if (m) return new Date(+m[3], +m[1] - 1, +m[2], 12).getTime();
    const t = Date.parse(s);
    return isNaN(t) ? fallback : t;
  }
  function humanGap(ms) {
    if (ms == null || !isFinite(ms)) return "unknown";
    if (ms < 36e5) return Math.max(1, Math.round(ms / 6e4)) + " min";
    if (ms < DAY) return Math.round(ms / 36e5) + "h";
    const d = Math.round(ms / DAY);
    return d === 1 ? "a day" : d + " days";
  }

  // ------------------------------------------------------------------ option extraction

  // Shared lazy capture: an option phrase, terminated by sentence end or by a
  // common conjunction/preposition, so "react for the frontend" yields "react".
  const TAIL_WORDS = "but|because|since|instead|so|and|or|as|if|while|which|that|it's|its|it is|again|tho|though|on|in|at|after|before|during|until|with|from|to|this|next|last|for|into|onto|over|today|tonight|tomorrow";
  const CAP = "([a-z0-9$][a-z0-9$.+#' -]*?)(?=\\s+(?:" + TAIL_WORDS + ")\\b|[,.;!?…]|$)";
  // Order matters: the first pattern that yields a clean option wins.
  const OPT_PATTERNS = [
    [new RegExp("\\bback (?:to|on|with) " + CAP), "revert"],
    [new RegExp("\\breturn(?:ing|ed)? to " + CAP), "revert"],
    [new RegExp("\\brevert(?:ing)? (?:to|back to) " + CAP), "revert"],
    [new RegExp("\\b(?:switch(?:ing|ed)?|chang(?:e|ing|ed)|mov(?:e|ing|ed)|migrat(?:e|ing|ed)|pivot(?:ing|ed)?) (?:to|over to|back to) " + CAP), "switch"],
    [new RegExp("\\b(?:switch|move|shift|push)(?:ing|ed)? [a-z0-9 -]{2,30}? to " + CAP), "switch"],
    [new RegExp("\\b(?:lets|let us) (?:do|go with|use|pick|choose|try|roll with|move to|switch to) " + CAP), "commit"],
    [new RegExp("\\b(?:were|we are|well|we will|im|i will|ive|i have) (?:going with|using|doing|choosing|picking|settling on|switching to|moving to|locking in|committing to|rolling with|sticking with|going to try) " + CAP), "commit"],
    [new RegExp("\\b(?:go|goes|going|gone|went) with " + CAP), "commit"],
    // yes/no style options (offer decisions) before the generic "going X" form,
    // but never swallow "sign up for" / "pass on" phrases
    [new RegExp("\\b(?:(?:decided|going|planning|plan|want|wanted|prefer|preferring)? ?to )?(accept|accepting|decline|declining|sign(?!\\s+up)|pass(?!\\s+on)|quit|staying|stay|leaving|leave|counter)\\b"), "yesno"],
    [new RegExp("\\b(?:starting|adopting|trying|going|moving|switching) (?:to )?" + CAP), "switch"],
    [new RegExp("\\b(?:use|using|used) " + CAP), "commit"],
    [new RegExp("\\b(?:choose|choosing|chose|chosen|pick|picking|picked|decided on|deciding on|settled on|settling on|locked in on|locking in on|locked in|locking in|committing to|committed to|booked|booking|ordered|ordering|signed up for|subscribed to|subscribing to|rolling with|sticking with) " + CAP), "commit"],
    // "X it is" — capture cannot cross dashes, so "Revert — Tuesday 10am it is"
    // yields "tuesday 10am" rather than the whole clause
    [new RegExp("(?<![a-z0-9$-])([a-z0-9$][a-z0-9$.+#' ]*?) it is\\b"), "commit"],
    [new RegExp("\\bfinal (?:answer|decision)[:\\s]+" + CAP), "commit"],
    [new RegExp("\\b(?:no to|not doing|pass(?:ing)? on|drop(?:ping)?|scrap(?:ping)?|cancel(?:ling|ing)?|shelv(?:e|ing)|ditch(?:ing)?|kill(?:ing)?) (?:the |a |an |our |my )?" + CAP), "reject"]
  ];
  // "Keto again." — a bare option standing alone after a flip is its own signal.
  const AGAIN_ONLY = /^([a-z0-9$][a-z0-9$.+#'-]*(?: [a-z0-9$.+#'-]+){0,2}) again\b/;

  const TAIL_NOISE = /\s+(it is|it's|for now|for good|for real|again|today|tomorrow|yesterday|now|this week|next week|this time|this cycle|guys|team|everyone|folks|instead|as well|too|then|on (?:mon|tues|wednes|thurs|fri|satur|sun)day)$/i;
  // Leading verb/article noise from loose captures, e.g. "going with react".
  const HEAD_NOISE = /^(going with|gone with|went with|use|using|used|lets|let us|move to|moving to|switch to|switching to|shift to|shifting to|do|doing|try|trying|take|taking|pick|picking|choose|choosing|chose|chosen|decided on|deciding on|settled on|settling on|the|a|an|our|my|i|we|to|into|onto|for)\s+/i;
  // Captures that are pure filler are not options at all.
  const STOP_OPTIONS = new Set(["it","that","this","them","us","him","her","one","now","then","there","here",
    "something","anything","everything","nothing","too","also","well","good","fine","great","ok","okay","real","due","still","already"]);

  function cleanOption(raw) {
    if (!raw) return null;
    let v = raw.toLowerCase().replace(/^[.'-]+|[.'-]+$/g, "").trim();
    v = v.replace(/^(the|a|an|our|my)\s+/, "");
    for (let i = 0; i < 3 && HEAD_NOISE.test(v); i++) v = v.replace(HEAD_NOISE, "").trim();
    for (let i = 0; i < 4 && TAIL_NOISE.test(v); i++) v = v.replace(TAIL_NOISE, "").trim();
    if (!v || v.length < 2 || v.length > 26) return null;
    if (STOP_OPTIONS.has(v)) return null;
    const words = v.split(" ");
    if (words.length > 3) v = words.slice(0, 3).join(" ");
    return canonOption(v) || v;
  }

  function extractOption(n) {
    for (const [re, kind] of OPT_PATTERNS) {
      const m = n.match(re);
      if (m) {
        const opt = cleanOption(m[1]);
        if (opt) return { option: opt, kind };
      }
    }
    const m = n.match(AGAIN_ONLY);
    if (m) {
      const opt = cleanOption(m[1]);
      if (opt) return { option: opt, kind: "revert" };
    }
    return null;
  }

  const CUE_WORDS = /\b(framework|diet|logo|standup|meeting|pricing|offer|hosting|database|vendor|plan|color|tier|subscription)\b/;

  function extractSubject(n) {
    let m = n.match(/\bfor (?:the |our |my |a )?([a-z][a-z0-9 -]{1,26}?)(?=$|[,.;!?]|\s(?:but|and|or|so|because|instead|to|with)\b)/);
    if (m) return m[1].trim();
    m = n.match(CUE_WORDS);
    return m ? m[1] : null;
  }

  // ------------------------------------------------------------------ decision extraction

  function lexHits(n, lexicon) {
    const hits = { commit: [], reversal: [], reject: [], hedge: [] };
    for (const phrase of lexicon.commit) if (hasPhrase(n, phrase)) hits.commit.push(phrase);
    for (const phrase of lexicon.reversal) if (hasPhrase(n, phrase)) hits.reversal.push(phrase);
    for (const phrase of lexicon.reject) if (hasPhrase(n, phrase)) hits.reject.push(phrase);
    for (const phrase of lexicon.hedge) if (hasPhrase(n, phrase)) hits.hedge.push(phrase);
    return hits;
  }

  function hasPhrase(n, phrase) {
    const p = norm(phrase);
    let i = n.indexOf(p);
    while (i !== -1) {
      const before = n[i - 1], after = n[i + p.length];
      const okBefore = !before || !/[a-z0-9$]/.test(before);
      const okAfter = !after || !/[a-z0-9$]/.test(after);
      if (okBefore && okAfter) return true;
      i = n.indexOf(p, i + 1);
    }
    return false;
  }

  const REVERT_PHRASES = new Set(["back to","returned to","returning to","revert","reverting","rolled back","rolling back","i take it back","take it back","back on"].map(norm));

  function extractSentence(sentence, lexicon) {
    const { s: n } = normWithMap(sentence);
    const hits = lexHits(n, lexicon);
    const signal = hits.commit.length + hits.reversal.length + hits.reject.length;
    const bareAgain = AGAIN_ONLY.test(n);
    if (!signal && !bareAgain) return null;

    const opt = extractOption(n);
    const revertish = opt?.kind === "revert" || hits.reversal.some(p => REVERT_PHRASES.has(norm(p))) || /\bagain\b/.test(n);

    let polarity = null;
    if (revertish && opt) polarity = "revert";
    else if (opt?.kind === "reject") polarity = "reject";
    else if ((hits.reversal.length > 0 || opt?.kind === "switch") && opt) polarity = "switch";
    else if (hits.commit.length > 0 && opt) polarity = "commit";
    else if (hits.reject.length > 0) polarity = "reject";
    else if (hits.commit.length > 0) polarity = "commit"; // reinforcement, no new option
    else if (opt && signal > 0) polarity = "commit";
    else if (hits.reversal.length > 0 && hits.reversal.some(p => !WEAK_REVERSALS.has(norm(p)))) polarity = "reject";
    if (!polarity) return null;

    let conf = 0.42;
    conf += 0.18 * Math.min(hits.commit.length, 2);
    if (hits.commit.some(p => STRONG_COMMITS.has(norm(p)))) conf += 0.1;
    conf += 0.1 * Math.min(hits.reversal.length, 2);
    conf -= 0.11 * Math.min(hits.hedge.length, 3);
    if (polarity === "commit" && !opt) conf -= 0.08;
    if (bareAgain) conf = Math.max(conf, 0.65);
    conf = Math.max(0.05, Math.min(0.98, conf));

    return {
      text: sentence,
      subject: extractSubject(n),
      option: opt ? opt.option : null,
      rejectedOption: polarity === "reject" ? opt?.option : null,
      polarity,
      reinforce: polarity === "commit" && !opt,
      confidence: Math.round(conf * 100) / 100,
      matched: [...hits.commit.slice(0, 3), ...hits.reversal.slice(0, 3), ...hits.reject.slice(0, 2)].filter(Boolean),
      hedges: hits.hedge.slice(0, 2)
    };
  }

  function buildDecisions(messages, manualDecisions, lexicon) {
    const out = [];
    for (const m of messages) {
      if (m.kind === "task") continue; // task history events arrive pre-extracted
      splitSentences(m.text).forEach((sentence, si) => {
        const d = extractSentence(sentence, lexicon);
        if (!d) return;
        out.push({
          ...d,
          id: "d" + hash32(m.sourceId + "#" + m.id + "#" + si),
          ts: m.ts, person: canonPerson(m.person),
          sourceId: m.sourceId, sourceName: m.sourceName || "", messageId: m.id
        });
      });
    }
    for (const d of manualDecisions || []) {
      out.push({
        id: d.id,
        text: d.text, subject: d.subject || null,
        option: d.option ? canonOption(d.option) : null,
        rejectedOption: null, polarity: d.polarity || "commit",
        confidence: d.confidence ?? 0.8, matched: d.matched || ["manual entry"],
        hedges: [], ts: d.ts, person: canonPerson(d.person),
        sourceId: d.sourceId || "manual", sourceName: d.sourceName || "Manual entry",
        messageId: null, manual: d.origin !== "task", origin: d.origin || "manual"
      });
    }
    return out.sort((a, b) => a.ts - b.ts);
  }

  // ------------------------------------------------------------------ parsers

  function parseWhatsApp(raw, sourceId) {
    const msgs = [];
    const re = /^(\d{1,2})[\/.-](\d{1,2})[\/.-](\d{2,4}),?\s+(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})(?::(\d{2}))?\s*([ap]\.?m\.?)?\s*[-–—]\s*([^:]{1,48}?):\s?(.*)$/gim;
    // Guess day-first vs month-first from the lines that make it unambiguous.
    let dayFirst = true, m, samples = [];
    while ((m = re.exec(raw))) samples.push(m);
    if (samples.some(s => +s[1] > 12) ) dayFirst = true;
    else if (samples.some(s => +s[2] > 12)) dayFirst = false;
    samples.forEach((s, i) => {
      const d = dayFirst ? +s[1] : +s[2];
      const mo = dayFirst ? +s[2] : +s[1];
      let hh = +s[4];
      const ap = (s[7] || "").replace(/\./g, "");
      if (ap === "pm" && hh < 12) hh += 12;
      if (ap === "am" && hh === 12) hh = 0;
      const year = s[3].length === 2 ? 2000 + +s[3] : +s[3];
      const ts = new Date(year, mo - 1, d, hh, +s[5], +(s[6] || 0)).getTime();
      msgs.push(mkMsg(sourceId, i, s[8].trim(), s[9].trim(), ts, "chat"));
    });
    return msgs;
  }

  function parseIsoChat(raw, sourceId) {
    const msgs = [];
