    let i = 0;
    for (const line of raw.split(/\n/)) {
      const m = line.match(/^[\[(]?(\d{4})-(\d{1,2})-(\d{1,2})[,\s]+(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})(?::(\d{2}))?[\])]?\s+([^:]{1,48}?):\s?(.*)$/);
      if (m) {
        const ts = new Date(+m[1], +m[2] - 1, +m[3], +m[4], +m[5], +(m[6] || 0)).getTime();
        msgs.push(mkMsg(sourceId, i++, m[7].trim(), m[8].trim(), ts, "chat"));
      } else if (line.trim() && msgs.length && /^[^:]{1,48}:/.test(line) === false) {
        // continuation line — append to previous message
        msgs[msgs.length - 1].text += " " + line.trim();
      }
    }
    return msgs;
  }

  // Slack-style copy/paste: a name + time header line, message on the next line(s).
  function parseSlackCopy(raw, sourceId) {
    const msgs = [];
    const lines = raw.split(/\n/);
    let cur = null, i = 0;
    const head = /^([A-Za-z][\w .'-]{1,32}?)\s+(\d{1,2}:\d{2})\s*([ap]m)?\s*(\[([^\]]+)\])?$/i;
    const dateIn = raw.match(/(jan|feb|mar|apr|may|jun|jul|aug|sep|oct|nov|dec)[a-z]*\.?\s+\d{1,2},?\.?\s+(\d{4})/i);
    const baseY = dateIn ? +dateIn[2] : new Date().getFullYear();
    const baseM = dateIn ? MONTHS.indexOf(dateIn[1].toLowerCase()) : new Date().getMonth();
    const baseD = dateIn ? +raw.match(new RegExp(dateIn[1] + "[a-z]*\\.?\\s+(\\d{1,2})", "i"))[1] : 1;
    for (const line of lines) {
      const m = line.trim().match(head);
      if (m) {
        let [_, hh, mm] = m[2].split(":");
        let h = +hh;
        if ((m[3] || "").toLowerCase() === "pm" && h < 12) h += 12;
        const ts = new Date(baseY, baseM, baseD, h, +mm).getTime() + (msgs.length * 60000);
        cur = { person: m[1].trim(), ts };
      } else if (cur && line.trim()) {
        msgs.push(mkMsg(sourceId, i++, cur.person, line.trim(), cur.ts, "chat"));
        cur.ts += 60000;
      }
    }
    return msgs;
  }

  function parseGenericChat(raw, sourceId) {
    const msgs = [];
    let lastTs = Date.now() - 3600e3;
    let i = 0;
    for (const line of raw.split(/\n/)) {
      const m = line.match(/^([^:]{1,40}):\s?(.+)$/);
      if (!m) continue;
      lastTs += 6e4;
      msgs.push(mkMsg(sourceId, i++, m[1].trim(), m[2].trim(), lastTs, "chat"));
    }
    return msgs;
  }

  function parseChatJson(data, sourceId) {
    const arr = Array.isArray(data) ? data : (data.messages || data.history || []);
    return arr.map((m, i) => {
      // prefer human names over slack user ids when an export carries both
      const person = canonPerson(m.person || m.real_name || m.user_profile?.real_name || m.name || m.username || m.user || "Unknown");
      const ts = parseDateAny(m.ts || m.time || m.date || m.timestamp, Date.now());
      return mkMsg(sourceId, i, person, String(m.text || m.message || ""), ts, "chat", m.subject);
    }).filter(m => m.text);
  }

  function parseEmails(raw, sourceId) {
    const chunks = /^From /m.test(raw) ? raw.split(/\n(?=From )/) : raw.split(/\n(?=From: )/);
    const msgs = [];
    chunks.forEach((chunk, i) => {
      if (!chunk.trim()) return;
      const sep = chunk.match(/\n\s*\n/);
      const head = sep ? chunk.slice(0, sep.index) : chunk;
      const body = sep ? chunk.slice(sep.index + sep[0].length) : "";
      const grab = re => (head.match(re) || [])[1];
      const from = grab(/^From:\s*(.+)$/im) || "Unknown";
      const dateStr = grab(/^Date:\s*(.+)$/im);
      const subject = (grab(/^Subject:\s*(.+)$/im) || "").replace(/^re:\s*/i, "");
      let person = from;
      const nm = from.match(/"?([^"<]+?)"?\s*<[^>]+>/);
      if (nm) person = nm[1].trim();
      else if (from.includes("@")) person = from.split("@")[0].replace(/[._]/g, " ");
      let text = body
        .split("\n")
        .filter(l => !/^\s*>/.test(l))                       // strip quoted lines
        .filter(l => !/^\s*on .{3,120} wrote:\s*$/i.test(l))  // strip "On ... wrote:" header
        .join("\n");
      text = text.replace(/\n--\s*\n[\s\S]*$/, "");           // classic signature marker
      text = text.replace(/\n(sent from|get outlook|best regards,)[\s\S]*$/i, "");
      text = text.trim();
      if (!text) return;
      msgs.push(mkMsg(sourceId, i, canonPerson(person), text, parseDateAny(dateStr, Date.now()), "email", subject));
    });
    return msgs;
  }

  // Tiny CSV reader — quotes, escaped quotes, newlines inside quotes.
  function parseCSV(text) {
    const rows = [];
    let row = [], cell = "", inQ = false;
    for (let i = 0; i < text.length; i++) {
      const c = text[i];
      if (inQ) {
        if (c === '"') { if (text[i + 1] === '"') { cell += '"'; i++; } else inQ = false; }
        else cell += c;
      } else if (c === '"') inQ = true;
      else if (c === ",") { row.push(cell); cell = ""; }
      else if (c === "\n" || c === "\r") {
        if (c === "\r" && text[i + 1] === "\n") i++;
        row.push(cell); cell = "";
        if (row.some(x => x !== "")) rows.push(row);
        row = [];
      } else cell += c;
    }
    row.push(cell);
    if (row.some(x => x !== "")) rows.push(row);
    return rows;
  }

  const pick = (obj, ...keys) => {
    for (const k of keys) {
      const hit = Object.keys(obj).find(ok => ok.toLowerCase().replace(/[\s_]/g, "") === k);
      if (hit != null && obj[hit] !== "" && obj[hit] != null) return obj[hit];
    }
    return undefined;
  };

  function taskEventDecision(sourceId, n, task, h, assignee) {
    const field = String(pick(h, "field", "type", "action") || "").toLowerCase();
    const from = String(pick(h, "from", "oldvalue", "old") ?? "");
    const to = String(pick(h, "to", "value", "newvalue", "new") ?? "");
    const at = parseDateAny(pick(h, "at", "date", "ts", "when", "time"), parseDateAny(pick(task, "updated", "updatedat"), Date.now()));
    const title = String(pick(task, "title", "name", "task", "summary") || "Untitled task");
    let option = null, polarity = "commit", text = "", matched = ["task change: " + (field || "update")];
    if (/due/.test(field)) {
      option = canonOption(to); polarity = "commit";
      text = `Moved due date of “${title}” from ${from || "unplanned"} to ${to}`;
    } else if (/status/.test(field)) {
      option = canonOption(to); polarity = "commit";
      text = `Changed status of “${title}” from ${from} to ${to}`;
    } else if (/priority/.test(field)) {
      option = canonOption(to); polarity = "commit";
      text = `Changed priority of “${title}” from ${from} to ${to}`;
    } else if (/delete|remove/.test(field)) {
      polarity = "reject"; option = null;
      text = `Deleted task “${title}”`;
      matched = ["task deleted"];
    } else if (/recreat|restor|reopen/.test(field)) {
      polarity = "revert"; option = canonOption(title);
      text = `Recreated task “${title}” after deletion`;
      matched = ["task recreated"];
    } else if (to) {
      option = canonOption(to); polarity = "commit";
      text = `Changed ${field || "field"} of “${title}” from ${from} to ${to}`;
    } else return null;
    return {
      id: "d" + hash32(sourceId + "#" + n), ts: at, person: canonPerson(assignee),
      text, subject: canonOption(title), option, rejectedOption: null,
      polarity, confidence: 0.75, matched, hedges: [],
      sourceId, sourceName: "", messageId: null, manual: false, origin: "task"
    };
  }

  function parseTasks(rawOrData, sourceId) {
    let tasks = null;
    if (typeof rawOrData === "string") {
      const t = rawOrData.trim();
      if (t[0] === "[" || t[0] === "{") {
        try { const j = JSON.parse(t); tasks = Array.isArray(j) ? j : (j.tasks || [j]); } catch { tasks = null; }
      }
      if (!tasks) {
        const rows = parseCSV(t);
        if (rows.length < 2) return { messages: [], taskDecisions: [] };
        const header = rows[0].map(h => h.trim());
        tasks = rows.slice(1).map(r => Object.fromEntries(header.map((h, i) => [h, r[i] ?? ""])));
      }
    } else tasks = Array.isArray(rawOrData) ? rawOrData : (rawOrData?.tasks || [rawOrData]);

    const messages = [], taskDecisions = [];
    let n = 0, mi = 0;
    for (const task of tasks) {
      const title = String(pick(task, "title", "name", "task", "summary") || "Untitled task");
      const assignee = pick(task, "assignee", "owner", "person", "user") || "Task tracker";
      const created = parseDateAny(pick(task, "created", "createdat", "createdat", "datecreated"), Date.now());
      messages.push(mkMsg(sourceId, mi++, assignee, `Created task “${title}”`, created, "task"));
      let history = pick(task, "history", "changes", "changelog", "activity");
      if (typeof history === "string") { try { history = JSON.parse(history); } catch { history = []; } }
      for (const h of Array.isArray(history) ? history : []) {
        const d = taskEventDecision(sourceId, n++, task, h, assignee);
        if (!d) continue;
        d.sourceName = "";
        taskDecisions.push(d);
        messages.push(mkMsg(sourceId, mi++, assignee, d.text, d.ts, "task"));
      }
    }
    return { messages, taskDecisions };
  }

  const mkMsg = (sourceId, i, person, text, ts, kind, subject) => ({
    id: "m" + hash32(sourceId + "#" + i),
    sourceId, person: canonPerson(person), text, ts, kind,
    subject: subject || null
  });

  function detectFormat(raw) {
    const t = (typeof raw === "string" ? raw : "").trim();
    if (/^[[{]/.test(t)) {
      try {
        const j = JSON.parse(t);
        const arr = Array.isArray(j) ? j : (j.tasks || j.messages || j.history || []);
        const first = arr[0] || {};
        if (Array.isArray(arr) && (first.title || first.status || first.history || first.changes)) return "tasks";
        if (Array.isArray(arr) && (first.text || first.message)) return "chat-json";
      } catch { /* fall through */ }
    }
    if (/^(From |From:|Date:|Subject:)/m.test(t) && /(wrote:|@|To:)/i.test(t)) return "email";
    if (/^title[ ,]|^name[ ,]|,status,|^task,/i.test(t) && /,/.test(t) && /\n/.test(t)) return "tasks";
    if (/\d{1,2}[\/.-]\d{1,2}[\/.-]\d{2,4},?\s+\d{1,2}:\d{2}/.test(t)) return "whatsapp";
    if (/^[\[(]?\d{4}-\d{1,2}-\d{1,2}[\])?,\s]/m.test(t)) return "iso-chat";
    if (/^[A-Za-z][\w .'-]{1,32}?\s+\d{1,2}:\d{2}\s*[ap]m?\b/im.test(t)) return "slack-copy";
    if (/^[^:\n]{1,40}:\s*\S/m.test(t)) return "generic-chat";
    return "unknown";
  }

  function parseRaw(raw, opts) {
    const sourceId = opts.sourceId;
    const hint = opts.kindHint;
    let format = detectFormat(raw);
    if (hint === "tasks" && (format === "unknown" || format === "chat-json")) format = "tasks";
    if (hint === "chat" && format === "unknown") format = "generic-chat";
    if (hint === "email" && format === "unknown") format = "email";

    if (format === "tasks") {
      let data = raw;
      if (typeof raw === "string" && /^[[{]/.test(raw.trim())) { try { data = JSON.parse(raw); } catch { /* keep string */ } }
      const { messages, taskDecisions } = parseTasks(data, sourceId);
      return { format, kind: "tasks", messages, taskDecisions };
    }
    if (format === "chat-json") {
      let messages = [];
      try { messages = parseChatJson(JSON.parse(raw), sourceId); } catch { /* bad json */ }
      return { format, kind: "chat", messages, taskDecisions: [] };
    }
    if (format === "email") return { format, kind: "email", messages: parseEmails(raw, sourceId), taskDecisions: [] };
    if (format === "whatsapp") return { format, kind: "chat", messages: parseWhatsApp(raw, sourceId), taskDecisions: [] };
    if (format === "iso-chat") return { format, kind: "chat", messages: parseIsoChat(raw, sourceId), taskDecisions: [] };
    if (format === "slack-copy") return { format, kind: "chat", messages: parseSlackCopy(raw, sourceId), taskDecisions: [] };
    if (format === "generic-chat") return { format, kind: "chat", messages: parseGenericChat(raw, sourceId), taskDecisions: [] };
    // last resort: treat the whole blob as one message from an unknown speaker
    return { format: "unknown", kind: "chat", messages: raw.trim() ? [mkMsg(sourceId, 0, "Unknown", raw.trim(), Date.now(), "chat")] : [], taskDecisions: [] };
  }

  // ------------------------------------------------------------------ topic grouping

  function tfMap(tokens, boost = {}) {
    const tf = {};
    for (const t of tokens) tf[t] = (tf[t] || 0) + 1;
    for (const [t, w] of Object.entries(boost)) tf[t] = (tf[t] || 0) + w;
    return tf;
  }

  function decisionTokens(d) {
    const tokens = tokenize(d.text);
    const boost = {};
    if (d.subject) tokenize(d.subject).forEach(t => boost[t] = (boost[t] || 0) + 2);
    const fam = familyOf(d.option);
    if (fam != null) tokenize(FAMILIES[fam].label).forEach(t => boost[t] = (boost[t] || 0) + 2);
    return { tokens, boost };
  }

  function buildVectors(decisions) {
    const docs = decisions.map(d => {
      const { tokens, boost } = decisionTokens(d);
      return { id: d.id, tokens, tf: tfMap(tokens, boost) };
    });
    const df = {};
    docs.forEach(doc => Object.keys(doc.tf).forEach(w => df[w] = (df[w] || 0) + 1));
    const N = Math.max(1, docs.length);
    docs.forEach(doc => {
      const v = {};
      for (const w in doc.tf) v[w] = doc.tf[w] * Math.log(1 + N / (1 + (df[w] || 0)));
      doc.vec = v;
      doc.top = Object.keys(v).sort((a, b) => v[b] - v[a]).slice(0, 12);
    });
    return docs;
  }

  function cosine(a, b) {
    let dot = 0, na = 0, nb = 0;
    for (const w in a) { na += a[w] * a[w]; if (b[w]) dot += a[w] * b[w]; }
    for (const w in b) nb += b[w] * b[w];
    return (na && nb) ? dot / Math.sqrt(na * nb) : 0;
  }

  function jaccard(A, B) {
    let inter = 0;
    for (const x of A) if (B.includes(x)) inter++;
    return inter / Math.max(1, A.length + B.length - inter);
  }

  function optionAffinity(option, vocab) {
    if (!option) return 0.4;
    const fam = familyOf(option);
    for (const v of vocab) {
      if (v === option) return 0.95;
      if (fam != null && familyOf(v) === fam) return 1;
    }
    return vocab.length ? 0.05 : 0.2;
  }

  function topicNameFor(d, doc) {
    const fam = familyOf(d.option);
    if (fam != null) return FAMILIES[fam].label;
    if (d.subject && d.subject.length <= 24) return d.subject;
    const tops = (doc?.top || []).filter(t => !/^\d/.test(t)).slice(0, 2);
    return tops.length ? tops.join(" ") : "untitled topic";
  }

  function assignTopics(decisions, existingTopics, threshold, fullRecluster) {
    const docs = buildVectors(decisions);
    const byId = new Map(docs.map(d => [d.id, d]));
    let topics;
    if (fullRecluster || !existingTopics?.length) {
      topics = [];
    } else {
      topics = existingTopics.map(t => ({
        id: t.id, name: t.name, kws: { ...(t.kws || {}) },
        optionVocab: [...(t.optionVocab || [])],
        top: Object.keys(t.kws || {}).slice(0, 12),
        created: t.created || Date.now(), count: 0
      }));
    }
    const assignments = {};
    const findTopic = (doc, d) => {
      let best = null, bestS = 0;
      for (const t of topics) {
        const cos = cosine(doc.vec, t.kws);
        const jac = jaccard(doc.top, t.top);
        const aff = optionAffinity(d.option, t.optionVocab);
        const s = 0.55 * cos + 0.2 * jac + 0.25 * aff;
        if (s > bestS) { bestS = s; best = t; }
      }
      return bestS >= threshold ? best : null;
    };
    for (let i = 0; i < decisions.length; i++) {
      const d = decisions[i], doc = byId.get(d.id);
      const known = !fullRecluster && existingTopics?.length && existingAssignments.get?.(d.id);
      if (known) { assignments[d.id] = known; const t = topics.find(t => t.id === known); if (t) { t.count++; } continue; }
      const t = findTopic(doc, d);
      if (t) {
        assignments[d.id] = t.id; t.count++;
        for (const w in doc.vec) t.kws[w] = (t.kws[w] || 0) * 0.985 + doc.vec[w];
        if (d.option && !t.optionVocab.includes(d.option)) t.optionVocab.push(d.option);
        t.top = Object.keys(t.kws).sort((a, b) => t.kws[b] - t.kws[a]).slice(0, 12);
      } else if (!d.option && !d.manual) {
        // reinforcement or contextless cancellation — only meaningful in context
        assignments[d.id] = null;
        continue;
      } else {
        const id = "t" + hash32(topicNameFor(d, doc) + "#" + d.id);
        const nt = { id, name: topicNameFor(d, doc), kws: { ...doc.vec }, optionVocab: d.option ? [d.option] : [], top: doc.top, created: d.ts, count: 1 };
        topics.push(nt);
        assignments[d.id] = id;
      }
    }
    return { topics, assignments };
  }

  // holder so assignTopics can respect stored assignments on incremental runs
  let existingAssignments = new Map();
  function assignTopicsIncremental(decisions, existingTopics, assignmentsIn, threshold) {
    existingAssignments = new Map(Object.entries(assignmentsIn || {}));
    const res = assignTopics(decisions, existingTopics, threshold, false);
    existingAssignments = new Map();
    return res;
  }
  function clusterFull(decisions, threshold) {
    return assignTopics(decisions, [], threshold, true);
  }

  // Used by merge/split on the main thread to keep keyword weights honest.
  function kwsOf(decisions) {
    const docs = buildVectors(decisions);
    const kws = {};
    docs.forEach(doc => { for (const w in doc.vec) kws[w] = (kws[w] || 0) + doc.vec[w]; });
    return kws;
  }

  // ------------------------------------------------------------------ reversal detection

  function detectReversals(decisions, windowMs) {
    const revs = [];
    const seen = [];
    let cur = null, lastCommitTs = null, lastCommitId = null;
    let lastRejectTs = null;
    for (const d of decisions) {
      const o = d.option ? canonOption(d.option) : null;
      if (d.polarity === "commit" || d.polarity === "switch") {
        if (o) {
          if (!cur && seen.includes(o)) {
            revs.push(rev(d, null, o, "boomerang", d.ts - lastRejectTs, `Re-committed ${o} after cancelling it`));
          } else if (cur && o !== cur) {
            const gap = d.ts - lastCommitTs;
            if (gap <= windowMs) {
              const boom = seen.includes(o);
              revs.push(rev(d, cur, o, boom ? "boomerang" : "switch", gap,
                boom ? `Boomerang — back to ${o} after ${cur}` : `Option changed: ${cur} → ${o}`));
            }
          }
          if (!seen.includes(o)) seen.push(o);
          cur = o; lastCommitTs = d.ts; lastCommitId = d.id; lastRejectTs = null;
        }
      } else if (d.polarity === "reject") {
        const target = d.rejectedOption ? canonOption(d.rejectedOption) : cur;
        if (cur && (!target || target === cur)) {
          const gap = d.ts - lastCommitTs;
          if (gap <= windowMs) revs.push(rev(d, cur, null, "reject", gap, `Cancelled ${cur} committed ${humanGap(gap)} earlier`));
          cur = null; lastRejectTs = d.ts;
        }
      } else if (d.polarity === "revert") {
        if (o) {
          if (cur && o !== cur) {
            const gap = d.ts - lastCommitTs;
            if (gap <= windowMs) {
              const boom = seen.includes(o);
              revs.push(rev(d, cur, o, boom ? "boomerang" : "revert", gap,
                boom ? `Boomerang — back to ${o} after ${cur}` : `Reverted from ${cur} back to ${o}`));
            }
          } else if (!cur && lastRejectTs != null) {
            revs.push(rev(d, null, o, "boomerang", d.ts - lastRejectTs, `Restored ${o} after it was cancelled`));
          }
          if (!seen.includes(o)) seen.push(o);
          cur = o; lastCommitTs = d.ts; lastCommitId = d.id; lastRejectTs = null;
        }
      }
    }
    return revs;

    function rev(d, from, to, type, gapMs, rule) {
      const fwd = isTaskProgression(from, to);
      if (fwd) return null;
      return {
        id: "r" + hash32(d.topicId + "#" + d.id + "#" + type),
        topicId: d.topicId, type, from, to,
        fromId: lastCommitId && type !== "boomerang" ? lastCommitId : d.id,
        toId: d.id, person: d.person, ts: d.ts,
        gapMs: isFinite(gapMs) ? Math.max(0, gapMs) : null, rule
      };
    }
  }

  // A task moving backlog → in progress → done is progress, not a flip.
  function isTaskProgression(from, to) {
    if (!from || !to) return false;
    const a = STATUS_ORDER.indexOf(from), b = STATUS_ORDER.indexOf(to);
    return a !== -1 && b !== -1 && b > a && TERMINAL_STATES.has(to);
  }

  // ------------------------------------------------------------------ scoring

  const LEVELS = [
    { max: 20, label: "Stable", idx: 0 },
    { max: 40, label: "Wobbly", idx: 1 },
    { max: 60, label: "Restless", idx: 2 },
    { max: 80, label: "Chaotic", idx: 3 },
    { max: 101, label: "Full Pendulum", idx: 4 }
  ];
  const levelOf = score => LEVELS.find(l => score < l.max);

  function normEntropy(counts) {
    const total = counts.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0);
    if (!total || counts.length < 2) return 0;
    let H = 0;
    for (const c of counts) { const p = c / total; H -= p * Math.log(p); }
    return H / Math.log(counts.length);
  }

  function scoreTopic(decisions, reversals) {
    const flips = reversals.length;
    const boomerangs = reversals.filter(r => r.type === "boomerang").length;
    const gaps = reversals.map(r => r.gapMs).filter(g => g != null && isFinite(g) && g >= 0);
    const avgGapMs = gaps.length ? gaps.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / gaps.length : null;
    const minGapMs = gaps.length ? Math.min(...gaps) : null;
    const minDays = minGapMs != null ? minGapMs / DAY : 90;

    const optCounts = {};
    decisions.forEach(d => { if (d.option) optCounts[d.option] = (optCounts[d.option] || 0) + 1; });
    const entropy = normEntropy(Object.values(optCounts));

    // alternation: of all the flips, how many swung back to something tried before
    const oscillation = flips ? boomerangs / flips : 0;

    // confidence drift: early third vs late third of the topic's decisions
    let drift = 0;
    if (decisions.length >= 4) {
      const sorted = [...decisions].sort((a, b) => a.ts - b.ts);
      const k = Math.max(1, Math.floor(sorted.length / 3));
      const early = avg(sorted.slice(0, k).map(d => d.confidence));
      const late = avg(sorted.slice(-k).map(d => d.confidence));
      drift = Math.abs(late - early);
    }
    const hedgeRatio = decisions.length ? decisions.filter(d => d.hedges?.length).length / decisions.length : 0;

    const flipF = Math.min(1, flips / 5);
    const boomF = Math.min(1, boomerangs / 2.5);
    const speedF = Math.min(1, 10 / (minDays + 4));
    const entropyF = entropy;
    const driftF = Math.min(1, drift * 2.2);
    const score = Math.round(100 * Math.min(1, Math.max(0,
      0.30 * flipF + 0.25 * boomF + 0.15 * speedF + 0.15 * oscillation + 0.10 * entropyF + 0.05 * driftF
    )));
    return {
      flips, boomerangs, avgGapMs, minGapMs, oscillation, entropy,
      confidenceDrift: Math.round(drift * 100) / 100, hedgeRatio: Math.round(hedgeRatio * 100) / 100,
      score, level: levelOf(score).label, levelIdx: levelOf(score).idx,
      factors: { flipF, boomF, speedF, oscillation, entropyF, driftF }
    };
  }

  function personStats(decisions, reversals, topicScores) {
    const people = {};
    const topicOf = new Map((topicScores || []).map(t => [t.id, t]));
    for (const d of decisions) {
      const p = people[d.person] || (people[d.person] = {
        person: d.person, decisions: 0, flips: 0, boomerangs: 0,
        topics: new Map(), hedges: 0, confs: [], firstTs: Infinity, lastTs: 0,
        flipHours: new Array(24).fill(0), flipDays: new Array(7).fill(0), gaps: []
      });
      p.decisions++; p.firstTs = Math.min(p.firstTs, d.ts); p.lastTs = Math.max(p.lastTs, d.ts);
      if (d.hedges?.length) p.hedges++;
      p.confs.push(d.confidence);
      const tid = d.topicId;
      if (tid) p.topics.set(tid, (p.topics.get(tid) || 0) + 1);
    }
    for (const r of reversals) {
      const p = people[r.person];
      if (!p) continue;
      p.flips++;
      if (r.type === "boomerang") p.boomerangs++;
      const dt = new Date(r.ts);
      p.flipHours[dt.getHours()]++;
      p.flipDays[dt.getDay()]++;
      if (r.gapMs != null && isFinite(r.gapMs)) p.gaps.push(r.gapMs);
    }
    const out = [];
    for (const p of Object.values(people)) {
      const minGap = p.gaps.length ? Math.min(...p.gaps) : null;
      const flipRate = p.decisions ? p.flips / p.decisions : 0;
      // weighted average of the topic scores this person drives
      let wsum = 0, wtot = 0;
      p.topics.forEach((cnt, tid) => { const t = topicOf.get(tid); if (t) { wsum += t.score * cnt; wtot += cnt; } });
      const topicScore = wtot ? wsum / wtot : 0;
      const score = Math.round(Math.min(100, Math.max(0,
        0.6 * topicScore + 25 * Math.min(1, flipRate * 2) + 20 * Math.min(1, p.boomerangs / 2.5)
      )));
      out.push({
        person: p.person, decisions: p.decisions, flips: p.flips, boomerangs: p.boomerangs,
        topics: [...p.topics.keys()], topicShare: [...p.topics.entries()].map(([id, n]) => ({ id, n })),
        flipRate: Math.round(flipRate * 100) / 100,
        minGapMs: minGap, avgConf: Math.round(avg(p.confs) * 100) / 100,
        hedgeRatio: p.decisions ? Math.round(p.hedges / p.decisions * 100) / 100 : 0,
        score, level: levelOf(score).label, levelIdx: levelOf(score).idx,
        flipHours: p.flipHours, flipDays: p.flipDays, firstTs: p.firstTs, lastTs: p.lastTs,
        tags: behaviorTags(p, minGap, flipRate)
      });
    }
    return out.sort((a, b) => b.score - a.score);
  }

  function behaviorTags(p, minGap, flipRate) {
    const tags = [];
    const night = p.flipHours.slice(21).concat(p.flipHours.slice(0, 5)).reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0);
    if (night >= Math.max(2, p.flips * 0.4) && p.flips >= 2) tags.push({ id: "night", label: "Night-time flipper" });
    if (p.flipDays[1] >= Math.max(2, p.flips * 0.4) && p.flips >= 2) tags.push({ id: "monday", label: "Monday reverser" });
    if (minGap != null && minGap < 2 * DAY && p.flips >= 3) tags.push({ id: "fast", label: "Fast flipper" });
    if (p.boomerangs >= 2) tags.push({ id: "boom", label: "Boomerang brain" });
    if (flipRate >= 0.5 && p.decisions >= 4) tags.push({ id: "serial", label: "Serial second-guesser" });
    if (p.hedges / Math.max(1, p.decisions) > 0.4) tags.push({ id: "hedge", label: "Hedge lord" });
    if (p.flips === 0 && p.decisions >= 3) tags.push({ id: "steady", label: "Steady hands" });
    if (!tags.length) tags.push({ id: "balanced", label: "Balanced decider" });
    return tags.slice(0, 3);
  }

  const avg = arr => arr.length ? arr.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / arr.length : 0;

  // ------------------------------------------------------------------ alerts

  const ORDINALS = { 1: "first", 2: "second", 3: "third", 4: "fourth", 5: "fifth" };
  const ordinal = n => ORDINALS[n] || n + "th";

  function suggestAlerts(topicScores, reversals, existingKeys, settings) {
    const alerts = [];
    const byTopic = {};
    reversals.forEach(r => { (byTopic[r.topicId] = byTopic[r.topicId] || []).push(r); });
    for (const t of topicScores) {
      const revs = (byTopic[t.id] || []).sort((a, b) => a.ts - b.ts);
      if (revs.length < (settings.alertMinReversals ?? 2)) continue;
      const latest = revs[revs.length - 1];
      const key = t.id + ":" + latest.id;
      if (existingKeys.has(key)) continue;
      const severity = (t.score >= (settings.alertCriticalScore ?? 70) || t.boomerangs >= 3) ? "critical" : "warn";
      alerts.push({
        id: "a" + hash32(key), key, topicId: t.id, reversalId: latest.id,
        severity, ts: latest.ts,
        title: `${t.name}: ${ordinal(revs.length)} flip detected`,
        body: `${latest.person} — ${latest.rule}. ${t.name} is now ${t.level} (score ${t.score}).`,
        read: false
      });
    }
    return alerts;
  }

  // ------------------------------------------------------------------ orchestrator

  // One call that walks the whole pipeline. progress(stage, pct) lets the worker
  // stream status back to the UI while the heavy loops churn.
  function analyze(payload, progress = () => {}) {
    const { messages = [], manualDecisions = [], existingTopics = [], assignments = {}, settings = {}, mode = "incremental" } = payload;
    const lex = { ...DEFAULT_LEXICON, ...(settings.lexicon || {}) };
    for (const k of Object.keys(lex)) if (!Array.isArray(lex[k])) delete lex[k];
    const threshold = settings.similarity ?? 0.3;
    const windowMs = (settings.reversalWindowDays ?? 45) * DAY;

    progress("extract", 8, "Scanning messages for decision language");
    const decisions = buildDecisions(messages, manualDecisions, lex);
    progress("extract", 34, `${decisions.length} decision sentences found`);

    progress("group", 40, "Grouping decisions into topics");
    let grouped;
    if (mode === "full" || !existingTopics.length) grouped = clusterFull(decisions, threshold);
    else grouped = assignTopicsIncremental(decisions, existingTopics, assignments, threshold);
    const { topics, assignments: outAssign } = grouped;
    decisions.forEach(d => { d.topicId = outAssign[d.id] || null; });
    // A bare "scratch that" with no option of its own almost certainly refers to
    // whatever the same person last committed to — attach it to that topic so the
    // cancellation lands in the right chain.
    const lastByPerson = {};
    for (const d of decisions) {
      const prev = lastByPerson[d.person];
      if (!d.option && !d.subject && d.polarity === "reject" && prev && prev.topicId && d.ts - prev.ts < 14 * DAY) {
        d.topicId = prev.topicId;
        outAssign[d.id] = prev.topicId;
      }
      lastByPerson[d.person] = d;
    }
    progress("group", 62, `${topics.length} topics`);

    progress("detect", 66, "Detecting reversals and boomerangs");
    const reversals = [];
    const byTopic = new Map();
    decisions.forEach(d => { if (d.topicId) { if (!byTopic.has(d.topicId)) byTopic.set(d.topicId, []); byTopic.get(d.topicId).push(d); } });
    for (const [tid, ds] of byTopic) {
      ds.sort((a, b) => a.ts - b.ts);
      reversals.push(...detectReversals(ds, windowMs));
    }
    progress("detect", 76, `${reversals.length} reversals`);

    progress("score", 80, "Scoring instability");
    const topicScores = topics.map(t => {
      const ds = byTopic.get(t.id) || [];
      const rv = reversals.filter(r => r.topicId === t.id);
      const s = scoreTopic(ds, rv);
      return { id: t.id, name: t.name, decisionCount: ds.length, ...s, optionVocab: t.optionVocab };
    });
    const people = personStats(decisions, reversals, topicScores);
    progress("score", 92, "Scores ready");

    progress("alerts", 96, "Checking alert thresholds");
    const existingKeys = new Set((payload.existingAlertKeys || []).map(a => a.key || a));
    const alerts = suggestAlerts(topicScores, reversals, existingKeys, settings);
    progress("done", 100, "Analysis complete");

    return {
      decisions, topics: topics.map(t => ({
        id: t.id, name: t.name, kws: t.kws, top: t.top,
        optionVocab: t.optionVocab, created: t.created
      })),
      reversals, topicScores, people, alerts
    };
  }

  return {
    DAY, FAMILIES, DEFAULT_LEXICON, LEVELS, ALIASES,
    norm, normWithMap, splitSentences, tokenize, stem, hash32,
    canonOption, canonPerson, parseDateAny, humanGap, parseCSV,
    detectFormat, parseRaw, buildDecisions, extractSentence,
    clusterFull, assignTopicsIncremental, kwsOf, topicNameFor,
    detectReversals, scoreTopic, personStats, suggestAlerts, analyze,
    levelOf
  };
})();

// Let the worker wrapper (and node tests) grab this without window shenanigans.
if (typeof module !== "undefined" && module.exports) module.exports = Engine;

/* ---------------------------------------------------------------
   utils — the small stuff everything leans on
--------------------------------------------------------------- */
const U = (() => {
  const $ = (sel, root = document) => root.querySelector(sel);
  const $$ = (sel, root = document) => Array.from(root.querySelectorAll(sel));

  const esc = s => String(s ?? "").replace(/[&<>"']/g, c =>
    ({ "&": "&amp;", "<": "&lt;", ">": "&gt;", '"': "&quot;", "'": "&#39;" }[c]));

  let uidSeq = 0;
  const uid = (p = "id") => p + "-" + (++uidSeq).toString(36) + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 7);

  const clamp = (v, a, b) => Math.min(b, Math.max(a, v));
  const lerp = (a, b, t) => a + (b - a) * t;
  const nap = ms => new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, ms));

  const debounce = (fn, ms) => {
    let t;
    return (...a) => { clearTimeout(t); t = setTimeout(() => fn(...a), ms); };
  };

  const MONTHS = ["Jan", "Feb", "Mar", "Apr", "May", "Jun", "Jul", "Aug", "Sep", "Oct", "Nov", "Dec"];
  const pad = n => String(n).padStart(2, "0");
  const fmtDate = ts => { const d = new Date(ts); return `${MONTHS[d.getMonth()]} ${d.getDate()}, ${d.getFullYear()}`; };
  const fmtDateShort = ts => { const d = new Date(ts); return `${MONTHS[d.getMonth()]} ${d.getDate()}`; };
  const fmtTime = ts => {
    const d = new Date(ts); let h = d.getHours(); const ap = h >= 12 ? "PM" : "AM";
    h = h % 12 || 12; return `${h}:${pad(d.getMinutes())} ${ap}`;
  };
  const fmtDateTime = ts => `${fmtDateShort(ts)} · ${fmtTime(ts)}`;
  const timeAgo = ts => {
    const s = Math.max(1, Math.round((Date.now() - ts) / 1000));
    if (s < 60) return s + "s ago";
    if (s < 3600) return Math.round(s / 60) + "m ago";
    if (s < 86400) return Math.round(s / 3600) + "h ago";
    if (s < 86400 * 30) return Math.round(s / 86400) + "d ago";
    return fmtDate(ts);
  };
  const fmtGap = ms => {
    if (ms == null || !isFinite(ms)) return "—";
    if (ms < 3600e3) return Math.max(1, Math.round(ms / 60e3)) + "m";
    if (ms < 86400e3) return Math.round(ms / 3600e3) + "h";
    const d = Math.round(ms / 86400e3);
    return d === 1 ? "1 day" : d + " days";
  };
  const isoDate = ts => { const d = new Date(ts); return `${d.getFullYear()}-${pad(d.getMonth() + 1)}-${pad(d.getDate())} ${pad(d.getHours())}:${pad(d.getMinutes())}`; };

  const initials = name => String(name || "?").split(/\s+/).map(w => w[0]).filter(Boolean).slice(0, 2).join("").toUpperCase();

  // deterministic hue from a string — keeps avatars consistently colored
  const strHue = s => { let h = 0; for (let i = 0; i < s.length; i++) h = (h * 31 + s.charCodeAt(i)) % 360; return h; };

  const download = (name, content, mime = "text/plain") => {
    const blob = new Blob([content], { type: mime + ";charset=utf-8" });
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const a = document.createElement("a");
    a.href = url; a.download = name;
    document.body.appendChild(a); a.click();
    setTimeout(() => { URL.revokeObjectURL(url); a.remove(); }, 600);
  };

  const copyText = async text => { try { await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text); return true; } catch { return false; } };

  return { $, $$, esc, uid, clamp, lerp, nap, debounce, MONTHS, pad,
    fmtDate, fmtDateShort, fmtTime, fmtDateTime, timeAgo, fmtGap, isoDate,
    initials, strHue, download, copyText };
})();

const icon = (name, size = 18, sw = 2) =>
  `<svg width="${size}" height="${size}" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="${sw}" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true">${ICONS[name] || ""}</svg>`;

const REDUCED = window.matchMedia("(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)").matches;
const COARSE = window.matchMedia("(pointer: coarse)").matches;

// highlight every matched lexicon phrase inside the original sentence — the
// normWithMap index bridge makes this exact even across punctuation edits
function highlightMatches(text, phrases) {
  if (!text) return "";
  const { s, map } = Engine.normWithMap(text);
  const cuts = [];
  for (const p of phrases || []) {
    const pn = Engine.norm(p);
    if (!pn) continue;
    let i = s.indexOf(pn);
    while (i !== -1) {
      const a = map[i], b = map[Math.min(i + pn.length - 1, map.length - 1)] + 1;
      if (a != null && b > a) cuts.push([a, b]);
      i = s.indexOf(pn, i + 1);
    }
  }
  if (!cuts.length) return U.esc(text);
  cuts.sort((a, b) => a[0] - b[0]);
  const merged = [cuts[0]];
  for (const c of cuts.slice(1)) {
    if (c[0] <= merged[merged.length - 1][1]) merged[merged.length - 1][1] = Math.max(merged[merged.length - 1][1], c[1]);
    else merged.push(c);
  }
  let out = "", pos = 0;
  for (const [a, b] of merged) {
    out += U.esc(text.slice(pos, a)) + "<mark>" + U.esc(text.slice(a, b)) + "</mark>";
    pos = b;
  }
  return out + U.esc(text.slice(pos));
}

/* ---------------------------------------------------------------
   event bus
--------------------------------------------------------------- */
const bus = (() => {
  const subs = new Map();
  return {
    on(ev, fn) {
      if (!subs.has(ev)) subs.set(ev, []);
      subs.get(ev).push(fn);
      return () => bus.off(ev, fn);
    },
    off(ev, fn) {
      const arr = subs.get(ev);
      if (arr) subs.set(ev, arr.filter(f => f !== fn));
    },
    emit(ev, data) {
      conz.log("bus", "emit " + ev);
      (subs.get(ev) || []).slice().forEach(fn => {
        try { fn(data); } catch (e) { console.error(e); conz.log("bus", "handler error: " + e.message, "error"); }
      });
    }
  };
})();

/* ---------------------------------------------------------------
   backend console — every api call, db write, worker message and
   bus event streams through here. the proof this thing is wired.
--------------------------------------------------------------- */
const conz = (() => {
  let body, fab, countEl, lineCount = 0;
  const MAX = 400;
  const filters = new Set(["api", "db", "wrk", "bus"]);

  function stamp() {
    const d = new Date();
    return `${String(d.getHours()).padStart(2, "0")}:${String(d.getMinutes()).padStart(2, "0")}:${String(d.getSeconds()).padStart(2, "0")}.${String(d.getMilliseconds()).padStart(3, "0")}`;
  }

  function log(tag, msg, cls) {
    if (!body) return;
    lineCount++;
    const line = document.createElement("div");
    line.className = "con-line" + (cls === "error" ? " error" : "");
    line.dataset.tag = tag;
    line.innerHTML = `<span class="cl-t">${stamp()}</span><span class="cl-tag ${tag}">${tag}</span><span class="cl-msg">${U.esc(msg)}</span>`;
    body.appendChild(line);
    while (body.children.length > MAX) body.firstChild.remove();
    const cs = getComputedStyle(body);
    if (body.scrollHeight - body.scrollTop - body.clientHeight < 80) body.scrollTop = body.scrollHeight;
    if (countEl) countEl.textContent = lineCount;
  }

  function init() {
    body = U.$("#con-body"); fab = U.$("#console-fab"); countEl = U.$("#con-count");
    U.$$(".con-filters button").forEach(b => {
      b.addEventListener("click", () => {
        const f = b.dataset.f;
        if (f === "clear") {
          body.innerHTML = ""; lineCount = 0; countEl.textContent = "0";
          return;
        }
        if (filters.has(f)) { filters.delete(f); b.classList.remove("on"); }
        else { filters.add(f); b.classList.add("on"); }
        U.$$(".con-line").forEach(l => { l.style.display = filters.has(l.dataset.tag) ? "" : "none"; });
      });
    });
    fab.addEventListener("click", () => toggle());
    log("bus", "console ready — waiting for traffic");
  }

  function toggle(force) {
    const con = U.$("#console");
    const open = force != null ? force : !con.classList.contains("open");
    con.classList.toggle("open", open);
    fab.setAttribute("aria-expanded", String(open));
    if (open) body.scrollTop = body.scrollHeight;
    return open;
  }
  const isOpen = () => U.$("#console").classList.contains("open");

  return { init, log, toggle, isOpen,
    api: m => log("api", m), db: m => log("db", m), wrk: m => log("wrk", m) };
})();

/* ---------------------------------------------------------------
   database layer — IndexedDB with a localStorage fallback for
   browsers that lock storage on file:// origins
--------------------------------------------------------------- */
const dbx = (() => {
  const NAME = "fliptrace";
  const VERSION = 1;

  // the stores and their indexes. to change the schema later, bump VERSION and
  // add a migration below — never edit an old one.
  const SCHEMA = {
    sources:   { key: "id" },
    messages:  { key: "id", indexes: ["sourceId", "ts", "person"] },
    decisions: { key: "id", indexes: ["topicId", "ts", "person"] },
    topics:    { key: "id" },
    reversals: { key: "id", indexes: ["topicId", "ts"] },
    alerts:    { key: "id", indexes: ["ts", "severity"] },
    settings:  { key: "key" }
  };
  const MIGRATIONS = {
    1(db, req) {
      for (const [name, def] of Object.entries(SCHEMA)) {
        const store = db.objectStoreNames.contains(name)
          ? req.transaction.objectStore(name)
          : db.createObjectStore(name, { keyPath: def.key });
        for (const idx of def.indexes || []) {
          if (!store.indexNames.contains(idx)) store.createIndex(idx, idx);
        }
      }
    }
  };

  let db = null, mode = "idb";
  let mem = null; // ls / memory adapter state

  function openIDB() {
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      let req;
      try { req = indexedDB.open(NAME, VERSION); }
      catch (e) { reject(e); return; }
      req.onupgradeneeded = e => {
        for (const [v, migrate] of Object.entries(MIGRATIONS)) {
          if (e.oldVersion < +v) migrate(req.result, req);
        }
      };
      req.onsuccess = () => resolve(req.result);
      req.onerror = () => reject(req.error);
      req.onblocked = () => reject(new Error("db blocked"));
    });
  }

  function loadLS() {
    try {
      const raw = localStorage.getItem(NAME);
      mem = raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : {};
      for (const s of Object.keys(SCHEMA)) if (!mem[s]) mem[s] = {};
      mode = "ls";
    } catch {
      mem = {};
      for (const s of Object.keys(SCHEMA)) mem[s] = {};
      mode = "memory";
    }
  }
  const persistLS = () => {
    if (mode !== "ls") return;
    try { localStorage.setItem(NAME, JSON.stringify(mem)); } catch { /* quota — keep running in memory */ }
  };

  async function open() {
    try {
      db = await openIDB();
      mode = "idb";
      conz.db(`open ${NAME} v${VERSION} (indexeddb)`);
    } catch (e) {
      loadLS();
      conz.db(`open ${NAME} v${VERSION} (${mode} fallback — ${e && e.name || "error"})`);
    }
    // sanity probe: some browsers open fine then fail on the first transaction
    try { await count("settings"); }
    catch { db = null; loadLS(); conz.db(`switching to ${mode} fallback`); }
  }

  const tx = (store, write) => db.transaction(store, write ? "readwrite" : "readonly").objectStore(store);
  const wrap = req => new Promise((res, rej) => { req.onsuccess = () => res(req.result); req.onerror = () => rej(req.error); });

  async function put(store, rec) {
    conz.db(`put ${store} #${rec.id ?? rec.key}`);
    if (mode === "idb") return wrap(tx(store, true).put(rec));
    mem[store][rec.id ?? rec.key] = rec; persistLS();
  }
  async function putMany(store, records) {
    if (!records?.length) return;
    if (records.length <= 3) records.forEach(r => conz.db(`put ${store} #${r.id ?? r.key}`));
    else conz.db(`put ${store} ×${records.length}`);
    if (mode === "idb") {
      const t = db.transaction(store, "readwrite");
      const os = t.objectStore(store);
      records.forEach(r => os.put(r));
      return new Promise((res, rej) => { t.oncomplete = res; t.onerror = () => rej(t.error); });
    }
    records.forEach(r => { mem[store][r.id ?? r.key] = r; });
    persistLS();
  }
  async function get(store, key) {
    if (mode === "idb") return wrap(tx(store).get(key));
    return mem[store][key] ?? null;
  }
  async function all(store) {
    if (mode === "idb") return wrap(tx(store).getAll());
    return Object.values(mem[store]);
  }
  async function byIndex(store, index, value) {
    if (mode === "idb") return wrap(tx(store).index(index).getAll(value));
    return Object.values(mem[store]).filter(r => r[index] === value);
  }
  async function del(store, key) {
    conz.db(`del ${store} #${key}`);
    if (mode === "idb") return wrap(tx(store, true).delete(key));
    delete mem[store][key]; persistLS();
  }
  async function delMany(store, keys) {
    if (!keys?.length) return;
    conz.db(`del ${store} ×${keys.length}`);
    if (mode === "idb") {
      const t = db.transaction(store, "readwrite");
      keys.forEach(k => t.objectStore(store).delete(k));
      return new Promise((res, rej) => { t.oncomplete = res; t.onerror = () => rej(t.error); });
    }
    keys.forEach(k => delete mem[store][k]); persistLS();
  }
  async function clear(store) {
    conz.db(`clear ${store}`);
    if (mode === "idb") return wrap(tx(store, true).clear());
    mem[store] = {}; persistLS();
  }
  async function count(store) {
    if (mode === "idb") return wrap(tx(store).count());
    return Object.keys(mem[store]).length;
  }
  async function clearAll() {
    for (const s of Object.keys(SCHEMA)) await clear(s);
  }

  return { open, put, putMany, get, all, byIndex, del, delMany, clear, count, clearAll,
    get mode() { return mode; } };
})();


/* ---------------------------------------------------------------
   api — a tiny REST-flavoured router. UI code only ever talks to
   this; services are the only things that touch the database.
--------------------------------------------------------------- */
const api = (() => {
  const routes = [];

  function add(method, pattern, handler) {
    const keys = [];
    const rx = new RegExp("^" + pattern.replace(/:[^/]+/g, m => {
      keys.push(m.slice(1));
      return "([^/?]+)";
    }) + "$");
    routes.push({ method, pattern, rx, keys, handler });
  }

  async function call(method, path, body) {
    const t0 = performance.now();
    const [pathOnly, qs] = String(path).split("?");
    const query = {};
    if (qs) qs.split("&").forEach(kv => {
      const [k, v] = kv.split("=");
      query[decodeURIComponent(k)] = decodeURIComponent(v || "");
    });
    const hit = routes.find(r => r.method === method && r.rx.test(pathOnly));
    if (!hit) {
      conz.api(`${method} ${pathOnly} → 404`);
      throw new Error(`No route: ${method} ${pathOnly}`);
    }
    const params = {};
    const m = pathOnly.match(hit.rx);
    hit.keys.forEach((k, i) => { params[k] = decodeURIComponent(m[i + 1]); });
    try {
      // a few ms of "network" so the console timeline feels honest
      await U.nap(3 + Math.random() * 12);
      const data = await hit.handler(params, body || {}, query);
      conz.api(`${method} ${pathOnly} → 200 ${(performance.now() - t0).toFixed(1)}ms`);
      return data;
    } catch (err) {
      conz.api(`${method} ${pathOnly} → 500 ${err.message}`);
      throw err;
    }
  }

  return { add, call,
    get: p => call("GET", p), post: (p, b) => call("POST", p, b),
    put: (p, b) => call("PUT", p, b), del: p => call("DELETE", p) };
})();

/* ---------------------------------------------------------------
   compute — the worker facade. heavy analysis happens off-thread;
   if the environment refuses blob workers we fall back to running
   the same engine synchronously with a progress shim.
--------------------------------------------------------------- */
const compute = (() => {
  let worker = null, broken = false, seq = 0;
  const pending = new Map();
  let mainRunOp = null;

  function ensureWorker() {
    if (worker || broken) return;
    try {
      const blob = new Blob([WORKER_SOURCE], { type: "text/javascript" });
      worker = new Worker(URL.createObjectURL(blob));
      worker.onmessage = e => {
        const m = e.data || {};
        const job = pending.get(m.id);
        if (!job) return;
        if (m.type === "progress") {
          conz.wrk(`◀ progress ${m.stage} ${Math.round(m.pct)}% — ${m.label}`);
          job.onProgress && job.onProgress(m);
        } else if (m.type === "result") {
          pending.delete(m.id);
          conz.wrk(`◀ result ${job.op} #${m.id}`);
          job.resolve(m);
        } else if (m.type === "error") {
          pending.delete(m.id);
          job.reject(new Error(m.error));
        }
      };
      worker.onerror = e => {
        console.error("worker error", e);
        broken = true;
      };
    } catch (e) {
      console.warn("no worker, falling back to main thread:", e);
      broken = true;
    }
    if (broken && !mainRunOp) mainRunOp = new Function(WORKER_SOURCE + "; return runOp;")();
  }

  function run(op, payload, onProgress, onParsed) {
    ensureWorker();
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      if (broken) {
        // same code, same behaviour, just synchronous
        try {
          const progress = (stage, pct, label) => {
            conz.wrk(`· progress ${stage} ${Math.round(pct)}% — ${label}`);
            onProgress && onProgress({ stage, pct, label });
          };
          const out = mainRunOp(op, payload, progress);
          resolve(out);
        } catch (err) { reject(err); }
        return;
      }
      const id = ++seq;
      pending.set(id, { op, resolve, reject, onProgress, onParsed });
      conz.wrk(`▶ ${op} #${id}`);
      worker.postMessage({ id, op, payload });
    });
  }

  return { run };
})();

/* ---------------------------------------------------------------
   services — the actual business logic behind the routes
--------------------------------------------------------------- */
const DEFAULT_SETTINGS = () => ({
  theme: "midnight",
  reversalWindowDays: 45,
  similarity: 0.3,
  alertMinReversals: 2,
  alertCriticalScore: 70,
  liveFeed: false,
  consoleOpen: false,
  cursor: true,
  calm: false,
  tourDone: false,
  lexicon: {
    commit: [...Engine.DEFAULT_LEXICON.commit],
    reversal: [...Engine.DEFAULT_LEXICON.reversal],
    reject: [...Engine.DEFAULT_LEXICON.reject],
    hedge: [...Engine.DEFAULT_LEXICON.hedge]
  }
});

const services = (() => {

  /* ---------- shared analysis plumbing ---------- */

  async function loadContext() {
    const [messages, topics, decisions, alerts] = await Promise.all([
      dbx.all("messages"), dbx.all("topics"), dbx.all("decisions"), dbx.all("alerts")
    ]);
    // task + manual decisions come pre-extracted — feed them back in on every run
    const presetDecisions = decisions.filter(d => d.origin === "task" || d.origin === "manual");
    const assignments = {};
    decisions.forEach(d => { if (d.topicId) assignments[d.id] = d.topicId; });
    return { messages, topics, decisions, presetDecisions, assignments, alertKeys: alerts.map(a => a.key) };
  }

  async function persistAnalysis(result, opts = {}) {
    const scoreById = new Map(result.topicScores.map(t => [t.id, t]));
    const keepIds = new Set(result.topics.map(t => t.id));

    await dbx.putMany("decisions", result.decisions);

    for (const t of result.topics) {
      const s = scoreById.get(t.id) || {};
      const rec = {
        id: t.id, name: t.name, kws: t.kws, top: t.top || Object.keys(t.kws || {}).slice(0, 12),
        optionVocab: t.optionVocab || [], created: t.created || Date.now(),
        score: s.score ?? 0, level: s.level || "Stable", levelIdx: s.levelIdx ?? 0,
        decisionCount: s.decisionCount ?? 0, flips: s.flips ?? 0, boomerangs: s.boomerangs ?? 0,
        avgGapMs: s.avgGapMs ?? null, minGapMs: s.minGapMs ?? null,
        oscillation: s.oscillation ?? 0, entropy: s.entropy ?? 0,
        confidenceDrift: s.confidenceDrift ?? 0, hedgeRatio: s.hedgeRatio ?? 0,
        factors: s.factors || null
      };
      await dbx.put("topics", rec);
    }

    // topics that vanished after a full re-cluster take their decisions with them
    const stale = (await dbx.all("topics")).filter(t => !keepIds.has(t.id));
    for (const t of stale) {
      await dbx.del("topics", t.id);
      const orphans = result.decisions.filter(d => d.topicId === t.id);
      for (const d of orphans) { d.topicId = null; }
      if (orphans.length) await dbx.putMany("decisions", orphans);
    }

    await dbx.putMany("reversals", result.reversals);

    if (result.alerts?.length) await dbx.putMany("alerts", result.alerts);
    return result.alerts || [];
  }

  async function announce(alerts, result) {
    await store.reload();
    bus.emit("data:changed", { analysis: result });
    for (const a of alerts) bus.emit("alert:new", a);
  }

  /* ---------- IngestService ---------- */

  async function ingest({ kind, raw, name }, onProgress, onParsed) {
    const rawHash = Engine.hash32(String(raw).trim());
    const sources = await dbx.all("sources");
    const dup = sources.find(s => s.rawHash === rawHash);
    if (dup) throw new Error(`"${dup.name}" is already imported — identical content`);

    const sourceId = U.uid("src");
    const ctx = await loadContext();
    const out = await compute.run("ingest", {
      raw, kindHint: kind, sourceId,
      settings: store.state.settings,
      existing: {
        messages: ctx.messages,
        presetDecisions: ctx.presetDecisions,
        topics: ctx.topics,
        assignments: ctx.assignments,
        alertKeys: ctx.alertKeys
      }
    }, onProgress, onParsed);

    const parsed = out.parsed;
    const source = {
      id: sourceId, kind: parsed.kind || kind || "chat", name: name || `Import ${new Date().toLocaleString()}`,
      addedAt: Date.now(), messageCount: parsed.messages.length,
      format: parsed.format, rawHash
    };
    parsed.messages.forEach(m => { m.sourceName = source.name; });
    parsed.taskDecisions.forEach(d => { d.sourceName = source.name; });

    await dbx.put("sources", source);
    if (parsed.messages.length) await dbx.putMany("messages", parsed.messages);
    const alerts = await persistAnalysis(out.result);
    await announce(alerts, out.result);

    const decisionsNew = out.result.decisions.filter(d => d.sourceId === sourceId).length;
    return { source, messages: parsed.messages.length, decisions: decisionsNew,
      topics: out.result.topics.length, reversals: out.result.reversals.length, alerts: alerts.length };
  }

  /* ---------- analysis re-runs ---------- */

  async function rerun(mode = "incremental", onProgress) {
    const ctx = await loadContext();
    const out = await compute.run("reanalyze", {
      messages: ctx.messages,
      manualDecisions: ctx.presetDecisions,
      existingTopics: mode === "incremental" ? ctx.topics : [],
      assignments: ctx.assignments,
      settings: store.state.settings, mode,
      existingAlertKeys: ctx.alertKeys
    }, onProgress);
    const alerts = await persistAnalysis(out.result);
    await announce(alerts, out.result);
    return { alerts: alerts.length };
  }

  async function rescore(onProgress) {
    const ctx = await loadContext();
    const decisions = await dbx.all("decisions");
    const out = await compute.run("rescore", {
      decisions, topics: ctx.topics, settings: store.state.settings, alertKeys: ctx.alertKeys
    }, onProgress);
    const alerts = await persistAnalysis(out.result);
    await announce(alerts, out.result);
    return { alerts: alerts.length };
  }

  /* ---------- manual decisions ---------- */

  async function addManualDecision(input) {
    const d = {
      id: U.uid("dman"), origin: "manual", manual: true,
      ts: input.ts || Date.now(),
      person: Engine.canonPerson(input.person || "Anonymous"),
      text: (input.text || "").trim(),
      subject: input.subject ? Engine.canonOption(input.subject) : null,
      option: input.option ? Engine.canonOption(input.option) : null,
      polarity: input.polarity || "commit",
      confidence: U.clamp(+input.confidence || 0.8, 0.05, 0.98),
      matched: ["manual entry"], hedges: [],
      sourceId: "manual", sourceName: "Manual entry", messageId: null
    };
    if (!d.text) throw new Error("A decision needs a sentence");
    const ctx = await loadContext();
    ctx.presetDecisions.push(d);
    const out = await compute.run("reanalyze", {
      messages: ctx.messages,
      manualDecisions: ctx.presetDecisions,
      existingTopics: ctx.topics, assignments: ctx.assignments,
      settings: store.state.settings, mode: "incremental",
      existingAlertKeys: ctx.alertKeys
    });
    const alerts = await persistAnalysis(out.result);
    await announce(alerts, out.result);
    return d;
  }

  /* ---------- topics: merge / split with undo+redo ---------- */

  const undoStack = [], redoStack = [];

  async function applyMerge(targetId, otherIds) {
    const topics = await dbx.all("topics");
    const decisions = await dbx.all("decisions");
    const target = topics.find(t => t.id === targetId);
    const others = topics.filter(t => otherIds.includes(t.id));
    if (!target || !others.length) throw new Error("Nothing to merge");

    const snapshot = {
      type: "merge",
      target: { ...target },
      others: others.map(o => ({ ...o })),
      decisions: decisions.filter(d => otherIds.includes(d.topicId)).map(d => ({ id: d.id, topicId: d.topicId }))
    };

    const moving = [];
    target.optionVocab = [...new Set([...(target.optionVocab || []), ...others.flatMap(o => o.optionVocab || [])])];
    for (const d of decisions) {
      if (otherIds.includes(d.topicId)) { d.topicId = targetId; moving.push(d); }
    }
    target.kws = Engine.kwsOf(decisions.filter(d => d.topicId === targetId));
    target.top = Object.keys(target.kws).sort((a, b) => target.kws[b] - target.kws[a]).slice(0, 12);

    await dbx.put("topics", target);
    if (moving.length) await dbx.putMany("decisions", moving);
    await dbx.delMany("topics", others.map(o => o.id));
    return snapshot;
  }

  async function mergeTopics(targetId, otherIds) {
    const snapshot = await applyMerge(targetId, otherIds);
    undoStack.push(snapshot); redoStack.length = 0;
    await rescore();
    bus.emit("topics:merged", { targetId });
    return true;
  }

  async function applySplit(topicId, decisionIds, name) {
    const topics = await dbx.all("topics");
    const decisions = await dbx.all("decisions");
    const parent = topics.find(t => t.id === topicId);
    const moving = decisions.filter(d => decisionIds.includes(d.id));
    if (!parent || !moving.length) throw new Error("Nothing to split");

    const snapshot = {
      type: "split",
      parent: { ...parent },
      moved: moving.map(d => ({ id: d.id, topicId: d.topicId })),
      childId: null
    };

    const child = {
      id: U.uid("tsplit"),
      name: name || `${parent.name} (split)`,
      kws: Engine.kwsOf(moving), top: [],
      optionVocab: [...new Set(moving.map(d => d.option).filter(Boolean))],
      created: Date.now()
    };
    child.top = Object.keys(child.kws).sort((a, b) => child.kws[b] - child.kws[a]).slice(0, 12);
    snapshot.childId = child.id;

    parent.kws = Engine.kwsOf(decisions.filter(d => d.topicId === topicId && !decisionIds.includes(d.id)));
    parent.top = Object.keys(parent.kws).sort((a, b) => parent.kws[b] - parent.kws[a]).slice(0, 12);

    moving.forEach(d => { d.topicId = child.id; });
    await dbx.put("topics", child);
    await dbx.put("topics", parent);
    await dbx.putMany("decisions", moving);
    return snapshot;
  }

  async function splitTopic(topicId, decisionIds, name) {
    const snapshot = await applySplit(topicId, decisionIds, name);
    undoStack.push(snapshot); redoStack.length = 0;
    await rescore();
    bus.emit("topics:split", { topicId, childId: snapshot.childId });
    return snapshot.childId;
  }

  async function undoTopicOp() {
    const op = undoStack.pop();
    if (!op) return null;
    if (op.type === "merge") {
      await dbx.put("topics", op.target);
      await dbx.putMany("topics", op.others);
      const decisions = await dbx.all("decisions");
      const back = [];
      for (const d of decisions) {
        const was = op.decisions.find(x => x.id === d.id);
        if (was && d.topicId === op.target.id && was.topicId !== op.target.id) { d.topicId = was.topicId; back.push(d); }
      }
      if (back.length) await dbx.putMany("decisions", back);
    } else if (op.type === "split") {
      await dbx.del("topics", op.childId);
      await dbx.put("topics", op.parent);
      const decisions = await dbx.all("decisions");
      const back = decisions.filter(d => op.moved.some(m => m.id === d.id));
      back.forEach(d => { d.topicId = op.parent.id; });
      if (back.length) await dbx.putMany("decisions", back);
    }
    redoStack.push(op);
    await rescore();
    bus.emit("topics:undo", op);
    return op.type;
  }

  async function redoTopicOp() {
    const op = redoStack.pop();
    if (!op) return null;
    // re-apply and push the fresh snapshot (split children get new ids)
    const snap = op.type === "merge"
      ? await applyMerge(op.target.id, op.others.map(o => o.id))
      : await applySplit(op.parent.id, op.moved.map(m => m.id));
    undoStack.push(snap);
    await rescore();
    bus.emit("topics:redo", op);
    return op.type;
  }

  /* ---------- alerts ---------- */

  async function markAlertRead(id) {
    const a = await dbx.get("alerts", id);
    if (a && !a.read) { a.read = true; await dbx.put("alerts", a); await store.reload(); bus.emit("data:changed"); }
    return true;
  }
  async function markAllRead() {
    const alerts = await dbx.all("alerts");
    const unread = alerts.filter(a => !a.read);
    unread.forEach(a => { a.read = true; });
    await dbx.putMany("alerts", unread);
    await store.reload(); bus.emit("data:changed");
  }
  async function clearRead() {
    const alerts = await dbx.all("alerts");
    await dbx.delMany("alerts", alerts.filter(a => a.read).map(a => a.id));
    await store.reload(); bus.emit("data:changed");
  }

  /* ---------- sources ---------- */

  async function deleteSource(id) {
    const messages = await dbx.byIndex("messages", "sourceId", id);
    const decisions = await dbx.all("decisions");
    const doomed = decisions.filter(d => d.sourceId === id);
    await dbx.delMany("messages", messages.map(m => m.id));
    await dbx.delMany("decisions", doomed.map(d => d.id));
    await dbx.del("sources", id);
    // reversals reference decisions that no longer exist — rebuild them
    const remaining = decisions.filter(d => d.sourceId !== id);
    const topics = await dbx.all("topics");
    const out = await compute.run("rescore", {
      decisions: remaining, topics, settings: store.state.settings,
      alertKeys: (await dbx.all("alerts")).map(a => a.key)
    });
    // drop reversals whose decisions vanished
    const liveIds = new Set(remaining.map(d => d.id));
    const staleRev = out.result.reversals.filter(r => !liveIds.has(r.toId));
    const keptRev = out.result.reversals.filter(r => liveIds.has(r.toId));
    await dbx.delMany("reversals", staleRev.map(r => r.id));
    const alerts = await persistAnalysis({ ...out.result, reversals: keptRev });
    await announce(alerts, out.result);
    return true;
  }

  /* ---------- demo + reset ---------- */

  async function loadDemo(onProgress) {
    const entries = Object.entries(DEMO);
    const summaries = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < entries.length; i++) {
      const [key, src] = entries[i];
      onProgress && onProgress({ stage: key, pct: 5 + (i / entries.length) * 90, label: src.name });
      try {
        summaries.push(await ingest({ kind: src.kind, raw: src.raw, name: src.name }));
      } catch (e) {
        if (!/already imported/.test(e.message)) throw e;
      }
    }
    onProgress && onProgress({ stage: "done", pct: 100, label: "Demo data ready" });
    return summaries;
  }

  async function resetAll() {
    await dbx.clearAll();
    undoStack.length = 0; redoStack.length = 0;
    const settings = DEFAULT_SETTINGS();
    settings.theme = store.state.settings.theme;       // keep the look the user chose
    settings.cursor = store.state.settings.cursor;
    settings.calm = store.state.settings.calm;
    settings.tourDone = true;                    // they've seen it
    await dbx.put("settings", { key: "app", ...settings });
    store.state.settings = settings;
    await store.reload();
    bus.emit("data:changed"); bus.emit("settings:changed");
  }

  /* ---------- export / import ---------- */

  async function exportJSON() {
    const [sources, messages, decisions, topics, reversals, alerts, settingsRec] = await Promise.all([
      dbx.all("sources"), dbx.all("messages"), dbx.all("decisions"), dbx.all("topics"),
      dbx.all("reversals"), dbx.all("alerts"), dbx.get("settings", "app")
    ]);
    return {
      app: "fliptrace", version: 1, exportedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      sources, messages, decisions, topics, reversals, alerts, settings: settingsRec
    };
  }

  function csvCell(v) {
    const s = String(v ?? "");
    return /[",\n]/.test(s) ? `"${s.replace(/"/g, '""')}"` : s;
  }

  async function exportCSV(kind) {
    const topicName = new Map((await dbx.all("topics")).map(t => [t.id, t.name]));
    if (kind === "reversals") {
      const rows = [["id", "topic", "type", "from", "to", "who", "when", "gap_days", "rule"]];
      const revs = await dbx.all("reversals");
      revs.sort((a, b) => a.ts - b.ts).forEach(r => rows.push([
        r.id, topicName.get(r.topicId) || "", r.type, r.from || "", r.to || "",
        r.person, U.isoDate(r.ts), r.gapMs != null ? (r.gapMs / Engine.DAY).toFixed(1) : "", r.rule
      ]));
      return rows.map(r => r.map(csvCell).join(",")).join("\n");
    }
    const rows = [["id", "when", "who", "topic", "polarity", "option", "confidence", "matched", "source", "sentence"]];
    const decs = await dbx.all("decisions");
    decs.sort((a, b) => a.ts - b.ts).forEach(d => rows.push([
      d.id, U.isoDate(d.ts), d.person, topicName.get(d.topicId) || "", d.polarity,
      d.option || "", d.confidence, (d.matched || []).join(" | "), d.sourceName || "", d.text
    ]));
    return rows.map(r => r.map(csvCell).join(",")).join("\n");
  }

  async function importJSON(dump) {
    if (!dump || dump.app !== "fliptrace") throw new Error("Not a FlipTrace export file");
    for (const store of ["sources", "messages", "decisions", "topics", "reversals", "alerts", "settings"]) {
      if (!Array.isArray(dump[store])) continue;
      await dbx.clear(store);
      await dbx.putMany(store, dump[store]);
    }
    await store.reload();
    bus.emit("data:changed"); bus.emit("settings:changed");
    return true;
  }

  return { ingest, rerun, rescore, addManualDecision, mergeTopics, splitTopic,
    undoTopicOp, redoTopicOp, markAlertRead, markAllRead, clearRead,
    deleteSource, loadDemo, resetAll, exportJSON, exportCSV, importJSON,
    undoDepth: () => undoStack.length, redoDepth: () => redoStack.length };
})();

/* ---------------------------------------------------------------
   store — UI-side cache. refreshed exclusively through the api.
--------------------------------------------------------------- */
const store = (() => {
  const state = {
    settings: DEFAULT_SETTINGS(),
    sources: [], messages: [], decisions: [], topics: [],
    reversals: [], alerts: [], people: []
  };

  async function init() {
    try {
      const saved = await dbx.get("settings", "app");
      if (saved) state.settings = { ...DEFAULT_SETTINGS(), ...saved, lexicon: { ...DEFAULT_SETTINGS().lexicon, ...(saved.lexicon || {}) } };
      else await dbx.put("settings", { key: "app", ...state.settings });
    } catch (e) { console.warn("settings load failed", e); }
    await reload();
  }

  async function reload() {
    const data = await api.get("/state");
    state.sources = data.sources; state.messages = data.messages;
    state.decisions = data.decisions; state.topics = data.topics;
    state.reversals = data.reversals; state.alerts = data.alerts;
    state.people = data.people;
  }

  const topicById = id => state.topics.find(t => t.id === id);
  const decisionsOf = id => state.decisions.filter(d => d.topicId === id).sort((a, b) => a.ts - b.ts);
  const reversalsOf = id => state.reversals.filter(r => r.topicId === id).sort((a, b) => a.ts - b.ts);
  const person = name => state.people.find(p => p.person === name);

  function stats() {
    const scored = state.topics.filter(t => t.decisionCount > 0);
    const avgScore = scored.length ? Math.round(scored.reduce((a, t) => a + (t.score || 0), 0) / scored.length) : 0;
    return {
      decisions: state.decisions.length,
      topics: state.topics.filter(t => t.decisionCount > 0).length,
      reversals: state.reversals.length,
      boomerangs: state.reversals.filter(r => r.type === "boomerang").length,
      avgScore, people: state.people.length,
      messages: state.messages.length, sources: state.sources.length,
      unreadAlerts: state.alerts.filter(a => !a.read).length
    };
  }

  // day -> reversal counts for the heatmap
  function reversalsByDay(from, to) {
    const map = new Map();
    state.reversals.forEach(r => {
      if (r.ts < from || r.ts > to) return;
      const day = new Date(r.ts); day.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
      const k = day.getTime();
      map.set(k, (map.get(k) || 0) + 1);
    });
    return map;
  }

  return { state, init, reload, topicById, decisionsOf, reversalsOf, person, stats, reversalsByDay };
})();

/* ---------------------------------------------------------------
   route registration — the "server" wiring
--------------------------------------------------------------- */
function registerRoutes() {
  api.add("GET", "/health", () => ({ ok: true, storage: dbx.mode, when: new Date().toISOString() }));

  api.add("GET", "/state", async () => {
    const [sources, messages, decisions, topics, reversals, alerts] = await Promise.all([
      dbx.all("sources"), dbx.all("messages"), dbx.all("decisions"),
      dbx.all("topics"), dbx.all("reversals"), dbx.all("alerts")
    ]);
    const people = Engine.personStats(decisions, reversals, topics);
    return { sources, messages, decisions, topics, reversals, alerts, people };
  });

  api.add("GET", "/sources", () => dbx.all("sources"));
  api.add("DELETE", "/sources/:id", (p) => services.deleteSource(p.id));

  api.add("POST", "/ingest", (p, b) => services.ingest(b, b.onProgress, b.onParsed));

  api.add("POST", "/decisions", (p, b) => services.addManualDecision(b));

  api.add("GET", "/topics", async () => {
    const topics = await dbx.all("topics");
    return topics.filter(t => t.decisionCount > 0)
      .sort((a, b) => (b.score || 0) - (a.score || 0));
  });
  api.add("PUT", "/topics/:id", async (p, b) => {
    const t = await dbx.get("topics", p.id);
    if (!t) throw new Error("No such topic");
    if (b.name) t.name = b.name.slice(0, 60);
    await dbx.put("topics", t);
    await store.reload(); bus.emit("data:changed");
    return t;
  });
  api.add("POST", "/topics/merge", (p, b) => services.mergeTopics(b.targetId, b.otherIds));
  api.add("POST", "/topics/split", (p, b) => services.splitTopic(b.topicId, b.decisionIds, b.name));
  api.add("POST", "/topics/undo", () => services.undoTopicOp());
  api.add("POST", "/topics/redo", () => services.redoTopicOp());

  api.add("POST", "/analyze", (p, b) => services.rerun(b.mode || "full", b.onProgress));
  api.add("POST", "/rescore", (p, b) => services.rescore(b && b.onProgress));

  api.add("GET", "/people", () => store.state.people);
  api.add("GET", "/alerts", async (p, b, q) => {
    const alerts = (await dbx.all("alerts")).sort((a, b) => b.ts - a.ts);
    if (q.sev) return alerts.filter(a => a.severity === q.sev);
    return alerts;
  });
  api.add("PUT", "/alerts/:id/read", (p) => services.markAlertRead(p.id));
  api.add("PUT", "/alerts/read-all", () => services.markAllRead());
  api.add("DELETE", "/alerts", () => services.clearRead());

  api.add("GET", "/settings", () => store.state.settings);
  api.add("PUT", "/settings", async (p, b) => {
    const next = { ...store.state.settings, ...b, lexicon: { ...store.state.settings.lexicon, ...(b.lexicon || {}) } };
    store.state.settings = next;
    await dbx.put("settings", { key: "app", ...next });
    bus.emit("settings:changed", next);
    const detectionKeys = ["reversalWindowDays", "similarity", "alertMinReversals", "alertCriticalScore", "lexicon"];
    if (detectionKeys.some(k => JSON.stringify(b[k]) !== JSON.stringify(undefined))) {
      await services.rescore();
    }
    return next;
  });

  api.add("POST", "/demo", (p, b) => services.loadDemo(b.onProgress));
  api.add("POST", "/reset", () => services.resetAll());

  api.add("GET", "/export/json", () => services.exportJSON());
  api.add("GET", "/export/csv", (p, b, q) => services.exportCSV(q.kind || "decisions"));
  api.add("POST", "/import", (p, b) => services.importJSON(b.dump));
}


/* ---------------------------------------------------------------
   ui kit — toasts, modals, tooltips, fancy controls
--------------------------------------------------------------- */
const ui = (() => {
  let revealObs = null;
  const TIP = () => U.$("#tooltip");

  /* ---------- toasts ---------- */
  function toast({ msg = "", title = "", type = "info", timeout = 4200, alert = false, onClick }) {
    const host = U.$("#toasts");
    const t = document.createElement("div");
    t.className = `toast ${type}${alert ? " alert" : ""}${alert && type === "error" ? " critical" : ""}`;
    const icoName = { ok: "check", info: "info", warn: "warn", error: "warn" }[type] || "info";
    t.innerHTML = `
      ${alert ? '<span class="shockwave"></span>' : ""}
      <div class="t-ico">${icon(icoName, 17)}</div>
      <div class="t-body">${title ? `<div class="t-title">${U.esc(title)}</div>` : ""}
        <div class="t-msg">${U.esc(msg)}</div></div>
      <button class="t-close" aria-label="Dismiss">${icon("x", 13)}</button>
      <span class="t-bar" style="animation-duration:${timeout}ms"></span>`;
    host.appendChild(t);
    const kill = () => { t.classList.add("out"); setTimeout(() => t.remove(), 380); };
    t.querySelector(".t-close").addEventListener("click", e => { e.stopPropagation(); kill(); });
    if (onClick) t.addEventListener("click", () => { onClick(); kill(); });
    if (timeout > 0) setTimeout(kill, timeout);
    while (host.children.length > 5) host.firstChild.remove();
    return t;
  }
  const toastError = (msg, title = "Something broke") => toast({ msg, title, type: "error", timeout: 6000 });

  /* ---------- modal ---------- */
  function modal({ title = "", body = "", actions = null, wide = false }) {
    return new Promise(resolve => {
      const root = U.$("#modal-root");
      root.innerHTML = "";
      root.classList.add("open");
      const back = document.createElement("div");
      back.className = "modal-backdrop";
      const box = document.createElement("div");
      box.className = "modal";
      if (wide) box.style.width = "min(880px,100%)";
      box.innerHTML = `<h3>${U.esc(title)}</h3><div class="modal-body">${body}</div>
        <div class="modal-actions"></div>`;
      root.append(back, box);
      const actHost = box.querySelector(".modal-actions");
      const close = val => { root.classList.remove("open"); root.innerHTML = ""; document.removeEventListener("keydown", onKey); resolve(val); };
      const onKey = e => { if (e.key === "Escape") { e.stopPropagation(); close(null); } };
      document.addEventListener("keydown", onKey);
      back.addEventListener("click", () => close(null));
      if (!actions) actions = [{ label: "Close", value: null }];
      actions.forEach(a => {
        const b = document.createElement("button");
        b.className = `btn ${a.cls || "btn-ghost"}`;
        b.innerHTML = `<span class="sweep"></span>${a.icon ? icon(a.icon, 15) : ""}${U.esc(a.label)}`;
        b.addEventListener("click", () => close(a.value));
        actHost.appendChild(b);
      });
      enhance(root);
      const first = actHost.querySelector("button");
      if (first) first.focus();
      box.querySelector(".modal-body").addEventListener("click", e => {
        // let pages hook clicks inside the modal body via data-act attributes
        const act = e.target.closest("[data-act]");
        if (act) close(act.dataset.act);
      });
    });
  }
  async function confirm({ msg, title = "Are you sure?", okLabel = "Confirm", danger = false }) {
    const v = await modal({
      title, body: `<p class="muted">${U.esc(msg)}</p>`,
      actions: [
        { label: "Cancel", value: false },
        { label: okLabel, value: true, cls: danger ? "btn-danger" : "btn-primary" }
      ]
    });
    return v === true;
  }

  /* ---------- progress modal (scan bar, no spinners here) ---------- */
  function progressModal(label = "Working") {
    const root = U.$("#modal-root");
    root.innerHTML = ""; root.classList.add("open");
    const back = document.createElement("div");
    back.className = "modal-backdrop";
    const box = document.createElement("div");
    box.className = "modal";
    box.innerHTML = `
      <h3>${U.esc(label)}</h3>
      <div class="progressing">
        <div class="progress-stage">
          <span class="ps-label" id="pm-label">Warming up…</span>
          <div class="scan-bar"><i id="pm-bar" style="width:2%"></i></div>
          <span class="ps-pct" id="pm-pct">0%</span>
        </div>
        <p class="small faint" style="margin-top:14px">Running the real pipeline: parse → extract → group → detect → score → alert</p>
      </div>`;
    root.append(back, box);
    return {
      update({ pct, label }) {
        const bar = box.querySelector("#pm-bar"), pc = box.querySelector("#pm-pct"), lb = box.querySelector("#pm-label");
        if (bar) bar.style.width = U.clamp(pct, 2, 100) + "%";
        if (pc) pc.textContent = Math.round(pct) + "%";
        if (lb && label) lb.textContent = label;
      },
      close() { root.classList.remove("open"); root.innerHTML = ""; }
    };
  }

  /* ---------- tooltip ---------- */
  function initTooltip() {
    const tip = TIP();
    let current = null;
    const show = el => {
      const text = el.dataset.tip;
      if (!text) return;
      current = el;
      tip.innerHTML = U.esc(text);
      tip.classList.add("show");
      const r = el.getBoundingClientRect();
      const tw = tip.offsetWidth, th = tip.offsetHeight;
      let x = U.clamp(r.left + r.width / 2 - tw / 2, 8, innerWidth - tw - 8);
      let y = r.top - th - 10;
