      if (y < 8) y = r.bottom + 10;
      tip.style.left = x + "px"; tip.style.top = y + "px";
    };
    const hide = () => { current = null; tip.classList.remove("show"); };
    document.addEventListener("mouseover", e => {
      const el = e.target.closest("[data-tip]");
      if (el) show(el);
      else if (current) hide();
    });
    document.addEventListener("focusin", e => {
      const el = e.target.closest("[data-tip]");
      if (el) show(el);
    });
    document.addEventListener("focusout", hide);
    window.addEventListener("scroll", hide, { passive: true });
  }

  /* ---------- shared interactions ---------- */
  function initInteractions() {
    // click ripples on every button
    document.addEventListener("pointerdown", e => {
      const btn = e.target.closest(".btn");
      if (!btn) return;
      const r = btn.getBoundingClientRect();
      const rip = document.createElement("span");
      rip.className = "ripple";
      const size = Math.max(r.width, r.height) * 1.1;
      rip.style.cssText = `width:${size}px;height:${size}px;left:${e.clientX - r.left - size / 2}px;top:${e.clientY - r.top - size / 2}px`;
      btn.appendChild(rip);
      setTimeout(() => rip.remove(), 600);
    });

    // magnetism for elements that ask for it
    if (!COARSE && !REDUCED) {
      document.addEventListener("pointermove", e => {
        const el = e.target.closest(".magnetic");
        U.$$(".magnetic").forEach(m => {
          if (m !== el) m.style.transform = "";
        });
        if (!el) return;
        const r = el.getBoundingClientRect();
        const dx = e.clientX - (r.left + r.width / 2), dy = e.clientY - (r.top + r.height / 2);
        el.style.transform = `translate(${dx * 0.18}px, ${dy * 0.28}px)`;
      });
    }
  }

  // 3D tilt + glare for cards, bound per element on render
  function bindTilt(el) {
    if (COARSE || REDUCED) return;
    let raf = null;
    el.addEventListener("pointermove", e => {
      if (raf) return;
      raf = requestAnimationFrame(() => {
        raf = null;
        const r = el.getBoundingClientRect();
        const px = (e.clientX - r.left) / r.width, py = (e.clientY - r.top) / r.height;
        el.style.transform = `perspective(900px) rotateX(${(0.5 - py) * 7}deg) rotateY(${(px - 0.5) * 9}deg) translateY(-3px)`;
        el.style.setProperty("--gx", px * 100 + "%");
        el.style.setProperty("--gy", py * 100 + "%");
      });
    });
    el.addEventListener("pointerleave", () => {
      el.style.transform = "";
    });
  }

  /* ---------- reveal on scroll ---------- */
  function initReveal() {
    revealObs = new IntersectionObserver(entries => {
      entries.forEach(en => {
        if (en.isIntersecting) { en.target.classList.add("revealed"); revealObs.unobserve(en.target); }
      });
    }, { threshold: 0.12 });
  }

  /* ---------- count-up numbers ---------- */
  function countUp(el, to, { dur = 1100, suffix = "" } = {}) {
    if (REDUCED || store.state.settings.calm) { el.textContent = to + suffix; return; }
    const from = 0, t0 = performance.now();
    const step = now => {
      const k = U.clamp((now - t0) / dur, 0, 1);
      const v = Math.round(from + (to - from) * (1 - Math.pow(1 - k, 3)));
      el.textContent = v + suffix;
      if (k < 1) requestAnimationFrame(step);
    };
    requestAnimationFrame(step);
  }

  /* ---------- enhance freshly rendered content ---------- */
  function enhance(root = document) {
    U.$$(".btn", root).forEach(b => {
      if (!b.querySelector(".sweep") && !b.classList.contains("btn-icon")) {
        const s = document.createElement("span");
        s.className = "sweep";
        b.prepend(s);
      }
    });
    U.$$(".tilt", root).forEach(el => { if (!el.dataset.tiltBound) { el.dataset.tiltBound = "1"; bindTilt(el); } });
    let idx = 0;
    U.$$("[data-reveal]", root).forEach(el => {
      if (!el.dataset.revealBound) {
        el.dataset.revealBound = "1";
        el.style.transitionDelay = Math.min(idx * 70, 420) + "ms";
        revealObs && revealObs.observe(el);
      }
      idx++;
    });
  }

  /* ---------- tabs ---------- */
  function tabs(host, items, onChange, activeId) {
    const glider = document.createElement("div");
    glider.className = "tab-glider";
    host.classList.add("tabs");
    host.appendChild(glider);
    const btns = items.map(it => {
      const b = document.createElement("button");
      b.className = "tab" + (it.id === activeId ? " on" : "");
      b.textContent = it.label;
      b.dataset.id = it.id;
      b.addEventListener("click", () => {
        U.$$(".tab", host).forEach(x => x.classList.toggle("on", x === b));
        moveGlider(b);
        onChange(it.id);
      });
      host.appendChild(b);
      return b;
    });
    function moveGlider(b) {
      glider.style.left = b.offsetLeft + "px";
      glider.style.width = b.offsetWidth + "px";
    }
    requestAnimationFrame(() => { const on = host.querySelector(".tab.on") || btns[0]; if (on) { moveGlider(on); if (!host.querySelector(".tab.on")) on.classList.add("on"); } });
    return { set: id => { const b = btns.find(x => x.dataset.id === id); if (b) b.click(); } };
  }

  /* ---------- dropdown ---------- */
  function dropdown({ options, value, onChange, label, wide = false }) {
    const host = document.createElement("div");
    host.className = "dropdown" + (wide ? " wide" : "");
    const cur = options.find(o => o.value === value) || options[0];
    host.innerHTML = `
      <button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" style="min-width:130px;justify-content:space-between" aria-haspopup="listbox">
        <span class="dd-cur">${U.esc(cur ? cur.label : "—")}</span>${icon("chevronDown", 14)}
      </button>
      <div class="dropdown-menu" role="listbox"></div>`;
    const btn = host.querySelector("button"), menu = host.querySelector(".dropdown-menu");
    options.forEach(o => {
      const b = document.createElement("button");
      b.setAttribute("role", "option");
      b.innerHTML = `${o.icon ? icon(o.icon, 14) : ""}<span>${U.esc(o.label)}</span>`;
      b.addEventListener("click", e => {
        e.stopPropagation();
        host.classList.remove("open");
        host.querySelector(".dd-cur").textContent = o.label;
        onChange && onChange(o.value, o);
      });
      menu.appendChild(b);
    });
    btn.addEventListener("click", e => {
      e.stopPropagation();
      U.$$(".dropdown.open").forEach(d => { if (d !== host) d.classList.remove("open"); });
      host.classList.toggle("open");
    });
    document.addEventListener("click", () => host.classList.remove("open"));
    return host;
  }

  /* ---------- misc builders pages share ---------- */
  const levelChip = t => `<span class="lvl lvl-${t.levelIdx ?? 0}">${U.esc(t.level || "Stable")}</span>`;

  function avatar(name, size = 38) {
    const h = U.strHue(name || "?");
    return `<span class="leader-ava" style="width:${size}px;height:${size}px;background:linear-gradient(135deg,hsl(${h} 80% 55%),hsl(${(h + 60) % 360} 80% 45%))">${U.esc(U.initials(name))}</span>`;
  }

  function empty(icoName, title, text, ctaLabel, onCta) {
    const wrap = document.createElement("div");
    wrap.className = "empty";
    wrap.innerHTML = `
      <svg width="120" height="86" viewBox="0 0 120 86" fill="none" aria-hidden="true">
        <line x1="20" y1="52" x2="100" y2="52" stroke="var(--brd-2)" stroke-width="2"/>
        <circle cx="30" cy="52" r="5" fill="var(--ok)" opacity=".9"><animate attributeName="opacity" values=".9;.3;.9" dur="2.4s" repeatCount="indefinite"/></circle>
        <circle cx="90" cy="52" r="5" fill="var(--ac3)" opacity=".9"><animate attributeName="opacity" values=".3;.9;.3" dur="2.4s" repeatCount="indefinite"/></circle>
        <line x1="60" y1="14" x2="60" y2="34" stroke="var(--ac1)" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round">
          <animateTransform attributeName="transform" type="rotate" values="-18 60 34;18 60 34;-18 60 34" dur="2.2s" repeatCount="indefinite" ease="ease-in-out"/>
        </line>
        <circle cx="60" cy="38" r="4" fill="var(--ac2)"/>
        <path d="M46 70h28" stroke="var(--brd-2)" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-dasharray="2 6"/>
      </svg>
      <h4>${U.esc(title)}</h4><p>${U.esc(text)}</p>`;
    if (ctaLabel) {
      const b = document.createElement("button");
      b.className = "btn btn-primary";
      b.innerHTML = `<span class="sweep"></span>${icon("spark", 15)} ${U.esc(ctaLabel)}`;
      b.addEventListener("click", onCta);
      wrap.appendChild(b);
    }
    return wrap;
  }

  function sectionHead(title, sub = "", toolsHTML = "") {
    return `<div class="sec-head" data-reveal>
      <h2>${title}</h2>${sub ? `<span class="sec-sub">${sub}</span>` : ""}
      ${toolsHTML ? `<div class="sec-tools">${toolsHTML}</div>` : ""}</div>`;
  }

  function init() {
    initTooltip();
    initInteractions();
    initReveal();
  }

  return { init, toast, toastError, modal, confirm, progressModal, enhance, tabs, dropdown,
    countUp, levelChip, avatar, empty, sectionHead, icon };
})();

/* ---------------------------------------------------------------
   theme manager
--------------------------------------------------------------- */
const theme = (() => {
  const order = ["midnight", "aurora", "ember"];
  const labels = { midnight: "Midnight", aurora: "Aurora", ember: "Ember" };
  function apply(name, quiet = true) {
    if (!labels[name]) name = "midnight";
    document.documentElement.dataset.theme = name;
    const meta = document.querySelector('meta[name="theme-color"]');
    if (meta) meta.content = getComputedStyle(document.body).backgroundColor;
    if (!quiet) bus.emit("theme:changed", name);
  }
  function current() { return document.documentElement.dataset.theme || "midnight"; }
  function cycle() {
    const next = order[(order.indexOf(current()) + 1) % order.length];
    apply(next);
    api.put("/settings", { theme: next }).catch(() => {});
    bus.emit("theme:changed", next);
    ui.toast({ msg: labels[next] + " theme", type: "info", timeout: 1600 });
    return next;
  }
  return { apply, cycle, current, order, labels };
})();

/* ---------------------------------------------------------------
   command palette — fuzzy search over everything
--------------------------------------------------------------- */
const palette = (() => {
  let open = false, sel = 0, items = [], inputEl, listEl, rootEl;

  function actions() {
    return [
      { label: "Load demo data", icon: "spark", hint: "action", run: () => pages.runDemo() },
      { label: "Re-run full analysis", icon: "refresh", hint: "action", run: () => pages.rerunFull() },
      { label: "Import center", icon: "import", hint: "page", run: () => router.go("/import") },
      { label: "Export data as JSON", icon: "download", hint: "action", run: () => pages.exportData("json") },
      { label: "Export decisions as CSV", icon: "download", hint: "action", run: () => pages.exportData("csv") },
      { label: "Export reversals as CSV", icon: "download", hint: "action", run: () => pages.exportData("revcsv") },
      { label: "Print stability report", icon: "book", hint: "action", run: () => pages.printReport() },
      { label: "Simulate live feed", icon: "zap", hint: "toggle", run: () => feed.toggle() },
      { label: "Toggle backend console", icon: "terminal", hint: "toggle", run: () => conz.toggle() },
      { label: "Theme: Midnight", icon: "moon", hint: "theme", run: () => { theme.apply("midnight"); api.put("/settings", { theme: "midnight" }).catch(() => {}); bus.emit("theme:changed", "midnight"); } },
      { label: "Theme: Aurora", icon: "spark", hint: "theme", run: () => { theme.apply("aurora"); api.put("/settings", { theme: "aurora" }).catch(() => {}); bus.emit("theme:changed", "aurora"); } },
      { label: "Theme: Ember", icon: "flame", hint: "theme", run: () => { theme.apply("ember"); api.put("/settings", { theme: "ember" }).catch(() => {}); bus.emit("theme:changed", "ember"); } },
      { label: "Keyboard shortcuts", icon: "command", hint: "action", run: () => shortcuts.toggle(true) },
      { label: "Restart guided tour", icon: "eye", hint: "action", run: () => tour.start() },
      { label: "Reset all data", icon: "trash", hint: "danger", run: () => pages.resetAll() }
    ];
  }

  function allItems() {
    const pageItems = router.pageList().map(p => ({
      label: p.title, icon: p.icon, hint: "page", run: () => router.go(p.path)
    }));
    const topicItems = store.state.topics.filter(t => t.decisionCount).map(t => ({
      label: t.name, icon: "topics", hint: `score ${t.score}`, run: () => router.go("/topics", { topic: t.id })
    }));
    const peopleItems = store.state.people.map(p => ({
      label: p.person, icon: "user", hint: `score ${p.score}`, run: () => router.go("/people", { person: p.person })
    }));
    return [...pageItems, ...actions(), ...topicItems, ...peopleItems];
  }

  // subsequence fuzzy match with a taste for word starts and streaks
  function fuzzy(q, text) {
    q = q.toLowerCase(); text = text.toLowerCase();
    if (!q) return { score: 1, marks: [] };
    let ti = 0, score = 0, streak = 0;
    const marks = [];
    for (let qi = 0; qi < q.length; qi++) {
      const ch = q[qi];
      if (ch === " ") continue;
      let found = text.indexOf(ch, ti);
      if (found === -1) return null;
      if (found === ti && qi > 0) streak += 2; else streak = 0;
      const wordStart = found === 0 || /\W/.test(text[found - 1]);
      score += 1 + streak + (wordStart ? 3 : 0);
      marks.push(found);
      ti = found + 1;
    }
    return { score: score / (q.length + 2), marks };
  }

  function highlight(text, marks) {
    if (!marks?.length) return U.esc(text);
    let out = "", pos = 0;
    [...marks].sort((a, b) => a - b).forEach(m => {
      if (m < pos) return;
      out += U.esc(text.slice(pos, m)) + "<mark>" + U.esc(text[m]) + "</mark>";
      pos = m + 1;
    });
    return out + U.esc(text.slice(pos));
  }

  function renderList(q) {
    const all = allItems();
    const scored = [];
    for (const it of all) {
      const m = fuzzy(q, it.label);
      if (m) scored.push({ it, m });
    }
    scored.sort((a, b) => b.m.score - a.m.score);
    items = scored.slice(0, 10).map(s => s.it);
    items.forEach(it => { it._marks = q ? fuzzy(q, it.label)?.marks : null; });
    sel = 0;
    draw();
  }

  function draw() {
    if (!items.length) {
      listEl.innerHTML = `<div class="pal-empty">Nothing matches — try "demo", "react" or "export"</div>`;
      return;
    }
    listEl.innerHTML = items.map((it, i) => `
      <div class="pal-item ${i === sel ? "sel" : ""}" data-i="${i}" role="option">
        <span class="pal-ico">${icon(it.icon, 14)}</span>
        <span>${highlight(it.label, it._marks)}</span>
        <span class="pal-hint">${U.esc(it.hint || "")}</span>
      </div>`).join("");
    const active = listEl.querySelector(".pal-item.sel");
    if (active) active.scrollIntoView({ block: "nearest" });
  }

  function exec(i) {
    const it = items[i];
    if (!it) return;
    close();
    try { it.run(); } catch (e) { ui.toastError(e.message); }
  }

  function toggle(force) {
    open = force != null ? force : !open;
    rootEl = U.$("#palette");
    rootEl.classList.toggle("open", open);
    rootEl.setAttribute("aria-hidden", String(!open));
    if (open) {
      inputEl = U.$("#pal-input"); listEl = U.$("#pal-list");
      inputEl.value = "";
      renderList("");
      setTimeout(() => inputEl.focus(), 30);
    }
  }

  function init() {
    rootEl = U.$("#palette");
    inputEl = U.$("#pal-input");
    listEl = U.$("#pal-list");
    U.$(".pal-back", rootEl).addEventListener("click", () => toggle(false));
    inputEl.addEventListener("input", () => renderList(inputEl.value.trim()));
    inputEl.addEventListener("keydown", e => {
      if (e.key === "ArrowDown") { e.preventDefault(); sel = Math.min(sel + 1, items.length - 1); draw(); }
      else if (e.key === "ArrowUp") { e.preventDefault(); sel = Math.max(sel - 1, 0); draw(); }
      else if (e.key === "Enter") { e.preventDefault(); exec(sel); }
    });
    listEl.addEventListener("click", e => {
      const el = e.target.closest(".pal-item");
      if (el) exec(+el.dataset.i);
    });
    listEl.addEventListener("pointermove", e => {
      const el = e.target.closest(".pal-item");
      if (el && +el.dataset.i !== sel) { sel = +el.dataset.i; draw(); }
    });
  }

  return { init, toggle, isOpen: () => open };
})();

/* ---------------------------------------------------------------
   shortcuts overlay + global keys
--------------------------------------------------------------- */
const shortcuts = (() => {
  const KEYS = [
    ["command palette", "Ctrl / ⌘ + K"],
    ["shortcut sheet", "?"],
    ["dashboard", "D"], ["timeline", "T"], ["topic explorer", "O"], ["pattern map", "M"],
    ["heatmap", "H"], ["people", "P"], ["import center", "I"], ["alerts", "A"],
    ["how it works", "W"], ["settings", "S"],
    ["undo topic merge", "U"], ["redo topic merge", "Y"],
    ["toggle live feed", "L"], ["backend console", "C"],
    ["close overlay", "Esc"]
  ];
  function toggle(force) {
    const el = U.$("#shortcuts");
    const open = force != null ? force : !el.classList.contains("open");
    el.classList.toggle("open", open);
    el.setAttribute("aria-hidden", String(!open));
  }
  function init() {
    U.$("#sc-rows").innerHTML = KEYS.map(([k, v]) =>
      `<div class="sc-row"><span>${k}</span><span class="kbd">${v}</span></div>`).join("");
    U.$("#shortcuts .sc-back").addEventListener("click", () => toggle(false));
    const route = { d: "/dashboard", t: "/timeline", o: "/topics", m: "/map", h: "/heatmap",
      p: "/people", i: "/import", a: "/alerts", w: "/how", s: "/settings" };
    document.addEventListener("keydown", e => {
      const typing = /^(input|textarea|select)$/i.test(e.target.tagName) || e.target.isContentEditable;
      if ((e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) && e.key.toLowerCase() === "k") {
        e.preventDefault(); palette.toggle(); return;
      }
      if (e.key === "Escape") {
        palette.toggle(false); toggle(false);
        U.$("#mobile-menu").classList.remove("open");
        return;
      }
      if (typing) return;
      if (e.key === "?") { toggle(); return; }
      if (e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey || e.altKey) return;
      const k = e.key.toLowerCase();
      if (route[k]) { router.go(route[k]); return; }
      if (k === "l") feed.toggle();
      else if (k === "c") conz.toggle();
      else if (k === "u") pages.undoTopicOp();
      else if (k === "y") pages.redoTopicOp();
    });
  }
  return { init, toggle };
})();

/* ---------------------------------------------------------------
   onboarding tour — spotlight cutout + bouncing arrow
--------------------------------------------------------------- */
const tour = (() => {
  const steps = [
    { title: "Catch the flip-flops", text: "FlipTrace reads decisions from your chats, emails and task boards — and flags it when the same choice keeps getting reversed.", center: true },
    { sel: "#nav-links", title: "Eleven rooms", text: "Dashboard, timeline, pattern map, heatmap, people… every page is live data, rendered from the local database." },
    { sel: "#console-fab", title: "There's a real backend in here", text: "Open the console to watch every API call, database write and worker message stream by with timestamps." },
    { sel: "#hero-demo", title: "Start with the demo", text: "Three people, three months, four sources, plenty of regret. Loads through the actual pipeline." },
    { sel: "#hero-launch", title: "Then explore", text: "The dashboard shows who's flipping, what's flipping, and how hard. Press Ctrl+K to jump anywhere." }
  ];
  let idx = 0, active = false;
  const root = () => U.$("#tour");

  function render() {
    const st = steps[idx];
    const spot = U.$("#tour-spot"), card = U.$("#tour-card");
    const target = st.sel ? document.querySelector(st.sel) : null;
    const ok = target && target.offsetParent !== null;
    let r = ok ? target.getBoundingClientRect() : null;
    if (!r) {
      // no target (mobile nav, different page) — spotlight the center card area
      spot.style.cssText = "left:50%;top:44%;width:0;height:0;opacity:0";
    } else {
      spot.style.cssText = `left:${r.left - 10}px;top:${r.top - 8}px;width:${r.width + 20}px;height:${r.height + 16}px;opacity:1`;
    }
    const cardW = 330;
    let cx, cy, arrow = "";
    if (!r) { cx = innerWidth / 2 - cardW / 2; cy = innerHeight * 0.3; }
    else {
      cx = U.clamp(r.left + r.width / 2 - cardW / 2, 12, innerWidth - cardW - 12);
      const below = r.bottom + 24, above = r.top - 240;
      if (below + 210 < innerHeight) { cy = below; arrow = `<span class="tour-arrow" style="left:${U.clamp(r.left + r.width / 2 - cx - 8, 8, cardW - 30)}px;top:-26px;transform:rotate(180deg)">${icon("chevronDown", 22)}</span>`; }
      else { cy = Math.max(12, above); arrow = `<span class="tour-arrow" style="left:${U.clamp(r.left + r.width / 2 - cx - 8, 8, cardW - 30)}px;bottom:-26px">${icon("chevronDown", 22)}</span>`; }
    }
    card.style.cssText += `;left:${cx}px;top:${cy}px`;
    card.innerHTML = `
      ${arrow}
      <div class="tour-step">step ${idx + 1} / ${steps.length}</div>
      <h3 style="margin:6px 0">${U.esc(st.title)}</h3>
      <p class="muted small" style="margin:0">${U.esc(st.text)}</p>
      <div class="tour-dots">${steps.map((_, i) => `<i class="${i === idx ? "on" : ""}"></i>`).join("")}</div>
      <div style="display:flex;gap:8px;justify-content:flex-end">
        ${idx > 0 ? '<button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" data-act="back">Back</button>' : ""}
        <button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" data-act="skip">Skip</button>
        <button class="btn btn-primary btn-sm" data-act="next"><span class="sweep"></span>${idx === steps.length - 1 ? "Finish" : "Next"}</button>
      </div>`;
    card.querySelectorAll("[data-act]").forEach(b => b.addEventListener("click", () => act(b.dataset.act)));
  }

  function act(a) {
    if (a === "next" && idx < steps.length - 1) { idx++; render(); }
    else if (a === "back" && idx > 0) { idx--; render(); }
    else stop(true);
  }

  function start() {
    if (active) return;
    active = true; idx = 0;
    root().classList.add("open");
    root().setAttribute("aria-hidden", "false");
    render();
    window.addEventListener("resize", render);
  }
  function stop(done) {
    active = false;
    root().classList.remove("open");
    root().setAttribute("aria-hidden", "true");
    window.removeEventListener("resize", render);
    if (done) {
      store.state.settings.tourDone = true;
      api.put("/settings", { tourDone: true }).catch(() => {});
    }
  }
  function init() {
    // first visit? roll out the carpet
    if (!store.state.settings.tourDone && !store.stats().decisions) {
      setTimeout(() => start(), 1400);
    }
  }
  return { init, start, stop };
})();


/* ---------------------------------------------------------------
   charts — canvas drawn by hand, animated on entry and on change
--------------------------------------------------------------- */
const charts = (() => {
  let C = {};
  function refreshColors() {
    const cs = getComputedStyle(document.body);
    const grab = n => cs.getPropertyValue(n).trim();
    C = { ac1: grab("--ac1"), ac2: grab("--ac2"), ac3: grab("--ac3"), ok: grab("--ok"),
      warn: grab("--warn"), danger: grab("--danger"), text: grab("--text"),
      muted: grab("--muted"), faint: grab("--faint"), bg0: grab("--bg-0"), bg2: grab("--bg-2") };
  }
  bus.on("theme:changed", refreshColors);

  function fitCanvas(canvas) {
    const dpr = Math.min(2, window.devicePixelRatio || 1);
    const w = canvas.clientWidth || canvas.width, h = canvas.clientHeight || canvas.height;
    if (canvas.width !== Math.round(w * dpr) || canvas.height !== Math.round(h * dpr)) {
      canvas.width = Math.round(w * dpr); canvas.height = Math.round(h * dpr);
    }
    const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
    ctx.setTransform(dpr, 0, 0, dpr, 0, 0);
    return { ctx, w, h };
  }

  /* ---- Instability Pulse — an ECG whose beat tracks the data ---- */
  function pulse(canvas) {
    let raf = null, running = false;
    let phase = 0, last = performance.now();
    let instability = 0.15, spikes = [];   // {x0} normalized recent reversal marks
    const history = new Array(240).fill(0);
    let beatAt = 0;

    function beat() {
      // one heartbeat waveform; taller and faster as instability climbs
      return t => {
        if (t < 0) t = 0;
        if (t > 1) t = 1;
        const spike = Math.exp(-Math.pow((t - 0.18) / 0.055, 2)) * 1;
        const dip = -Math.exp(-Math.pow((t - 0.34) / 0.07, 2)) * 0.34;
        const bump = Math.exp(-Math.pow((t - 0.62) / 0.16, 2)) * 0.22;
        return (spike + dip + bump) * (0.22 + instability * 0.78);
      };
    }

    function frame(now) {
      if (!running) return;
      const dt = Math.min(0.05, (now - last) / 1000); last = now;
      const { ctx, w, h } = fitCanvas(canvas);
      const beatDur = U.lerp(1.5, 0.5, instability);
      phase += dt / beatDur;
      if (phase >= 1) { phase -= 1; beatAt = now; }
      const wf = beat();
      const since = (now - beatAt) / 1000 / beatDur;
      history.push(wf(since) + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.02 * (0.4 + instability));
      history.shift();

      ctx.clearRect(0, 0, w, h);
      // grid
      ctx.strokeStyle = "rgba(148,163,255,0.07)"; ctx.lineWidth = 1;
      for (let gy = 1; gy < 4; gy++) { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(0, h * gy / 4); ctx.lineTo(w, h * gy / 4); ctx.stroke(); }

      const base = h * 0.68;
      const grad = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, w, 0);
      grad.addColorStop(0, C.ac1); grad.addColorStop(0.6, C.ac2); grad.addColorStop(1, C.ac3);
      ctx.beginPath();
      history.forEach((v, i) => {
        const x = (i / (history.length - 1)) * w;
        const y = base - v * h * 0.52;
        i ? ctx.lineTo(x, y) : ctx.moveTo(x, y);
      });
      ctx.strokeStyle = grad; ctx.lineWidth = 2;
      ctx.shadowColor = C.ac1; ctx.shadowBlur = 12;
      ctx.stroke(); ctx.shadowBlur = 0;

      // reversal markers along the baseline (recent = brighter)
      const recent = store.state.reversals.filter(r => Date.now() - r.ts < 30 * 86400e3);
      recent.slice(-14).forEach((r, i) => {
        const age = (Date.now() - r.ts) / (30 * 86400e3);
        const x = w - 8 - (i / 14) * (w - 20);
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(x, base + 10, 3, 0, 7);
        ctx.fillStyle = r.type === "boomerang" ? C.ac3 : C.warn;
        ctx.globalAlpha = 1 - age; ctx.fill();
        if (r.type === "boomerang") { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(x, base + 10, 6, 0, 7); ctx.strokeStyle = C.ac3; ctx.globalAlpha = (1 - age) * 0.5; ctx.stroke(); }
        ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
      });
      raf = requestAnimationFrame(frame);
    }

    return {
      start() { if (!running) { running = true; last = performance.now(); raf = requestAnimationFrame(frame); } },
      stop() { running = false; if (raf) cancelAnimationFrame(raf); },
      setInstability(v) { instability = U.clamp(v, 0, 1); }
    };
  }

  /* ---- radial gauge ---- */
  function gauge(canvas, value, { label = "", level = "", animate = true, size = 190 } = {}) {
    let raf = null, shown = 0, target = value, t0 = performance.now();
    const A0 = Math.PI * 0.75, A1 = Math.PI * 2.25;

    function draw() {
      const { ctx, w, h } = fitCanvas(canvas);
      const cx = w / 2, cy = h / 2 + 6, r = Math.min(w, h) / 2 - 18;
      ctx.clearRect(0, 0, w, h);
      ctx.lineCap = "round";
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(cx, cy, r, A0, A1);
      ctx.strokeStyle = "rgba(148,163,255,0.12)"; ctx.lineWidth = 11; ctx.stroke();

      const frac = U.clamp(shown / 100, 0, 1);
      const grad = ctx.createLinearGradient(cx - r, cy - r, cx + r, cy + r);
      grad.addColorStop(0, C.ac1); grad.addColorStop(0.55, C.ac3); grad.addColorStop(1, levelIdxColor(value));
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(cx, cy, r, A0, A0 + (A1 - A0) * frac);
      ctx.strokeStyle = grad; ctx.lineWidth = 11;
      ctx.shadowColor = grad; ctx.shadowBlur = 16;
      ctx.stroke(); ctx.shadowBlur = 0;

      // ticks
      for (let i = 0; i <= 10; i++) {
        const a = A0 + (A1 - A0) * (i / 10);
        const inner = frac >= i / 10 ? r - 15 : r - 13;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(cx + Math.cos(a) * inner, cy + Math.sin(a) * inner);
        ctx.lineTo(cx + Math.cos(a) * (r - 20), cy + Math.sin(a) * (r - 20));
        ctx.strokeStyle = frac >= i / 10 ? "rgba(255,255,255,.35)" : "rgba(148,163,255,.18)";
        ctx.lineWidth = frac >= i / 10 ? 2 : 1;
        ctx.stroke();
      }

      ctx.textAlign = "center";
      ctx.fillStyle = C.text; ctx.font = `700 ${r * 0.42}px Space Grotesk, sans-serif`;
      ctx.fillText(Math.round(shown), cx, cy + r * 0.12);
      ctx.fillStyle = C.muted; ctx.font = `600 ${Math.max(10, r * 0.11)}px Manrope, sans-serif`;
      ctx.fillText(label, cx, cy + r * 0.38);
      if (level) {
        ctx.font = `800 ${Math.max(10, r * 0.1)}px Manrope, sans-serif`;
        ctx.fillStyle = levelIdxColor(value);
        ctx.fillText(level.toUpperCase(), cx, cy + r * 0.62);
      }
    }
    function levelIdxColor(v) {
      return v < 20 ? C.ok : v < 40 ? C.ac2 : v < 60 ? C.warn : v < 80 ? "#ff9a5c" : C.ac3;
    }
    function tick(now) {
      const k = animate ? U.clamp((now - t0) / 1000, 0, 1) : 1;
      shown = value * (1 - Math.pow(1 - k, 3));
      draw();
      if (k < 1) raf = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    }
    raf = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    return {
      update(v, lvl) { value = v; level = lvl || level; t0 = performance.now() - 250; if (!raf) raf = requestAnimationFrame(tick); },
      redraw: draw
    };
  }

  /* ---- radar chart ---- */
  function radar(canvas, labels, values, { animate = true } = {}) {
    let raf = null, t0 = performance.now();
    function draw(now) {
      const { ctx, w, h } = fitCanvas(canvas);
      const cx = w / 2, cy = h / 2, r = Math.min(w, h) / 2 - 34;
      const n = labels.length;
      const k = animate ? U.clamp((now - t0) / 900, 0, 1) : 1;
      const ease = 1 - Math.pow(1 - k, 3);
      ctx.clearRect(0, 0, w, h);
      const angle = i => -Math.PI / 2 + i * 2 * Math.PI / n;

      for (let ring = 1; ring <= 4; ring++) {
        ctx.beginPath();
        for (let i = 0; i <= n; i++) {
          const a = angle(i % n), rr = r * ring / 4;
          const x = cx + Math.cos(a) * rr, y = cy + Math.sin(a) * rr;
          i ? ctx.lineTo(x, y) : ctx.moveTo(x, y);
        }
        ctx.strokeStyle = "rgba(148,163,255,0.13)"; ctx.lineWidth = 1; ctx.stroke();
      }
      for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        const a = angle(i);
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(cx, cy);
        ctx.lineTo(cx + Math.cos(a) * r, cy + Math.sin(a) * r);
        ctx.strokeStyle = "rgba(148,163,255,0.13)"; ctx.stroke();
        ctx.fillStyle = C.muted; ctx.font = "600 10.5px Manrope, sans-serif";
        ctx.textAlign = Math.abs(Math.cos(a)) < 0.3 ? "center" : Math.cos(a) > 0 ? "left" : "right";
        ctx.fillText(labels[i], cx + Math.cos(a) * (r + 14), cy + Math.sin(a) * (r + 14) + 3);
      }

      ctx.beginPath();
      for (let i = 0; i <= n; i++) {
        const a = angle(i % n), v = U.clamp(values[i % n] ?? 0, 0, 1) * ease;
        const x = cx + Math.cos(a) * r * v, y = cy + Math.sin(a) * r * v;
        i ? ctx.lineTo(x, y) : ctx.moveTo(x, y);
      }
      ctx.closePath();
      const grad = ctx.createRadialGradient(cx, cy, 0, cx, cy, r);
      grad.addColorStop(0, "rgba(124,92,255,0.45)"); grad.addColorStop(1, "rgba(34,211,238,0.08)");
      ctx.fillStyle = grad; ctx.fill();
      ctx.strokeStyle = C.ac1; ctx.lineWidth = 2;
      ctx.shadowColor = C.ac1; ctx.shadowBlur = 14; ctx.stroke(); ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
      for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        const a = angle(i), v = U.clamp(values[i] ?? 0, 0, 1) * ease;
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(cx + Math.cos(a) * r * v, cy + Math.sin(a) * r * v, 3.4, 0, 7);
        ctx.fillStyle = C.ac2; ctx.fill();
      }
      if (k < 1) raf = requestAnimationFrame(draw);
    }
    raf = requestAnimationFrame(draw);
    return { stop: () => raf && cancelAnimationFrame(raf) };
  }

  function init() { refreshColors(); }
  return { init, refreshColors, fitCanvas, pulse, gauge, radar, colors: () => C };
})();

/* ---------------------------------------------------------------
   background particle field — reacts to the pointer, changes
   temperament per page
--------------------------------------------------------------- */
const bg = (() => {
  const MOODS = {
    calm:   { count: 150, speed: 0.35, swirl: 0.12, attract: 0.4,  link: 115, jitter: 0.05, colors: ["ac1", "ac2"] },
    swirl:  { count: 170, speed: 0.55, swirl: 0.9,  attract: 0.22, link: 135, jitter: 0.08, colors: ["ac1", "ac3", "ac2"] },
    frantic:{ count: 160, speed: 1.5,  swirl: 1.5,  attract: -0.45, link: 95, jitter: 1.1, colors: ["danger", "ac3", "warn"] },
    matrix: { count: 140, speed: 0.28, swirl: 0.04, attract: 0.5,  link: 100, jitter: 0.03, colors: ["ok", "ac2"] },
    ember:  { count: 150, speed: 0.85, swirl: 0.45, attract: 0.3,  link: 125, jitter: 0.25, colors: ["warn", "danger", "ac1"] },
    focus:  { count: 110, speed: 0.22, swirl: 0.06, attract: 0.55, link: 90,  jitter: 0.02, colors: ["ac2", "ac1"] }
  };
  let canvas, ctx, W, H, raf = null;
  let parts = [];
  const mouse = { x: -1e4, y: -1e4, down: false, nx: 0.5, ny: 0.5 };
  let mood = { ...MOODS.calm }, target = { ...MOODS.calm };
  let colorList = [];

  function resolveColors() {
    const C = charts.colors();
    const map = { ac1: C.ac1, ac2: C.ac2, ac3: C.ac3, ok: C.ok, warn: C.warn, danger: C.danger };
    colorList = mood.colors.map(k => map[k] || C.ac1);
  }
  bus.on("theme:changed", resolveColors);

  function targetCount() {
    const area = Math.min(1.4, (W * H) / (1440 * 900));
    return Math.round(target.count * area * (W < 700 ? 0.55 : 1));
  }

  function spawn() {
    return {
      x: Math.random() * W, y: Math.random() * H,
      vx: (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.3, vy: (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.3,
      z: 0.25 + Math.random() * 0.75,          // depth for parallax + size
      ci: Math.floor(Math.random() * 3),
      ph: Math.random() * Math.PI * 2
    };
  }
  function syncCount() {
    const want = targetCount();
    while (parts.length < want) parts.push(spawn());
    if (parts.length > want) parts.length = want;
    resolveColors();
  }

  function frame() {
    // ease the mood toward its target so page changes feel like weather, not switches
    for (const k of ["speed", "swirl", "attract", "link", "jitter"]) mood[k] = U.lerp(mood[k], target[k], 0.02);
    if (Math.abs(mood.count - target.count) > 4) mood.count = U.lerp(mood.count, target.count, 0.05);
    syncCount();

    const C = charts.colors();
    const cx = W / 2, cy = H / 2;
    const jitter = mood.jitter, speedK = mood.speed;

    for (const p of parts) {
      const dx = p.x - cx, dy = p.y - cy;
      const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
      // slow orbit around the middle
      p.vx += (-dy / d) * mood.swirl * 0.02 * p.z;
      p.vy += (dx / d) * mood.swirl * 0.02 * p.z;
      // pointer field
      const mdx = p.x - mouse.x, mdy = p.y - mouse.y;
      const md = Math.hypot(mdx, mdy);
      const R = 190;
      if (md < R) {
        const f = (1 - md / R) * (mouse.down ? -2.2 : mood.attract) * 0.55;
        p.vx -= (mdx / (md || 1)) * f;
        p.vy -= (mdy / (md || 1)) * f;
      }
      p.vx += (Math.random() - 0.5) * jitter * 0.35;
      p.vy += (Math.random() - 0.5) * jitter * 0.35;
      p.vx *= 0.94; p.vy *= 0.94;
      const vmax = 1.8 * speedK + 0.25;
      const v = Math.hypot(p.vx, p.vy);
      if (v > vmax) { p.vx = p.vx / v * vmax; p.vy = p.vy / v * vmax; }
      p.x += p.vx * (0.4 + p.z) * speedK + 0.12;
      p.y += p.vy * (0.4 + p.z) * speedK;
      if (p.x < -30) p.x = W + 30; if (p.x > W + 30) p.x = -30;
      if (p.y < -30) p.y = H + 30; if (p.y > H + 30) p.y = -30;
    }

    ctx.clearRect(0, 0, W, H);
    const LD = mood.link;
    for (let i = 0; i < parts.length; i++) {
      const a = parts[i];
      for (let j = i + 1; j < parts.length; j++) {
        const b = parts[j];
        const dx = a.x - b.x; if (dx > LD || dx < -LD) continue;
        const dy = a.y - b.y; if (dy > LD || dy < -LD) continue;
        const d2 = dx * dx + dy * dy;
        if (d2 > LD * LD) continue;
        const alpha = (1 - Math.sqrt(d2) / LD) * 0.16 * Math.min(a.z, b.z);
        if (alpha < 0.015) continue;
        ctx.strokeStyle = colorList[a.ci % colorList.length] || C.ac1;
        ctx.globalAlpha = alpha;
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(a.x, a.y); ctx.lineTo(b.x, b.y); ctx.stroke();
      }
    }
    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
    for (const p of parts) {
      const par = (mouse.nx - 0.5) * 26 * p.z * p.z;   // gentle parallax drift
      const r = 0.7 + p.z * 1.7;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(p.x + par, p.y + (mouse.ny - 0.5) * 16 * p.z * p.z, r, 0, 7);
      ctx.fillStyle = colorList[p.ci % colorList.length] || C.ac1;
      ctx.globalAlpha = 0.25 + p.z * 0.6;
      ctx.fill();
    }
    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
    raf = requestAnimationFrame(frame);
  }

  function resize() {
    const dpr = Math.min(1.75, window.devicePixelRatio || 1);
    W = innerWidth; H = innerHeight;
    canvas.width = Math.round(W * dpr); canvas.height = Math.round(H * dpr);
    ctx.setTransform(dpr, 0, 0, dpr, 0, 0);
    syncCount();
  }

  function setMood(name) {
    target = { ...(MOODS[name] || MOODS.calm) };
  }

  function init() {
    canvas = U.$("#bg-canvas");
    ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
    resize();
    window.addEventListener("resize", resize);
    window.addEventListener("pointermove", e => {
      mouse.x = e.clientX; mouse.y = e.clientY;
      mouse.nx = e.clientX / innerWidth; mouse.ny = e.clientY / innerHeight;
    }, { passive: true });
    window.addEventListener("pointerdown", () => { mouse.down = true; });
    window.addEventListener("pointerup", () => { mouse.down = false; });
    document.addEventListener("visibilitychange", () => {
      if (document.hidden) { if (raf) cancelAnimationFrame(raf); raf = null; }
      else if (!raf && !store.state.settings.calm && !REDUCED) raf = requestAnimationFrame(frame);
    });
    if (store.state.settings.calm || REDUCED) {
      // draw a single static frame so the page still has depth
      setTimeout(() => { syncCount(); for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) { /* settle */ } ctx.globalAlpha = 1; }, 100);
      canvas.style.opacity = "0.5";
    } else {
      raf = requestAnimationFrame(frame);
    }
  }
  function recalibrate() {
    // called when calm mode flips
    if (store.state.settings.calm || REDUCED) {
      if (raf) cancelAnimationFrame(raf); raf = null;
    } else if (!raf) raf = requestAnimationFrame(frame);
  }

  return { init, setMood, recalibrate };
})();

/* ---------------------------------------------------------------
   custom cursor + click sparks
--------------------------------------------------------------- */
const cursor = (() => {
  let dot, ring, sparksCanvas, sctx, raf = null, enabled = false;
  let rx = innerWidth / 2, ry = innerHeight / 2, tx = rx, ty = ry;
  const burstParts = [];

  function burst(x, y, n = 14, colors) {
    if (!sparksCanvas) return;
    const C = charts.colors();
    const pal = colors || [C.ac1, C.ac2, C.ac3];
    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
      const a = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2, sp = 1.5 + Math.random() * 4;
      burstParts.push({ x, y, vx: Math.cos(a) * sp, vy: Math.sin(a) * sp, life: 1, r: 1 + Math.random() * 2.4, c: pal[i % pal.length] });
    }
    if (!raf2) raf2 = requestAnimationFrame(sparksFrame);
  }
  let raf2 = null;
  function sparksFrame() {
    const dpr = Math.min(2, devicePixelRatio || 1);
    sctx.clearRect(0, 0, sparksCanvas.width / dpr, sparksCanvas.height / dpr);
    for (let i = burstParts.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
      const p = burstParts[i];
      p.x += p.vx; p.y += p.vy; p.vy += 0.07; p.vx *= 0.985; p.life -= 0.022;
      if (p.life <= 0) { burstParts.splice(i, 1); continue; }
      sctx.globalAlpha = p.life;
      sctx.beginPath(); sctx.arc(p.x, p.y, p.r * p.life, 0, 7);
      sctx.fillStyle = p.c; sctx.fill();
    }
    sctx.globalAlpha = 1;
    if (burstParts.length) raf2 = requestAnimationFrame(sparksFrame);
    else { raf2 = null; sctx.clearRect(0, 0, sparksCanvas.width, sparksCanvas.height); }
  }

  function loop() {
    rx = U.lerp(rx, tx, 0.18); ry = U.lerp(ry, ty, 0.18);
    ring.style.transform = `translate(${rx}px,${ry}px) translate(-50%,-50%)`;
    if (enabled) raf = requestAnimationFrame(loop);
  }

  const HOT = "a,button,.tab,.chip,.tilt,input,select,textarea,.heat-cell,.pal-item,.switch,.check,label";

  function enable() {
    if (COARSE || REDUCED) return;
    enabled = true;
    document.body.classList.add("has-cursor");
    raf = requestAnimationFrame(loop);
  }
  function disable() {
    enabled = false;
    document.body.classList.remove("has-cursor");
    if (raf) cancelAnimationFrame(raf);
  }

  function init() {
    dot = U.$("#cursor-dot"); ring = U.$("#cursor-ring");
    sparksCanvas = U.$("#sparks"); sctx = sparksCanvas.getContext("2d");
    const rs = () => {
      const dpr = Math.min(2, devicePixelRatio || 1);
      sparksCanvas.width = innerWidth * dpr; sparksCanvas.height = innerHeight * dpr;
      sctx.setTransform(dpr, 0, 0, dpr, 0, 0);
    };
    rs(); window.addEventListener("resize", rs);

    window.addEventListener("pointermove", e => {
      tx = e.clientX; ty = e.clientY;
      if (enabled) dot.style.transform = `translate(${tx}px,${ty}px) translate(-50%,-50%)`;
    }, { passive: true });
    document.addEventListener("pointerover", e => {
      ring.classList.toggle("hovering", !!e.target.closest(HOT));
    });
    window.addEventListener("pointerdown", e => { ring.classList.add("pressing"); });
    window.addEventListener("pointerup", () => ring.classList.remove("pressing"));
    window.addEventListener("click", e => {
      if (!enabled) return;
      burst(e.clientX, e.clientY, 12);
    });
    if (store.state.settings.cursor) enable();
  }

  return { init, enable, disable, burst, get enabled() { return enabled; } };
})();

/* ---------------------------------------------------------------
   clip engine — small keyframe timeline driving the explainer
   "videos" (canvas scenes, no video files anywhere)
--------------------------------------------------------------- */
const clips = (() => {
  const E = {
    linear: t => t,
    in: t => t * t,
    out: t => 1 - (1 - t) * (1 - t),
    inout: t => t < 0.5 ? 2 * t * t : 1 - 2 * (1 - t) * (1 - t),
    back: t => 1 + 2.70158 * Math.pow(t - 1, 3) + 1.70158 * Math.pow(t - 1, 2)
  };
  // interpolate a value across keyframes [[t0,v0],[t1,v1],…]
  function kf(t, frames, ease = "inout") {
    if (t <= frames[0][0]) return frames[0][1];
    for (let i = 1; i < frames.length; i++) {
      if (t <= frames[i][0]) {
        const [t0, v0] = frames[i - 1], [t1, v1] = frames[i];
        return v0 + (v1 - v0) * E[ease]((t - t0) / (t1 - t0));
      }
    }
    return frames[frames.length - 1][1];
  }
  const R = (ctx, x, y, w, h, r) => {
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(x + r, y);
    ctx.arcTo(x + w, y, x + w, y + h, r); ctx.arcTo(x + w, y + h, x, y + h, r);
    ctx.arcTo(x, y + h, x, y, r); ctx.arcTo(x, y, x + w, y, r);
    ctx.closePath();
  };

  class Player {
    constructor(canvas, { duration = 11, draw, captions = [], captionEl = null }) {
      this.canvas = canvas; this.duration = duration; this.draw = draw;
      this.captions = captions; this.captionEl = captionEl;
      this.playing = false; this.t = 0; this.last = 0; this.raf = null;
      this.holdUntil = 0;
    }
    get k() { return this.t / this.duration; }
    frame(now) {
      if (!this.playing) return;
      if (!this.last) this.last = now;
      const dt = (now - this.last) / 1000; this.last = now;
      if (now > this.holdUntil) this.t += dt;
      if (this.t >= this.duration) { this.t = 0; this.holdUntil = now + 900; }
      this.render();
      this.onTick && this.onTick();
      this.raf = requestAnimationFrame(this.frame.bind(this));
    }
    render() {
      const dpr = Math.min(2, devicePixelRatio || 1);
      const w = this.canvas.clientWidth || 300, h = this.canvas.clientHeight || 170;
      if (this.canvas.width !== w * dpr) { this.canvas.width = w * dpr; this.canvas.height = h * dpr; }
      const ctx = this.canvas.getContext("2d");
      ctx.setTransform(dpr, 0, 0, dpr, 0, 0);
      ctx.clearRect(0, 0, w, h);
      this.draw(ctx, this.k, w, h, { kf, E, R, C: charts.colors() });
      this.caption(this.k);
    }
    caption(k) {
      if (!this.captionEl) return;
      const cur = [...this.captions].reverse().find(c => k >= c.t);
      if (!cur) { this.captionEl.innerHTML = ""; return; }
      const chars = Math.floor(U.clamp((k - cur.t) / 0.16, 0, 1) * cur.text.length);
      const text = cur.text.slice(0, chars);
      if (this.captionEl.dataset.cap !== cur.text) this.captionEl.dataset.cap = cur.text;
      if (this.captionEl.textContent !== text)
        this.captionEl.innerHTML = U.esc(text) + (chars < cur.text.length ? '<span class="caret"></span>' : "");
    }
    play() { if (this.playing) return; this.playing = true; this.last = 0; this.raf = requestAnimationFrame(this.frame.bind(this)); }
    pause() { this.playing = false; if (this.raf) cancelAnimationFrame(this.raf); }
    toggle() { this.playing ? this.pause() : this.play(); }
    seek(k) { this.t = U.clamp(k, 0, 1) * this.duration; this.render(); }
  }

  function mount(container, scene) {
    container.innerHTML = `
      <div class="clip-box">
        <canvas class="clip-canvas" role="img" aria-label="${U.esc(scene.aria || scene.title)}"></canvas>
        <div class="clip-bar">
          <button class="clip-play" aria-label="Play or pause">${icon("play", 14)}</button>
          <input class="clip-scrub range" type="range" min="0" max="1000" value="0" aria-label="Clip position">
          <span class="clip-time">0:00 / 0:00</span>
          <button class="clip-play" style="background:var(--glass-2);color:var(--muted)" aria-label="Replay">${icon("replay", 14)}</button>
        </div>
        <div class="clip-caption"></div>
      </div>`;
    const canvas = container.querySelector("canvas");
    const capEl = container.querySelector(".clip-caption");
    const player = new Player(canvas, { duration: scene.duration, draw: scene.draw, captions: scene.captions, captionEl: capEl });
    const btn = container.querySelector(".clip-play");
    const replay = container.querySelectorAll(".clip-play")[1];
    const scrub = container.querySelector(".clip-scrub");
    const timeEl = container.querySelector(".clip-time");
    const fmt = s => `${Math.floor(s / 60)}:${String(Math.floor(s % 60)).padStart(2, "0")}`;
    player.onTick = () => {
      scrub.value = Math.round(player.k * 1000);
      timeEl.textContent = `${fmt(player.t)} / ${fmt(player.duration)}`;
    };
    btn.addEventListener("click", () => {
      player.toggle();
      btn.innerHTML = icon(player.playing ? "pause" : "play", 14);
    });
    replay.addEventListener("click", () => { player.t = 0; player.play(); btn.innerHTML = icon("pause", 14); });
    let scrubbing = false;
    scrub.addEventListener("pointerdown", () => { scrubbing = true; player.pause(); });
    scrub.addEventListener("input", () => player.seek(scrub.value / 1000));
    window.addEventListener("pointerup", () => { if (scrubbing) { scrubbing = false; if (autoWanted()) player.play(); btn.innerHTML = icon(player.playing ? "pause" : "play", 14); } });
    const autoWanted = () => container.dataset.auto === "1" && !REDUCED && !store.state.settings.calm;
    const io = new IntersectionObserver(entries => {
      entries.forEach(en => {
        container.dataset.auto = en.isIntersecting ? "1" : "0";
        if (en.isIntersecting && autoWanted()) { player.play(); btn.innerHTML = icon("pause", 14); }
        else if (!en.isIntersecting) player.pause();
      });
    }, { threshold: 0.55 });
    io.observe(canvas);
    player.render();
    return player;
  }

  /* ---------- the four scenes ---------- */

  const chatBubble = (ctx, x, y, w, h, alpha, C, hiLine = -1, hiA = 0) => {
    ctx.globalAlpha = alpha;
    R(ctx, x, y, w, h, 10);
    ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(148,163,255,0.10)"; ctx.fill();
    ctx.strokeStyle = "rgba(148,163,255,0.28)"; ctx.stroke();
    for (let l = 0; l < 3; l++) {
      const lw = w * (l === 1 ? 0.72 : 0.5);
      ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(170,180,230,0.35)";
      R(ctx, x + 12, y + 12 + l * 13, lw, 6, 3); ctx.fill();
    }
    if (hiLine >= 0) {
      ctx.globalAlpha = alpha * hiA;
      R(ctx, x + 8, y + 8 + hiLine * 13, w - 16, 12, 5);
      ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(34,211,238,0.35)"; ctx.fill();
      ctx.strokeStyle = C.ac2; ctx.stroke();
    }
    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
  };

  const chip = (ctx, x, y, text, C, scale = 1, glow = 0.12) => {
    ctx.save();
    ctx.translate(x, y); ctx.scale(scale, scale);
    ctx.font = "700 11px Manrope, sans-serif";
    const w = ctx.measureText(text).width + 26;
    R(ctx, -w / 2, -11, w, 22, 11);
    ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(124,92,255,0.16)"; ctx.fill();
    ctx.strokeStyle = C.ac1; ctx.lineWidth = 1.4;
    ctx.shadowColor = C.ac1; ctx.shadowBlur = 14 * glow * 8;
    ctx.stroke(); ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
    ctx.fillStyle = "#e9edff"; ctx.textAlign = "center"; ctx.textBaseline = "middle";
    ctx.fillText(text, 0, 1);
    ctx.restore();
    return w;
  };

  const scenes = {
    clip1: {
      title: "Messages become decisions", duration: 12, aria: "Chat bubbles are scanned and decisions pop out as chips",
      captions: [{ t: 0.02, text: "Chats, emails and task boards pour in as raw text…" },
                 { t: 0.42, text: "a scanner hunts for commitment and reversal language…" },
                 { t: 0.72, text: "and every real decision pops out with a confidence score." }],
      draw(ctx, k, w, h, { kf, E, R, C }) {
        const names = ["Sam:", "Priya:", "Sam:"];
        const lines = ["let's go with React — final answer", "ok, updating the specs", "actually, switching to Vue instead"];
        for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
          const t0 = i * 0.09;
          const x = kf(k, [[t0, -w], [t0 + 0.14, 26]], "out");
          const y = 20 + i * 58;
          const hi = i === 2 ? 1 : -1;
          const hiA = kf(k, [[0.48, 0], [0.55, 1]], "inout");
          chatBubble(ctx, x, y, w * 0.52, 48, 1, C, hi, hiA);
          ctx.globalAlpha = 1; ctx.fillStyle = i === 0 ? C.ac2 : i === 1 ? C.ok : C.ac3;
          ctx.font = "700 10px Manrope"; ctx.textAlign = "left";
          ctx.fillText(names[i], x + 12, y - 6);
        }
        // scanning beam
        const bx = kf(k, [[0.4, 0], [0.62, w]], "inout");
        if (k > 0.4 && k < 0.62) {
          const g = ctx.createLinearGradient(bx - 30, 0, bx + 30, 0);
          g.addColorStop(0, "rgba(34,211,238,0)"); g.addColorStop(0.5, "rgba(34,211,238,0.5)"); g.addColorStop(1, "rgba(34,211,238,0)");
          ctx.fillStyle = g; ctx.fillRect(bx - 30, 10, 60, h - 20);
        }
        // decision chips fly out to the right
        const opts = [["go with React", 0.5], ["switching to Vue", 0.62], ["final answer", 0.74]];
        opts.forEach(([txt, t0], i) => {
          if (k < t0) return;
          const kk = U.clamp((k - t0) / 0.12, 0, 1);
          const x = kf(kk, [[0, w * 0.55], [1, w * 0.78]], "back");
          const y = 30 + i * 46 + kf(kk, [[0, 60], [1, 0]], "out");
          chip(ctx, x, y, txt, C, E.back(kk), 1);
          if (kk >= 1) {
            ctx.font = "600 9px JetBrains Mono, monospace"; ctx.fillStyle = C.muted; ctx.textAlign = "left";
            ctx.fillText("0.88 confidence", x + 46, y + 3);
          }
        });
      }
    },
    clip2: {
      title: "Same topic, different days", duration: 12, aria: "Scattered decision chips are pulled into topic clusters",
      captions: [{ t: 0.02, text: "Decisions made weeks apart look unrelated at first…" },
                 { t: 0.4, text: "TF-IDF similarity and shared option vocabulary pull them together…" },
                 { t: 0.75, text: "until each topic holds the whole story." }],
      draw(ctx, k, w, h, { kf, E, R, C }) {
        const clusters = [
          { label: "framework choice", x: w * 0.3, y: h * 0.36, color: C.ac1,
            chips: ["react", "vue", "svelte", "react", "vue"] },
          { label: "diet plan", x: w * 0.72, y: h * 0.62, color: C.ac3,
            chips: ["keto", "vegan", "keto"] },
          { label: "meeting time", x: w * 0.62, y: h * 0.2, color: C.ac2,
            chips: ["tuesday 10am", "monday 9am"] }
        ];
        const seed = i => ({ x: (Math.sin(i * 12.9898) * 43758.5453 % 1 + 1) % 1, y: (Math.sin(i * 78.233) * 12543.12 % 1 + 1) % 1 });
        let idx = 0;
        clusters.forEach((cl, ci) => {
          // attractor ring
          const ra = kf(k, [[0.3 + ci * 0.05, 0], [0.45 + ci * 0.05, 1]], "out");
          ctx.globalAlpha = ra * 0.8;
          ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(cl.x, cl.y, 52, 0, 7);
          ctx.strokeStyle = cl.color; ctx.setLineDash([4, 6]); ctx.stroke(); ctx.setLineDash([]);
          ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
          cl.chips.forEach(() => {
            const i = idx++;
            const s = seed(i + ci * 31);
            const kk = kf(k, [[0.18 + (i % 5) * 0.06, 0], [0.62, 1]], "inout");
            const tx = s.x * (w - 90) + 45, ty = s.y * (h - 60) + 30;
            const ang = i * 2.4 + k * 2;
            const ox = Math.cos(ang) * (30 - 22 * kk), oy = Math.sin(ang) * (22 - 16 * kk);
            const x = tx + (cl.x + ox - tx) * kk, y = ty + (cl.y + oy - ty) * kk;
            chip(ctx, x, y, cl.chips[i % cl.chips.length], C, 0.85, kk);
          });
          if (k > 0.72) {
            ctx.font = "700 11px Manrope"; ctx.textAlign = "center";
            ctx.fillStyle = cl.color;
            ctx.globalAlpha = kf(k, [[0.72, 0], [0.8, 1]], "out");
            ctx.fillText(cl.label, cl.x, cl.y + 70);
            ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
          }
        });
      }
    },
    clip3: {
      title: "The flip", duration: 11, aria: "A decision pendulum swings between two options and shockwaves mark reversals",
      captions: [{ t: 0.02, text: "A topic holds a chain of choices…" },
                 { t: 0.3, text: "each swing to a different option is a reversal…" },
                 { t: 0.66, text: "and A → B → A is the loudest signal of all: a boomerang." }],
      draw(ctx, k, w, h, { kf, E, C }) {
        const y = h * 0.62, x0 = w * 0.2, x1 = w * 0.8;
        ctx.strokeStyle = "rgba(148,163,255,0.25)"; ctx.lineWidth = 2;
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x0 - 30, y); ctx.lineTo(x1 + 30, y); ctx.stroke();
        const pole = (x, name, color, active) => {
          ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(x, y, 16, 0, 7);
          ctx.fillStyle = active ? color : "rgba(148,163,255,0.12)"; ctx.fill();
          ctx.strokeStyle = color; ctx.lineWidth = 2; ctx.stroke();
          if (active) { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(x, y, 22, 0, 7); ctx.strokeStyle = color; ctx.globalAlpha = 0.4; ctx.stroke(); ctx.globalAlpha = 1; }
          ctx.font = "700 11px Manrope"; ctx.textAlign = "center"; ctx.fillStyle = "#e9edff";
          ctx.fillText(name, x, y - 32);
        };
        // swing script: A → B → A → B
        const swings = [[0.14, 0], [0.36, 1], [0.58, 0], [0.8, 1]];
        let cur = 0;
        for (const [t, v] of swings) if (k >= t) cur = v;
        const ballX = x0 + (x1 - x0) * kf(k, [[0.02, 0], [0.14, 0], [0.3, 1], [0.5, 1], [0.66, 0], [0.82, 0], [0.94, 1]], "inout");
        // pendulum arc + line from anchor
        const anchorY = h * 0.12;
        ctx.strokeStyle = "rgba(148,163,255,0.3)";
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(w / 2, anchorY); ctx.lineTo(ballX, y - 26); ctx.stroke();
        // shockwaves on arrival
        const arrivals = [[0.3, 1], [0.66, 0], [0.94, 1]];
        arrivals.forEach(([t, side]) => {
          if (k < t || k > t + 0.14) return;
          const age = (k - t) / 0.14;
          ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(side ? x1 : x0, y, 18 + age * 46, 0, 7);
          ctx.strokeStyle = C.danger; ctx.globalAlpha = 1 - age; ctx.lineWidth = 2.5;
          ctx.stroke(); ctx.globalAlpha = 1; ctx.lineWidth = 1;
        });
        pole(x0, "React", C.ac2, cur === 0);
        pole(x1, "Vue", C.ac3, cur === 1);
        // the ball
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(ballX, y - 26, 9, 0, 7);
        const g = ctx.createRadialGradient(ballX, y - 26, 1, ballX, y - 26, 14);
        g.addColorStop(0, "#fff"); g.addColorStop(1, cur ? C.ac3 : C.ac2);
        ctx.fillStyle = g; ctx.shadowColor = cur ? C.ac3 : C.ac2; ctx.shadowBlur = 18; ctx.fill(); ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
        // flip counter
        const flips = arrivals.filter(([t]) => k > t).length;
        ctx.font = "800 12px Manrope"; ctx.textAlign = "left"; ctx.fillStyle = C.warn;
        if (flips) ctx.fillText(`${flips} reversal${flips > 1 ? "s" : ""} detected`, x0 - 28, h * 0.16);
        if (k > 0.7) {
          ctx.fillStyle = C.ac3; ctx.font = "800 12px Manrope";
          ctx.fillText("BOOMERANG: React → Vue → React", x0 - 28, h * 0.16 + 22);
        }
      }
    },
    clip4: {
      title: "The score", duration: 12, aria: "A gauge sweeps up while scoring factors stack into the final number",
      captions: [{ t: 0.02, text: "Every topic gets a Flip-Flop Score from 0 to 100…" },
                 { t: 0.38, text: "flip count, boomerangs, speed, oscillation and entropy stack up…" },
                 { t: 0.78, text: "Stable → Wobbly → Restless → Chaotic → Full Pendulum." }],
      draw(ctx, k, w, h, { kf, E, R, C }) {
        const cx = w * 0.27, cy = h * 0.58, r = Math.min(w, h) * 0.27;
        const a0 = Math.PI * 0.75, a1 = Math.PI * 2.25;
        const val = kf(k, [[0.06, 0], [0.5, 87]], "out");
        ctx.lineCap = "round";
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(cx, cy, r, a0, a1);
        ctx.strokeStyle = "rgba(148,163,255,0.14)"; ctx.lineWidth = 10; ctx.stroke();
        const frac = val / 100;
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(cx, cy, r, a0, a0 + (a1 - a0) * frac);
        ctx.strokeStyle = C.ac3; ctx.lineWidth = 10;
        ctx.shadowColor = C.ac3; ctx.shadowBlur = 16; ctx.stroke(); ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
        ctx.fillStyle = "#e9edff"; ctx.font = `700 ${r * 0.5}px Space Grotesk`; ctx.textAlign = "center";
        ctx.fillText(Math.round(val), cx, cy + r * 0.14);
        ctx.font = "600 10px Manrope"; ctx.fillStyle = C.muted;
        ctx.fillText("FLIP-FLOP SCORE", cx, cy + r * 0.42);

        const factors = [
          ["flip count", 0.8, 0.3], ["boomerangs", 0.75, 0.44], ["flip speed", 0.9, 0.58], ["oscillation", 0.5, 0.72], ["entropy", 0.65, 0.86]
        ];
        const bx = w * 0.52, bw = w * 0.36;
        factors.forEach(([label, v, t0], i) => {
          const kk = kf(k, [[t0, 0], [t0 + 0.12, v]], "out");
          const y = h * 0.2 + i * (h * 0.15);
          ctx.fillStyle = C.muted; ctx.font = "600 10.5px Manrope"; ctx.textAlign = "left";
          if (k > t0 - 0.04) ctx.fillText(label, bx, y - 6);
          R(ctx, bx, y, bw, 9, 5); ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(148,163,255,0.12)"; ctx.fill();
          R(ctx, bx, y, Math.max(9, bw * kk), 9, 5);
          const g = ctx.createLinearGradient(bx, 0, bx + bw, 0);
          g.addColorStop(0, C.ac1); g.addColorStop(1, C.ac3);
          ctx.fillStyle = g; ctx.fill();
        });
        if (k > 0.86) {
          const pop = kf(k, [[0.86, 0], [0.92, 1]], "back");
          ctx.save(); ctx.translate(w * 0.7, h * 0.92); ctx.scale(E.back(pop), E.back(pop));
          ctx.font = "800 13px Manrope"; ctx.textAlign = "center";
          R(ctx, -64, -14, 128, 28, 14);
          ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(255,61,154,0.2)"; ctx.fill();
          ctx.strokeStyle = C.ac3; ctx.stroke();
          ctx.fillStyle = "#ffd9ec"; ctx.fillText("CHAOTIC", 0, 4);
          ctx.restore();
        }
      }
    }
  };

  return { mount, scenes };
})();


/* ---------------------------------------------------------------
   router — hash routes, page lifecycle, moods, wipe transitions
--------------------------------------------------------------- */
const router = (() => {
  const PAGES = [
    { path: "/", key: "hero", title: "Home", icon: "spark", mood: "calm", nav: false },
    { path: "/dashboard", key: "dashboard", title: "Dashboard", icon: "dashboard", mood: "calm", nav: true },
    { path: "/timeline", key: "timeline", title: "Timeline", icon: "timeline", mood: "focus", nav: true },
    { path: "/topics", key: "topics", title: "Topics", icon: "topics", mood: "focus", nav: true },
    { path: "/map", key: "map", title: "Pattern map", icon: "map", mood: "swirl", nav: true },
    { path: "/heatmap", key: "heatmap", title: "Heatmap", icon: "heatmap", mood: "matrix", nav: true },
    { path: "/people", key: "people", title: "People", icon: "people", mood: "calm", nav: true },
    { path: "/import", key: "import", title: "Import", icon: "import", mood: "focus", nav: true },
    { path: "/alerts", key: "alerts", title: "Alerts", icon: "alert", mood: "ember", nav: true },
    { path: "/how", key: "how", title: "How it works", icon: "book", mood: "calm", nav: true },
    { path: "/settings", key: "settings", title: "Settings", icon: "settings", mood: "focus", nav: true }
  ];
  let current = null;
  let disposers = [];
  let rerenderSoon = null;

  const pageList = () => PAGES.filter(p => p.nav);

  function parseHash() {
    const raw = (location.hash || "#/").slice(1);
    const [path, qs] = raw.split("?");
    const params = {};
    if (qs) qs.split("&").forEach(kv => {
      const [k, v] = kv.split("=");
      if (k) params[decodeURIComponent(k)] = decodeURIComponent(v || "");
    });
    return { path: path || "/", params };
  }

  function go(path, params) {
    const qs = params && Object.keys(params).length
      ? "?" + Object.entries(params).map(([k, v]) => encodeURIComponent(k) + "=" + encodeURIComponent(v)).join("&")
      : "";
    if (location.hash === "#" + path + qs) handle(); else location.hash = path + qs;
  }

  function runDisposers() {
    disposers.forEach(fn => { try { fn(); } catch (e) { console.warn(e); } });
    disposers = [];
  }
  function pushDisposer(fn) { disposers.push(fn); }

  // page-scoped bus subscription: dies with the page
  function sub(ev, fn) {
    bus.on(ev, fn);
    pushDisposer(() => bus.off(ev, fn));
  }

  function setActiveNav(page) {
    U.$$(".nav-link").forEach(b => {
      b.classList.toggle("active", b.dataset.path === page.path);
      b.setAttribute("aria-current", b.dataset.path === page.path ? "page" : "false");
    });
    U.$$("#mobile-menu a").forEach(a => a.classList.toggle("active", a.dataset.path === page.path));
  }

  function playWipe(midway) {
    const wipe = U.$("#wipe");
    wipe.classList.remove("play");
    void wipe.offsetWidth;
    wipe.classList.add("play");
    setTimeout(midway, 240);
    setTimeout(() => wipe.classList.remove("play"), 700);
  }

  function renderPage(page, params) {
    runDisposers();
    const el = U.$("#page-" + page.key);
    el.classList.remove("page-enter");
    try {
      const out = pages[page.key] && pages[page.key](el, params || {});
      if (typeof out === "function") pushDisposer(out);
    } catch (e) {
      console.error(e);
      el.innerHTML = "";
      el.appendChild(ui.empty("warn", "This page hit a snag", e.message || String(e)));
      ui.toastError(e.message || String(e), "Render error");
    }
    void el.offsetWidth;
    el.classList.add("page-enter");
    ui.enhance(el);
  }

  function handle(first = false) {
    const { path, params } = parseHash();
    const page = PAGES.find(p => p.path === path) || PAGES[0];
    const samePage = current === page;
    if (samePage && !first) { renderPage(page, params); return; }

    const swap = () => {
      U.$$(".page").forEach(s => s.classList.remove("active"));
      U.$("#page-" + page.key).classList.add("active");
      window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: "instant" in window ? "instant" : "auto" });
      bg.setMood(page.mood);
      setActiveNav(page);
      current = page;
      renderPage(page, params);
    };
    if (first || REDUCED || store.state.settings.calm) swap();
    else playWipe(swap);
    U.$("#mobile-menu").classList.remove("open");
  }

  function init() {
    window.addEventListener("hashchange", () => handle());
    handle(true);
    // live data updates re-render whatever page you're on
    rerenderSoon = U.debounce(() => {
      if (current) renderPage(current, parseHash().params);
    }, 350);
    bus.on("data:changed", () => rerenderSoon());
  }

  return { init, go, pageList, parseHash, pushDisposer, sub,
    get current() { return current; } };
})();

/* ---------------------------------------------------------------
   feed — simulated live wire. real messages through the real
   pipeline, so alerts and charts react exactly as they would
--------------------------------------------------------------- */
const feed = (() => {
  const SCRIPT = [
    { person: "Sam Rivera", text: "Okay, final answer: we're going with SolidJS for the marketing site." },
    { person: "Marcus Chen", text: "Starting paleo today — it's settled." },
    { person: "Sam Rivera", text: "Actually, switching the base plan to $49." },
    { person: "Priya Nair", text: "Back to teal for the logo." },
    { person: "Sam Rivera", text: "Let's move standup to Friday 4pm." },
    { person: "Marcus Chen", text: "Keto again. I take it back." },
    { person: "Priya Nair", text: "On second thought, let's do magenta for the logo." },
    { person: "Sam Rivera", text: "Revert — $29 it is." },
    { person: "Marcus Chen", text: "Switching to vegan, the doctor insists." },
    { person: "Sam Rivera", text: "Scratch that — back to React for the marketing site." },
    { person: "Priya Nair", text: "Final decision: decline. I'm staying put." },
    { person: "Sam Rivera", text: "Never mind, Friday was a mistake. Tuesday 10am it is." }
  ];
  let timer = null, i = 0;

  async function step() {
    const m = SCRIPT[i];
    i++;
    try {
      const res = await api.post("/ingest", { kind: "chat", raw: `${m.person}: ${m.text}`, name: "Live wire" });
      ui.toast({
        title: "New message on the wire",
        msg: `${m.person}: ${m.text}`,
        type: res.decisions ? "ok" : "info", timeout: 5200
      });
    } catch (e) {
      ui.toastError(e.message, "Live feed");
    }
    if (i >= SCRIPT.length) {
      toggle(false);
      ui.toast({ msg: "Live script finished — every message went through the real pipeline", type: "info", timeout: 5000 });
    }
  }

  function toggle(force) {
    const on = force != null ? force : !timer;
    if (on && !timer) {
      timer = setInterval(step, 9000);
      step();
    } else if (!on && timer) {
      clearInterval(timer);
      timer = null;
    }
    const btn = U.$("#feed-toggle");
    if (btn) {
      btn.classList.toggle("btn-primary", !!timer);
      btn.classList.toggle("btn-ghost", !timer);
      btn.setAttribute("aria-pressed", String(!!timer));
    }
    if (force == null) ui.toast({ msg: timer ? "Live feed on — a new message every few seconds" : "Live feed paused", type: "info", timeout: 2400 });
    return !!timer;
  }

  return { toggle, on: () => !!timer };
})();

/* ---------------------------------------------------------------
   pages — one render function per route. each may return a
   cleanup function the router calls before the next page.
--------------------------------------------------------------- */
const pages = {};

// tiny text prompt built on the modal root (ui.modal is action-based)
function askText(title, placeholder, initial = "") {
  return new Promise(resolve => {
    const root = U.$("#modal-root");
    root.innerHTML = ""; root.classList.add("open");
    const back = document.createElement("div");
    back.className = "modal-backdrop";
    const box = document.createElement("div");
    box.className = "modal";
    box.innerHTML = `<h3>${U.esc(title)}</h3>
      <div class="field"><input class="input" id="ask-input" placeholder="${U.esc(placeholder)}" value="${U.esc(initial)}"></div>
      <div class="modal-actions">
        <button class="btn btn-ghost" data-v="">Cancel</button>
        <button class="btn btn-primary" data-v="ok"><span class="sweep"></span>Save</button>
      </div>`;
    root.append(back, box);
    const input = box.querySelector("#ask-input");
    setTimeout(() => { input.focus(); input.select(); }, 40);
    const close = val => { root.classList.remove("open"); root.innerHTML = ""; document.removeEventListener("keydown", onKey); resolve(val); };
    const onKey = e => { if (e.key === "Escape") close(null); if (e.key === "Enter") close(input.value.trim()); };
    document.addEventListener("keydown", onKey);
    back.addEventListener("click", () => close(null));
    box.querySelectorAll("[data-v]").forEach(b => b.addEventListener("click", () => close(b.dataset.v === "ok" ? input.value.trim() : null)));
  });
}

/* ============================ HERO ============================ */
pages.hero = function (el) {
  const title = U.$("#hero-title");
  if (!title.dataset.built) {
    title.dataset.built = "1";
    "FlipTrace".split("").forEach((ch, i) => {
      const s = document.createElement("span");
      s.textContent = ch;
      s.style.setProperty("--dx", (Math.random() * 260 - 130).toFixed(0) + "px");
      s.style.setProperty("--dy", (Math.random() * 200 - 40).toFixed(0) + "px");
      s.style.setProperty("--rr", (Math.random() * 120 - 60).toFixed(0) + "deg");
      s.style.animationDelay = (0.15 + i * 0.07) + "s";
      title.appendChild(s);
    });
    // letters land in sequence, then the whole word picks up the gradient
    setTimeout(() => title.querySelectorAll("span").forEach(s => s.classList.add("in")), 80);
    setTimeout(() => { title.style.filter = "drop-shadow(0 0 26px rgba(124,92,255,.35))"; }, 1400);
  } else {
    title.querySelectorAll("span").forEach(s => s.classList.add("in"));
  }

  // typewriter subtitle
  const PHRASES = [
    "catching decisions that refuse to stay decided.",
    "chats, emails and task boards — distilled into one pulse.",
    "every flip, every boomerang, scored and timestamped.",
    "ingest → extract → group → detect → score → alert.",
    "runs entirely in this file. no server, no keys."
  ];
  const typerEl = U.$("#typer");
  let pi = 0, ci = 0, deleting = false, typerTimer = null;
  const tickType = () => {
    const phrase = PHRASES[pi % PHRASES.length];
    if (!deleting) {
      ci++;
      typerEl.textContent = phrase.slice(0, ci);
      if (ci >= phrase.length) { deleting = true; typerTimer = setTimeout(tickType, 2100); return; }
      typerTimer = setTimeout(tickType, 34 + Math.random() * 40);
    } else {
      ci--;
      typerEl.textContent = phrase.slice(0, ci);
      if (ci <= 0) { deleting = false; pi++; typerTimer = setTimeout(tickType, 420); return; }
      typerTimer = setTimeout(tickType, 14);
    }
  };
  tickType();

  // YES ↔ NO pendulum
  const pend = U.$("#hero-pend");
  let raf = null, t0 = performance.now();
  const drawPend = now => {
    const { ctx, w, h } = charts.fitCanvas(pend);
    const t = (now - t0) / 1000;
    ctx.clearRect(0, 0, w, h);
    const cx = w / 2, cy = 26, len = h - 92;
    const angle = Math.sin(t * 2.1) * 0.72;
    const bx = cx + Math.sin(angle) * len, by = cy + Math.cos(angle) * len;

    // arc path + side words
    ctx.strokeStyle = "rgba(148,163,255,0.16)"; ctx.lineWidth = 1.5;
    ctx.setLineDash([3, 7]);
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(cx, cy, len, Math.PI * 0.32, Math.PI * 0.68); ctx.stroke();
    ctx.setLineDash([]);
    const C = charts.colors();
    const word = (x, txt, col, hot) => {
      ctx.font = "800 22px Space Grotesk, sans-serif"; ctx.textAlign = "center";
      ctx.fillStyle = col; ctx.globalAlpha = hot ? 1 : 0.4;
      ctx.shadowColor = col; ctx.shadowBlur = hot ? 26 : 0;
      ctx.fillText(txt, x, h - 34);
      ctx.shadowBlur = 0; ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
    };
    const leftHot = Math.sin(angle) < -0.35, rightHot = Math.sin(angle) > 0.35;
    word(w * 0.16, "YES", C.ok, leftHot);
    word(w * 0.84, "NO", C.ac3, rightHot);

    // trail
    for (let k = 1; k <= 5; k++) {
      const a2 = Math.sin((t - k * 0.055) * 2.1) * 0.72;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(cx + Math.sin(a2) * len, cy + Math.cos(a2) * len, 10 - k * 1.4, 0, 7);
      ctx.fillStyle = C.ac2; ctx.globalAlpha = 0.14 - k * 0.02;
      ctx.fill(); ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
    }
    // rod + pivot + bob
    ctx.strokeStyle = "rgba(233,237,255,0.5)"; ctx.lineWidth = 2;
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(cx, cy); ctx.lineTo(bx, by); ctx.stroke();
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(cx, cy, 5, 0, 7); ctx.fillStyle = C.text; ctx.fill();
    const g = ctx.createRadialGradient(bx - 3, by - 3, 1, bx, by, 15);
    g.addColorStop(0, "#ffffff"); g.addColorStop(1, leftHot ? C.ok : rightHot ? C.ac3 : C.ac1);
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(bx, by, 11, 0, 7);
    ctx.fillStyle = g; ctx.shadowColor = C.ac1; ctx.shadowBlur = 22; ctx.fill(); ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
    raf = requestAnimationFrame(drawPend);
  };
  if (!REDUCED && !store.state.settings.calm) raf = requestAnimationFrame(drawPend);
  else { const { ctx, w, h } = charts.fitCanvas(pend); ctx.clearRect(0, 0, w, h); }

  // live meta counters (or a teaser when empty)
  const meta = U.$("#hero-meta");
  const s = store.stats();
  if (s.decisions) {
    meta.innerHTML = `
      <div class="hm"><b data-count="${s.decisions}">0</b><span>decisions traced</span></div>
      <div class="hm"><b data-count="${s.topics}">0</b><span>topics grouped</span></div>
      <div class="hm"><b data-count="${s.reversals}">0</b><span>reversals caught</span></div>
      <div class="hm"><b data-count="${s.boomerangs}">0</b><span>boomerangs</span></div>
      <div class="hm"><b data-count="${s.people}">0</b><span>people profiled</span></div>`;
    meta.querySelectorAll("b").forEach(b => ui.countUp(b, +b.dataset.count, { dur: 1400 }));
  } else {
    meta.innerHTML = `
      <div class="hm"><b>3</b><span>months of demo data</span></div>
      <div class="hm"><b>3</b><span>people</span></div>
      <div class="hm"><b>4</b><span>source types</span></div>
      <div class="hm"><b>0</b><span>servers required</span></div>`;
  }

  U.$("#hero-launch").onclick = () => router.go("/dashboard");
  U.$("#hero-demo").onclick = () => pages.runDemo();

  return () => {
    if (raf) cancelAnimationFrame(raf);
    clearTimeout(typerTimer);
  };
};

/* ========================== DASHBOARD ========================== */
pages.dashboard = function (el) {
  const s = store.stats();
  if (!s.decisions) {
    el.innerHTML = `<div class="sec-head"><h2><span class="grad-text">Mission dashboard</span></h2></div>`;
    el.appendChild(ui.empty("spark", "Nothing to trace yet",
      "Import your chats, emails or task board — or load three months of demo data and watch the whole pipeline light up.",
      "Load demo data", () => pages.runDemo()));
    const alt = document.createElement("button");
    alt.className = "btn btn-ghost";
    alt.style.marginTop = "8px";
    alt.innerHTML = `<span class="sweep"></span>${icon("import", 15)} Open the import center`;
    alt.onclick = () => router.go("/import");
    el.querySelector(".empty").appendChild(alt);
    return;
  }

  const topTopics = [...store.state.topics].filter(t => t.decisionCount > 0).sort((a, b) => b.score - a.score).slice(0, 5);
  const people = [...store.state.people].slice(0, 6);
  const recentAlerts = [...store.state.alerts].sort((a, b) => b.ts - a.ts).slice(0, 3);

  el.innerHTML = `
    ${ui.sectionHead(`<span class="grad-text">Mission dashboard</span>`,
      `<span class="mono small">${s.messages} messages · ${s.sources} sources · ${U.esc(dbx.mode)} storage</span>`)}
    <div class="stat-grid">
      <div class="card stat tilt" data-reveal><div class="s-ico">${icon("layers", 17)}</div>
        <div class="s-label">Decisions traced</div><div class="s-value" id="st-dec">0</div>
        <div class="s-sub">${s.messages} messages scanned</div></div>
      <div class="card stat tilt" data-reveal><div class="s-ico">${icon("topics", 17)}</div>
        <div class="s-label">Live topics</div><div class="s-value" id="st-top">0</div>
        <div class="s-sub">grouped by similarity</div></div>
      <div class="card stat tilt" data-reveal><div class="s-ico" style="color:var(--warn)">${icon("refresh", 17)}</div>
        <div class="s-label">Reversals</div><div class="s-value" id="st-rev">0</div>
        <div class="s-sub">${s.boomerangs} boomerangs among them</div></div>
      <div class="card stat tilt" data-reveal><div class="s-ico" style="color:var(--ac3)">${icon("gauge", 17)}</div>
        <div class="s-label">Avg flip-flop score</div><div class="s-value" id="st-avg">0</div>
        <div class="s-sub">${Engine.levelOf(s.avgScore).label} across all topics</div></div>
    </div>
    <div class="dash-grid">
      <div class="dash-col">
        <div class="card pulse-wrap" data-reveal>
          <div class="pulse-head">
            <h3>Instability pulse</h3>
            <span class="pulse-live"><i></i>monitoring ${store.state.reversals.length} events</span>
          </div>
          <canvas id="pulse-canvas" style="width:100%;height:150px" aria-label="Instability pulse chart"></canvas>
        </div>
        <div class="card" data-reveal>
          <h3 style="font-size:1.02rem">Top flip-flopped topics</h3>
          <div id="dash-topics"></div>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="dash-col">
        <div class="card" data-reveal>
          <h3 style="font-size:1.02rem">Person leaderboard <span class="faint small">— most unstable first</span></h3>
          <div id="dash-people"></div>
        </div>
        <div class="card" data-reveal>
          <h3 style="font-size:1.02rem">Latest alerts</h3>
          <div id="dash-alerts"></div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>`;

  ui.countUp(el.querySelector("#st-dec"), s.decisions);
  ui.countUp(el.querySelector("#st-top"), s.topics);
  ui.countUp(el.querySelector("#st-rev"), s.reversals);
