  ui.countUp(el.querySelector("#st-avg"), s.avgScore);

  const pulse = charts.pulse(el.querySelector("#pulse-canvas"));
  pulse.setInstability(s.avgScore / 100);
  pulse.start();

  el.querySelector("#dash-topics").innerHTML = topTopics.map(t => `
    <div class="topic-mini" data-topic="${t.id}" role="button" tabindex="0" data-tip="Open ${U.esc(t.name)}">
      <div class="tm-score lvl-txt lvl-${t.levelIdx}">${t.score}</div>
      <div class="tm-body">
        <div class="tm-name">${U.esc(t.name)}</div>
        <div class="tm-sub">${t.decisionCount} decisions · ${t.flips} flips · ${t.boomerangs} boomerangs</div>
        <div class="tm-bar"><i data-w="${t.score}"></i></div>
      </div>
      ${ui.levelChip(t)}
    </div>`).join("") || `<p class="faint small">No topics yet.</p>`;
  requestAnimationFrame(() => el.querySelectorAll(".tm-bar i").forEach(b => b.style.width = b.dataset.w + "%"));
  el.querySelectorAll(".topic-mini").forEach(n => {
    n.addEventListener("click", () => router.go("/topics", { topic: n.dataset.topic }));
    n.addEventListener("keydown", e => { if (e.key === "Enter") router.go("/topics", { topic: n.dataset.topic }); });
  });

  el.querySelector("#dash-people").innerHTML = people.map((p, i) => `
    <div class="leader-row" data-person="${U.esc(p.person)}" role="button" tabindex="0">
      <span class="leader-rank">${i + 1}</span>
      ${ui.avatar(p.person)}
      <div class="leader-body">
        <div style="display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:center">
          <strong>${U.esc(p.person)}</strong>${ui.levelChip(p)}
        </div>
        <div class="small muted">${p.decisions} decisions · ${p.flips} flips · ${p.boomerangs} boomerangs</div>
        <div class="leader-bar"><i data-w="${p.score}"></i></div>
      </div>
      <div class="tm-score" style="font-size:1.3rem">${p.score}</div>
    </div>`).join("");
  requestAnimationFrame(() => el.querySelectorAll(".leader-bar i").forEach(b => b.style.width = b.dataset.w + "%"));
  el.querySelectorAll(".leader-row").forEach(n => {
    n.addEventListener("click", () => router.go("/people", { person: n.dataset.person }));
    n.addEventListener("keydown", e => { if (e.key === "Enter") router.go("/people", { person: n.dataset.person }); });
  });

  el.querySelector("#dash-alerts").innerHTML = recentAlerts.map(a => {
    const topic = store.topicById(a.topicId);
    return `<div class="mini-alert" data-alert="${a.id}" role="button" tabindex="0">
      <span style="color:var(--${a.severity === "critical" ? "danger" : "warn"});flex:none;margin-top:2px">${icon("zap", 15)}</span>
      <div><strong class="small">${U.esc(a.title)}</strong>
        <div class="small muted">${U.esc(a.body)}</div></div>
    </div>`;
  }).join("") || `<p class="faint small">Quiet for now — alerts fire when a topic hits its second reversal.</p>`;
  el.querySelectorAll(".mini-alert").forEach(n => n.addEventListener("click", () => router.go("/alerts")));

  return () => pulse.stop();
};

/* ========================== TIMELINE ========================== */
pages.timeline = function (el, params) {
  const topics = store.state.topics.filter(t => t.decisionCount > 0)
    .sort((a, b) => b.flips - a.flips || b.score - a.score);
  if (!topics.length) {
    el.innerHTML = `<div class="sec-head"><h2>Timeline</h2></div>`;
    el.appendChild(ui.empty("timeline", "No decisions to lay out yet",
      "The timeline draws every decision as a glowing node and every reversal as a swinging arc.",
      "Load demo data", () => pages.runDemo()));
    return;
  }
  let topicId = params.topic && topics.some(t => t.id === params.topic) ? params.topic : topics[0].id;

  el.innerHTML = `
    ${ui.sectionHead("Reversal timeline", "drag to pan · scroll to zoom · hover a node for the source sentence",
      `<div class="tl-tools-inner" style="display:flex;gap:8px">
        <button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" id="tl-out">${icon("-", 14)}</button>
        <button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" id="tl-fit" data-tip="Fit to view">${icon("target", 14)}</button>
        <button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" id="tl-in">${icon("+", 14)}</button>
      </div>`)}
    <div class="tl-chips" id="tl-chips"></div>
    <div class="tl-wrap">
      <canvas id="tl-canvas" class="tl-canvas" aria-label="Decision timeline"></canvas>
      <div class="tl-legend">
        <span><i style="background:var(--ok)"></i>commit</span>
        <span><i style="background:var(--ac1)"></i>switch</span>
        <span><i style="background:var(--warn)"></i>revert</span>
        <span><i style="background:var(--danger)"></i>reject</span>
        <span><i style="background:var(--ac3);border-radius:0;height:3px;width:14px"></i>boomerang arc</span>
      </div>
      <div class="tl-tip" id="tl-tip"></div>
    </div>`;

  const canvas = el.querySelector("#tl-canvas");
  const tip = el.querySelector("#tl-tip");
  const wrap = el.querySelector(".tl-wrap");
  const chipsHost = el.querySelector("#tl-chips");
  const POL_COL = { commit: "var(--ok)", switch: "var(--ac1)", revert: "var(--warn)", reject: "var(--danger)" };
  const POL_HEX = { commit: "#3ddc97", switch: "#a78bff", revert: "#ffb020", reject: "#ff4d6b" };

  let zoom = 1, pan = 0, raf = null, hoverNode = null;
  let decisions = [], revs = [], lanes = [], t0 = 0, t1 = 1;
  const ac = new AbortController();
  const SIG = { signal: ac.signal };

  function drawChips() {
    chipsHost.innerHTML = topics.map(t => `
      <button class="chip btn-chip ${t.id === topicId ? "on" : ""}" data-t="${t.id}">
        ${U.esc(t.name)} <span class="faint mono">${t.flips}↺</span></button>`).join("");
    chipsHost.querySelectorAll("[data-t]").forEach(c => c.addEventListener("click", () => {
      topicId = c.dataset.t;
      zoom = 1; pan = 0;
      drawChips(); loadData();
    }, SIG));
  }

  function loadData() {
    decisions = store.decisionsOf(topicId);
    revs = store.reversalsOf(topicId);
    const optOrder = [];
    decisions.forEach(d => { if (d.option && !optOrder.includes(d.option)) optOrder.push(d.option); });
    lanes = optOrder;
    t0 = decisions.length ? decisions[0].ts - 86400e3 : Date.now() - 86400e3;
    t1 = decisions.length ? decisions[decisions.length - 1].ts + 86400e3 : Date.now() + 86400e3;
    if (t1 - t0 < 7 * 86400e3) t1 = t0 + 7 * 86400e3;
    draw();
  }

  const laneY = i => 44 + (i + 0.5) * ((canvas.clientHeight - 92) / Math.max(lanes.length, 3));
  function xOf(ts) {
    const innerW = canvas.clientWidth - 70;
    return 34 + ((ts - t0) / (t1 - t0)) * innerW * zoom - pan;
  }
  const nodePos = d => {
    const li = d.option ? lanes.indexOf(d.option) : lanes.length;
    return { x: xOf(d.ts), y: laneY(li < 0 ? lanes.length : li) };
  };

  function draw(time = performance.now()) {
    const { ctx, w, h } = charts.fitCanvas(canvas);
    const C = charts.colors();
    ctx.clearRect(0, 0, w, h);

    // lanes + option labels
    ctx.font = "600 10px JetBrains Mono, monospace";
    for (let i = 0; i <= lanes.length; i++) {
      const y = laneY(i);
      ctx.strokeStyle = "rgba(148,163,255,0.10)";
      ctx.setLineDash([2, 6]);
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(30, y); ctx.lineTo(w - 12, y); ctx.stroke();
      ctx.setLineDash([]);
      const label = i < lanes.length ? lanes[i] : "no option";
      ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(154,165,204,0.75)"; ctx.textAlign = "right";
      ctx.fillText(label.length > 14 ? label.slice(0, 13) + "…" : label, 96, y - Math.min(lanes.length, 6) * 0 + 3);
    }

    // time axis ticks
    const innerW = canvas.clientWidth - 70;
    const spanDays = (t1 - t0) / 86400e3;
    const pxPerDay = (innerW * zoom) / spanDays;
    const step = [1, 2, 3, 7, 14, 30, 60, 90, 180].find(s => s * pxPerDay > 78) || 180;
    const start = new Date(t0); start.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
    for (let ts = start.getTime(); ts <= t1 + step * 86400e3; ts += step * 86400e3) {
      const x = xOf(ts);
      if (x < 30 || x > w - 12) continue;
      ctx.strokeStyle = "rgba(148,163,255,0.16)";
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x, 30); ctx.lineTo(x, h - 34); ctx.stroke();
      ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(154,165,204,0.7)"; ctx.textAlign = "center"; ctx.font = "600 9.5px JetBrains Mono, monospace";
      const d = new Date(ts);
      ctx.fillText(`${d.getDate()}/${d.getMonth() + 1}` + (step >= 30 ? "/" + String(d.getFullYear()).slice(2) : ""), x, h - 20);
    }

    const byId = new Map(decisions.map(d => [d.id, d]));

    // reversal arcs
    revs.forEach((r, i) => {
      const a = byId.get(r.fromId), b = byId.get(r.toId);
      if (!a || !b) return;
      const pa = nodePos(a), pb = nodePos(b);
      if (Math.max(pa.x, pb.x) < -80 || Math.min(pa.x, pb.x) > w + 80) return;
      const boom = r.type === "boomerang";
      const lift = Math.min(110, Math.abs(pb.x - pa.x) * 0.42 + 36);
      const cy = Math.min(pa.y, pb.y) - lift + Math.sin(time / 600 + i) * 3; // pendulum snap
      const mx = (pa.x + pb.x) / 2;
      const col = boom ? C.ac3 : r.type === "reject" ? C.danger : C.warn;
      ctx.strokeStyle = col;
      ctx.lineWidth = boom ? 2.4 : 1.6;
      ctx.globalAlpha = 0.9;
      ctx.setLineDash(boom ? [] : [7, 5]);
      ctx.lineDashOffset = -time / 40;
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(pa.x, pa.y - 8);
      ctx.quadraticCurveTo(mx, cy, pb.x, pb.y - 8);
      ctx.shadowColor = col; ctx.shadowBlur = boom ? 14 : 6;
      ctx.stroke(); ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
      ctx.setLineDash([]); ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
      // traveling spark
      const tt = ((time / 1400) + i * 0.27) % 1;
      const qx = (1 - tt) * (1 - tt) * pa.x + 2 * (1 - tt) * tt * mx + tt * tt * pb.x;
      const qy = (1 - tt) * (1 - tt) * (pa.y - 8) + 2 * (1 - tt) * tt * cy + tt * tt * (pb.y - 8);
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(qx, qy, 2.6, 0, 7);
      ctx.fillStyle = col; ctx.fill();
    });

    // nodes
    decisions.forEach((d, i) => {
      const p = nodePos(d);
      if (p.x < -40 || p.x > w + 40) return;
      const col = POL_HEX[d.polarity] || "#a78bff";
      const isRevTarget = revs.some(r => r.toId === d.id);
      const pulse = isRevTarget ? 1 + Math.sin(time / 300 + i) * 0.18 : 1;
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(p.x, p.y, (hoverNode === d.id ? 9 : 6.5) * pulse, 0, 7);
      ctx.fillStyle = col; ctx.shadowColor = col; ctx.shadowBlur = hoverNode === d.id ? 22 : 12;
      ctx.fill(); ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
      if (hoverNode === d.id) {
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(p.x, p.y, 13, 0, 7);
        ctx.strokeStyle = col; ctx.globalAlpha = 0.5; ctx.stroke(); ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
      }
    });

    raf = requestAnimationFrame(draw);
  }

  function findNode(mx, my) {
    let best = null, bd = 18;
    decisions.forEach(d => {
      const p = nodePos(d);
      const dist = Math.hypot(p.x - mx, p.y - my);
      if (dist < bd) { bd = dist; best = d; }
    });
    return best;
  }

  canvas.addEventListener("pointermove", e => {
    const r = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
    const mx = e.clientX - r.left, my = e.clientY - r.top;
    if (drag.active) {
      pan = U.clamp(pan - (e.clientX - drag.x), 0, (canvas.clientWidth - 70) * (zoom - 1));
      drag.x = e.clientX;
      return;
    }
    const n = findNode(mx, my);
    hoverNode = n ? n.id : null;
    canvas.style.cursor = n ? "pointer" : "grab";
    if (n) {
      const rev = revs.find(r => r.toId === n.id);
      tip.innerHTML = `
        <div class="small" style="margin-bottom:6px"><strong>${U.esc(n.person)}</strong>
          <span class="faint">· ${U.fmtDateTime(n.ts)}</span></div>
        <div>${highlightMatches(n.text, n.matched)}</div>
        <div style="display:flex;gap:6px;margin-top:8px;flex-wrap:wrap">
          <span class="pol pol-${n.polarity}">${n.polarity}</span>
          ${n.option ? `<span class="chip">${U.esc(n.option)}</span>` : ""}
          <span class="chip mono">conf ${n.confidence}</span>
          ${rev ? `<span class="chip sev-${rev.type === "boomerang" ? "critical" : "warn"}">${U.esc(rev.rule)}</span>` : ""}
        </div>`;
      tip.classList.add("show");
      const tw = tip.offsetWidth, th = tip.offsetHeight;
      tip.style.left = U.clamp(mx + 16, 4, canvas.clientWidth - tw - 4) + "px";
      tip.style.top = U.clamp(my - th - 14 < 4 ? my + 18 : my - th - 14, 4, canvas.clientHeight - th - 4) + "px";
    } else tip.classList.remove("show");
  }, SIG);

  canvas.addEventListener("pointerleave", () => { hoverNode = null; tip.classList.remove("show"); }, SIG);

  const drag = { active: false, x: 0 };
  canvas.addEventListener("pointerdown", e => {
    const r = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
    const n = findNode(e.clientX - r.left, e.clientY - r.top);
    if (n) {
      const rev = revs.find(r2 => r2.toId === n.id);
      ui.modal({
        title: `${n.person} · ${U.fmtDateTime(n.ts)}`,
        body: `<p style="font-size:1.02rem">${highlightMatches(n.text, n.matched)}</p>
          <div style="display:flex;gap:7px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-top:10px">
            <span class="pol pol-${n.polarity}">${n.polarity}</span>
            ${n.option ? `<span class="chip">${icon("target", 12)} ${U.esc(n.option)}</span>` : ""}
            <span class="chip mono">confidence ${n.confidence}</span>
            <span class="chip">${icon("message", 12)} ${U.esc(n.sourceName || "unknown source")}</span>
            ${rev ? `<span class="chip sev-critical">${icon("zap", 12)} ${U.esc(rev.rule)}</span>` : ""}
          </div>`,
        actions: [
          { label: "Open topic", value: "topic", cls: "btn-primary", icon: "topics" },
          { label: "Close", value: null }
        ]
      }).then(v => { if (v === "topic") router.go("/topics", { topic: n.topicId }); });
      return;
    }
    drag.active = true; drag.x = e.clientX;
    canvas.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);
    canvas.style.cursor = "grabbing";
  }, SIG);
  canvas.addEventListener("pointerup", () => { drag.active = false; canvas.style.cursor = "grab"; }, SIG);
  canvas.addEventListener("pointercancel", () => { drag.active = false; }, SIG);

  canvas.addEventListener("wheel", e => {
    e.preventDefault();
    zoom = U.clamp(zoom * (1 - e.deltaY * 0.0016), 0.6, 24);
    pan = U.clamp(pan, 0, (canvas.clientWidth - 70) * (zoom - 1));
  }, { ...SIG, passive: false });

  el.querySelector("#tl-in").addEventListener("click", () => { zoom = U.clamp(zoom * 1.5, 0.6, 24); pan = U.clamp(pan, 0, (canvas.clientWidth - 70) * (zoom - 1)); }, SIG);
  el.querySelector("#tl-out").addEventListener("click", () => { zoom = U.clamp(zoom / 1.5, 0.6, 24); pan = U.clamp(pan, 0, (canvas.clientWidth - 70) * (zoom - 1)); }, SIG);
  el.querySelector("#tl-fit").addEventListener("click", () => { zoom = 1; pan = 0; }, SIG);

  drawChips();
  loadData();

  return () => { ac.abort(); if (raf) cancelAnimationFrame(raf); };
};

/* ======================= TOPIC EXPLORER ======================= */
pages.topics = function (el, params) {
  // module-level so a live-feed rerender keeps the context
  pages.topics._sel = params.topic || pages.topics._sel;
  pages.topics._mode = params.topic ? "view" : (pages.topics._mode || "view");
  const sel = () => pages.topics._sel;
  const mode = () => pages.topics._mode;

  const all = store.state.topics.filter(t => t.decisionCount > 0).sort((a, b) => b.score - a.score);
  if (!all.length) {
    el.innerHTML = `<div class="sec-head"><h2>Topic explorer</h2></div>`;
    el.appendChild(ui.empty("topics", "No topics yet",
      "Decisions get grouped into topics by TF-IDF similarity and shared option vocabulary.",
      "Load demo data", () => pages.runDemo()));
    return;
  }
  if (!all.some(t => t.id === sel())) pages.topics._sel = all[0].id;
  pages.topics._splitSel = pages.topics._splitSel instanceof Set ? pages.topics._splitSel : new Set();

  let search = "";
  el.innerHTML = `
    ${ui.sectionHead("Topic explorer", "every decision chain, its state diagram and its score breakdown",
      `<button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" id="tp-undo" data-tip="Undo merge (U)">${icon("undo", 14)}</button>
       <button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" id="tp-redo" data-tip="Redo merge (Y)">${icon("redo", 14)}</button>`)}
    <div class="topics-layout">
      <div>
        <div class="field"><input class="input" id="tp-search" placeholder="Search topics…" value="${U.esc(search)}"></div>
        <div class="topic-list" id="tp-list"></div>
      </div>
      <div id="tp-detail"></div>
    </div>`;

  const listHost = el.querySelector("#tp-list");
  const detailHost = el.querySelector("#tp-detail");

  function drawList() {
    const q = search.trim().toLowerCase();
    const items = all.filter(t => !q || t.name.toLowerCase().includes(q) || (t.optionVocab || []).some(o => o.includes(q)));
    listHost.innerHTML = items.map(t => `
      <div class="topic-item ${t.id === sel() ? "sel" : ""}" data-t="${t.id}" role="button" tabindex="0">
        <div class="ti-score lvl-${t.levelIdx}">${t.score}</div>
        <div style="flex:1;min-width:0">
          <div class="ti-name">${U.esc(t.name)}</div>
          <div class="ti-sub">${t.decisionCount} decisions · ${t.flips} flips</div>
        </div>
        ${ui.levelChip(t)}
      </div>`).join("") || `<p class="faint small" style="padding:10px">Nothing matches.</p>`;
    listHost.querySelectorAll("[data-t]").forEach(n => {
      const act = e => {
        const id = n.dataset.t;
        if (mode() === "merge" && id !== sel()) {
          const target = store.topicById(sel()), other = store.topicById(id);
          ui.confirm({
            title: "Merge topics?",
            msg: `Merge “${other.name}” into “${target.name}”? Their decisions, vocabulary and keywords combine — you can undo with U.`,
            okLabel: "Merge"
          }).then(async ok => {
            if (!ok) return;
            try {
              await api.post("/topics/merge", { targetId: target.id, otherIds: [other.id] });
              pages.topics._mode = "view";
              ui.toast({ msg: `Merged “${other.name}” into “${target.name}”`, type: "ok" });
            } catch (err) { ui.toastError(err.message); }
          });
        } else {
          pages.topics._sel = id;
          pages.topics._mode = "view";
          drawList(); drawDetail();
        }
      };
      n.addEventListener("click", act);
      n.addEventListener("keydown", e => { if (e.key === "Enter") act(e); });
    });
  }

  function drawDetail() {
    const t = store.topicById(sel());
    if (!t) { detailHost.innerHTML = `<div class="card"><p class="faint">Select a topic.</p></div>`; return; }
    const decs = store.decisionsOf(t.id);
    const revs = store.reversalsOf(t.id);
    const inSplit = mode() === "split";
    const inMerge = mode() === "merge";

    // option chain: consecutive options with flip arrows
    let chainHTML = "";
    decs.forEach((d, i) => {
      const rev = revs.find(r => r.toId === d.id);
      if (i > 0) {
        const cls = rev ? (rev.type === "boomerang" ? "boom" : "flip") : "";
        chainHTML += `<span class="chain-arrow ${cls}">${rev ? (rev.type === "boomerang" ? "↺" : "→") + "<small>" + U.fmtGap(rev.gapMs) + "</small>" : "·"}</span>`;
      }
      const label = d.option || (d.polarity === "reject" ? "✕" : "…");
      chainHTML += `<span class="chain-node ${i === 0 ? "first" : ""} ${i === decs.length - 1 ? "now" : ""}"
        data-tip="${U.esc(d.polarity)} · conf ${d.confidence}">${U.esc(label)}</span>`;
    });

    const f = t.factors || {};
    const factors = [
      ["Flip count", f.flipF, t.flips],
      ["Boomerangs", f.boomF, t.boomerangs],
      ["Flip speed", f.speedF, t.minGapMs != null ? U.fmtGap(t.minGapMs) : "—"],
      ["Oscillation", f.oscillation, Math.round((t.oscillation || 0) * 100) + "%"],
      ["Entropy", f.entropyF, Math.round((t.entropy || 0) * 100) + "%"],
      ["Confidence drift", f.driftF, t.confidenceDrift]
    ];

    detailHost.innerHTML = `
      <div class="card beam">
        <div class="td-head">
          <h3>${U.esc(t.name)}</h3>
          ${ui.levelChip(t)}
          <button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" id="tp-rename" data-tip="Rename">${icon("edit", 13)}</button>
          <button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" id="tp-merge" data-tip="Merge another topic into this one">${icon("merge", 13)} ${inMerge ? "pick target…" : "Merge"}</button>
          <button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" id="tp-split" data-tip="Split selected decisions into a new topic">${icon("split", 13)} Split</button>
        </div>
        ${inMerge ? `<div class="chip sev-warn" style="margin-bottom:10px">Merge mode — click another topic in the list to absorb it</div>` : ""}
        <div class="td-stats">
          <div class="td-stat"><b>${t.decisionCount}</b><span>decisions</span></div>
          <div class="td-stat"><b style="color:var(--warn)">${t.flips}</b><span>flips</span></div>
          <div class="td-stat"><b style="color:var(--ac3)">${t.boomerangs}</b><span>boomerangs</span></div>
          <div class="td-stat"><b>${U.fmtGap(t.avgGapMs)}</b><span>avg gap</span></div>
          <div class="td-stat"><b>${U.fmtGap(t.minGapMs)}</b><span>fastest flip</span></div>
          <div class="td-stat"><b>${Math.round((t.entropy || 0) * 100)}%</b><span>entropy</span></div>
        </div>
        <h4 style="margin-top:16px">Decision chain</h4>
        <div class="chain">${chainHTML || '<span class="faint small">—</span>'}</div>
        <h4 style="margin-top:16px">State diagram <span class="faint small">— options as states, particles flow with each transition</span></h4>
        <canvas id="tp-states" style="width:100%;height:230px" aria-label="Option state diagram"></canvas>
        <h4 style="margin-top:16px">Score breakdown <span class="faint small">— weights the Flip-Flop Score</span></h4>
        <div>${factors.map(([n, v, label]) => `
          <div class="factor-row"><span class="f-name">${n}</span>
            <div class="f-bar"><i data-w="${Math.round(U.clamp(v || 0, 0, 1) * 100)}"></i></div>
            <span class="f-val">${U.esc(String(label))}</span></div>`).join("")}
        </div>
        <h4 style="margin-top:18px">Decisions <span class="faint small">— highlighted = the phrase that triggered extraction</span>
          ${inSplit ? `<span class="chip on" style="float:right">${pages.topics._splitSel.size} selected</span>` : ""}</h4>
        ${inSplit ? `<div style="display:flex;gap:8px;margin-bottom:10px">
            <button class="btn btn-primary btn-sm" id="tp-split-go" ${pages.topics._splitSel.size ? "" : "disabled"}>
              <span class="sweep"></span>${icon("split", 13)} Create split topic</button>
            <button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" id="tp-split-cancel">Cancel</button></div>` : ""}
        <div id="tp-decs"></div>
      </div>`;

    requestAnimationFrame(() => detailHost.querySelectorAll(".f-bar i").forEach(b => b.style.width = b.dataset.w + "%"));

    detailHost.querySelector("#tp-decs").innerHTML = decs.map(d => {
      const rev = revs.find(r => r.toId === d.id);
      return `<div class="decision-row">
        ${inSplit ? `<span class="check"><input type="checkbox" data-split="${d.id}" ${pages.topics._splitSel.has(d.id) ? "checked" : ""}>
          <span class="box">${icon("check", 11)}</span></span>` : ""}
        <div class="d-when">${U.fmtDateTime(d.ts)}</div>
        <div class="d-main">
          <div class="d-text">${highlightMatches(d.text, d.matched)}</div>
          <div class="d-meta">
            <span class="pol pol-${d.polarity}">${d.polarity}</span>
            ${d.option ? `<span class="chip">${icon("target", 11)} ${U.esc(d.option)}</span>` : ""}
            <span class="chip" style="padding:2px 8px">${icon("user", 11)} ${U.esc(d.person)}</span>
            <span class="conf-bar" data-tip="confidence ${d.confidence}"><i style="width:${Math.round(d.confidence * 100)}%"></i></span>
            <span class="faint small">${U.esc(d.sourceName || "")}</span>
            ${rev ? `<span class="chip sev-${rev.type === "boomerang" ? "critical" : "warn"}">${icon("zap", 11)} ${U.esc(rev.rule)}</span>` : ""}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>`;
    }).join("");

    detailHost.querySelectorAll("[data-split]").forEach(cb => cb.addEventListener("change", () => {
      if (cb.checked) pages.topics._splitSel.add(cb.dataset.split);
      else pages.topics._splitSel.delete(cb.dataset.split);
      drawDetail();
    }));

    detailHost.querySelector("#tp-rename").onclick = async () => {
      const name = await askText("Rename topic", "A clearer name…", t.name);
      if (!name) return;
      try { await api.put(`/topics/${t.id}`, { name }); } catch (e) { ui.toastError(e.message); }
    };
    detailHost.querySelector("#tp-merge").onclick = () => {
      pages.topics._mode = inMerge ? "view" : "merge";
      drawDetail(); drawList();
    };
    detailHost.querySelector("#tp-split").onclick = () => {
      pages.topics._mode = "split";
      pages.topics._splitSel = new Set();
      drawDetail();
    };
    const splitGo = detailHost.querySelector("#tp-split-go");
    if (splitGo) splitGo.onclick = async () => {
      const ids = [...pages.topics._splitSel];
      if (!ids.length) return;
      const name = await askText("New topic name", "Name the split topic", `${t.name} (split)`);
      if (!name) return;
      try {
        await api.post("/topics/split", { topicId: t.id, decisionIds: ids, name });
        pages.topics._mode = "view";
        pages.topics._splitSel = new Set();
        ui.toast({ msg: `Split ${ids.length} decisions into “${name}”`, type: "ok" });
      } catch (e) { ui.toastError(e.message); }
    };
    const splitCancel = detailHost.querySelector("#tp-split-cancel");
    if (splitCancel) splitCancel.onclick = () => { pages.topics._mode = "view"; pages.topics._splitSel = new Set(); drawDetail(); };

    drawStates(decs);
  }

  // state diagram: options on a circle, animated flow along transitions
  function drawStates(decs) {
    const canvas = detailHost.querySelector("#tp-states");
    if (!canvas) return;
    let raf;
    const opts = [];
    decs.forEach(d => { if (d.option && !opts.includes(d.option)) opts.push(d.option); });
    const trans = new Map(); // "a→b" -> count
    let prev = null;
    decs.forEach(d => {
      if (!d.option) return;
      if (prev && prev !== d.option) {
        const k = prev + "→" + d.option;
        trans.set(k, (trans.get(k) || 0) + 1);
      }
      prev = d.option;
    });
    const C = charts.colors();
    const frame = time => {
      const { ctx, w, h } = charts.fitCanvas(canvas);
      ctx.clearRect(0, 0, w, h);
      const cx = w / 2, cy = h / 2 + 4, r = Math.min(w, h) / 2 - 44;
      const n = Math.max(opts.length, 3);
      const pos = i => {
        const a = -Math.PI / 2 + i * 2 * Math.PI / n;
        return { x: cx + Math.cos(a) * r, y: cy + Math.sin(a) * r };
      };
      // edges
      trans.forEach((count, key) => {
        const [a, b] = key.split("→");
        const ia = opts.indexOf(a), ib = opts.indexOf(b);
        if (ia < 0 || ib < 0) return;
        const pa = pos(ia), pb = pos(ib);
        const mx = (pa.x + pb.x) / 2, my = (pa.y + pb.y) / 2;
        const pull = 26 + count * 6;
        const bend = { x: mx + (mx - cx) / r * pull, y: my + (my - cy) / r * pull };
        const boom = a !== b && decs.some((d, i) => d.option === b && i > 0 && decs[i - 1].option === a && store.reversalsOf(sel()).some(rv => rv.toId === d.id));
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(pa.x, pa.y);
        ctx.quadraticCurveTo(bend.x, bend.y, pb.x, pb.y);
        ctx.strokeStyle = boom ? C.ac3 : "rgba(148,163,255,0.4)";
        ctx.lineWidth = 1.2 + count * 0.9;
        ctx.setLineDash(boom ? [] : [5, 4]); ctx.lineDashOffset = -time / 60;
        ctx.stroke(); ctx.setLineDash([]);
        // flowing particles
        for (let k = 0; k < count; k++) {
          const tt = ((time / 1600) + k * 0.35 + ia * 0.13) % 1;
          const x = (1 - tt) * (1 - tt) * pa.x + 2 * (1 - tt) * tt * bend.x + tt * tt * pb.x;
          const y = (1 - tt) * (1 - tt) * pa.y + 2 * (1 - tt) * tt * bend.y + tt * tt * pb.y;
          ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(x, y, 2.2, 0, 7);
          ctx.fillStyle = boom ? C.ac3 : C.ac2; ctx.fill();
        }
      });
      // nodes
      opts.forEach((o, i) => {
        const p = pos(i);
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(p.x, p.y, 20, 0, 7);
        ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(16,20,42,0.9)"; ctx.fill();
        ctx.strokeStyle = C.ac1; ctx.lineWidth = 1.6;
        ctx.shadowColor = C.ac1; ctx.shadowBlur = 10; ctx.stroke(); ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
        ctx.fillStyle = "#e9edff"; ctx.font = "700 10px Manrope"; ctx.textAlign = "center";
        ctx.fillText(o.length > 10 ? o.slice(0, 9) + "…" : o, p.x, p.y + 3.5);
      });
      raf = requestAnimationFrame(frame);
    };
    raf = requestAnimationFrame(frame);
    router.pushDisposer(() => cancelAnimationFrame(raf));
  }

  el.querySelector("#tp-search").addEventListener("input", e => {
    search = e.target.value;
    drawList();
  });

  const undoBtn = el.querySelector("#tp-undo"), redoBtn = el.querySelector("#tp-redo");
  const syncUndo = () => {
    undoBtn.disabled = !services.undoDepth();
    redoBtn.disabled = !services.redoDepth();
  };
  syncUndo();
  undoBtn.onclick = () => pages.undoTopicOp();
  redoBtn.onclick = () => pages.redoTopicOp();
  router.sub("topics:merged", syncUndo);
  router.sub("topics:split", syncUndo);
  router.sub("topics:undo", syncUndo);
  router.sub("topics:redo", syncUndo);

  drawList();
  drawDetail();
};


/* ======================= PATTERN MAP ======================= */
pages.map = function (el) {
  if (!store.state.topics.some(t => t.decisionCount)) {
    el.innerHTML = `<div class="sec-head"><h2>Pattern map</h2></div>`;
    el.appendChild(ui.empty("map", "Nothing to map yet",
      "Topics and their options become a living constellation — edge thickness is flip count, jitter is instability.",
      "Load demo data", () => pages.runDemo()));
    return;
  }

  el.innerHTML = `
    ${ui.sectionHead("Pattern map", "topics and options as a living graph — drag nodes, hover for detail, click a topic to open it")}
    <div class="card" style="padding:8px">
      <canvas id="map-canvas" style="width:100%;height:560px;touch-action:none;cursor:grab" aria-label="Topic and option force graph"></canvas>
    </div>
    <p class="faint small" style="margin-top:10px">node size = decisions · jitter = instability · magenta edges = flip paths (thicker = more flips) · hand-rolled force simulation</p>`;

  const canvas = el.querySelector("#map-canvas");
  const ac = new AbortController();
  const SIG = { signal: ac.signal };
  let raf = null, hoverNode = null, dragNode = null;

  // build the graph from the store
  const nodes = [], edges = [], nodeIdx = new Map();
  store.state.topics.filter(t => t.decisionCount).forEach(t => {
    const n = { id: "T" + t.id, kind: "topic", label: t.name, ref: t,
      r: 15 + Math.min(20, t.decisionCount * 2.6), score: t.score,
      x: 0, y: 0, vx: 0, vy: 0 };
    nodes.push(n); nodeIdx.set(n.id, n);
  });
  const optNodes = new Map();
  store.state.decisions.forEach(d => {
    if (!d.option || !d.topicId) return;
    const key = d.topicId + "|" + d.option;
    if (!optNodes.has(key)) {
      const n = { id: "O" + key, kind: "option", label: d.option, topicId: d.topicId,
        r: 6.5, x: 0, y: 0, vx: 0, vy: 0, count: 0 };
      optNodes.set(key, n); nodes.push(n); nodeIdx.set(n.id, n);
    }
    optNodes.get(key).count++;
    edges.push({ a: nodeIdx.get("T" + d.topicId), b: optNodes.get(key), kind: "member", w: 0.5 });
  });
  // flip edges between options of the same topic
  store.state.reversals.forEach(r => {
    if (!r.from || !r.to) return;
    const a = nodeIdx.get("O" + r.topicId + "|" + r.from);
    const b = nodeIdx.get("O" + r.topicId + "|" + Engine.canonOption(r.to));
    if (a && b && a !== b) edges.push({ a, b, kind: "flip", w: 1.5 });
  });

  // scatter starting positions on a ring so the sim starts civilised
  nodes.forEach((n, i) => {
    const a = (i / nodes.length) * Math.PI * 2;
    n.x = 300 + Math.cos(a) * (120 + Math.random() * 120);
    n.y = 250 + Math.sin(a) * (100 + Math.random() * 100);
  });

  const frame = time => {
    const { ctx, w, h } = charts.fitCanvas(canvas);
    const C = charts.colors();

    // physics — repulsion, springs, centering gravity, instability jitter
    if (!dragNode || !dragNode.kind) { /* still simulate while dragging others */ }
    for (let i = 0; i < nodes.length; i++) {
      const a = nodes[i];
      for (let j = i + 1; j < nodes.length; j++) {
        const b = nodes[j];
        let dx = a.x - b.x, dy = a.y - b.y;
        let d2 = dx * dx + dy * dy;
        if (d2 < 1) { dx = Math.random() - 0.5; dy = Math.random() - 0.5; d2 = 1; }
        const d = Math.sqrt(d2);
        const rep = 2400 / d2;
        const fx = dx / d * rep, fy = dy / d * rep;
        a.vx += fx; a.vy += fy; b.vx -= fx; b.vy -= fy;
      }
    }
    edges.forEach(e => {
      const dx = e.b.x - e.a.x, dy = e.b.y - e.a.y;
      const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
      const rest = e.kind === "flip" ? 150 : 78;
      const f = (d - rest) * 0.012;
      const fx = dx / d * f, fy = dy / d * f;
      e.a.vx += fx; e.a.vy += fy; e.b.vx -= fx; e.b.vy -= fy;
    });
    nodes.forEach(n => {
      n.vx += (w / 2 - n.x) * 0.0026;
      n.vy += (h / 2 - n.y) * 0.0026;
      if (n.kind === "topic" && n.score > 45) {
        // restless topics physically fidget
        const j = (n.score - 45) / 55;
        n.vx += (Math.random() - 0.5) * j * 1.5;
        n.vy += (Math.random() - 0.5) * j * 1.5;
      }
      if (n === dragNode) { n.vx = 0; n.vy = 0; return; }
      n.vx *= 0.86; n.vy *= 0.86;
      n.x += n.vx; n.y += n.vy;
      n.x = U.clamp(n.x, 26, w - 26); n.y = U.clamp(n.y, 26, h - 26);
    });

    ctx.clearRect(0, 0, w, h);
    edges.forEach(e => {
      const hot = e.kind === "flip";
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(e.a.x, e.a.y); ctx.lineTo(e.b.x, e.b.y);
      ctx.strokeStyle = hot ? C.ac3 : "rgba(148,163,255,0.16)";
      ctx.lineWidth = hot ? 1.4 + e.w : 1;
      ctx.globalAlpha = hot ? 0.85 : 0.7;
      if (hot) { ctx.shadowColor = C.ac3; ctx.shadowBlur = 8; }
      ctx.stroke(); ctx.shadowBlur = 0; ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
      if (hot) {
        const tt = (time / 1300 + e.a.x * 0.01) % 1;
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(e.a.x + (e.b.x - e.a.x) * tt, e.a.y + (e.b.y - e.a.y) * tt, 2.4, 0, 7);
        ctx.fillStyle = C.ac3; ctx.fill();
      }
    });

    nodes.forEach(n => {
      const col = n.kind === "topic"
        ? ["#3ddc97", "#22d3ee", "#ffb020", "#ff9a5c", "#ff3d9a"][n.ref.levelIdx ?? 0]
        : C.ac2;
      if (n === hoverNode || n === dragNode) {
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(n.x, n.y, n.r + 7, 0, 7);
        ctx.strokeStyle = col; ctx.globalAlpha = 0.5; ctx.stroke(); ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
      }
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(n.x, n.y, n.r, 0, 7);
      if (n.kind === "topic") {
        const g = ctx.createRadialGradient(n.x - n.r * 0.35, n.y - n.r * 0.35, 1, n.x, n.y, n.r);
        g.addColorStop(0, col); g.addColorStop(1, "rgba(16,20,42,0.95)");
        ctx.fillStyle = g;
        ctx.shadowColor = col; ctx.shadowBlur = 18;
        ctx.fill(); ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
        ctx.strokeStyle = col; ctx.lineWidth = 1.4; ctx.stroke();
      } else {
        ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(16,20,42,0.95)"; ctx.fill();
        ctx.strokeStyle = col; ctx.lineWidth = 1.2; ctx.stroke();
      }
      ctx.fillStyle = n.kind === "topic" ? "#e9edff" : "rgba(154,165,204,0.9)";
      ctx.font = (n.kind === "topic" ? "700 11px" : "600 9.5px") + " Manrope, sans-serif";
      ctx.textAlign = "center";
      ctx.fillText(n.label.length > 16 ? n.label.slice(0, 15) + "…" : n.label, n.x, n.y + n.r + 12);
    });
    raf = requestAnimationFrame(frame);
  };
  raf = requestAnimationFrame(frame);

  const pick = (mx, my) => {
    for (let i = nodes.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
      const n = nodes[i];
      if (Math.hypot(n.x - mx, n.y - my) < n.r + 8) return n;
    }
    return null;
  };
  canvas.addEventListener("pointerdown", e => {
    const r = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
    const n = pick(e.clientX - r.left, e.clientY - r.top);
    if (n) { dragNode = n; canvas.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId); canvas.style.cursor = "grabbing"; }
  }, SIG);
  canvas.addEventListener("pointermove", e => {
    const r = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
    const mx = e.clientX - r.left, my = e.clientY - r.top;
    if (dragNode) { dragNode.x = mx; dragNode.y = my; return; }
    hoverNode = pick(mx, my);
    canvas.style.cursor = hoverNode ? "pointer" : "grab";
    if (hoverNode) {
      const n = hoverNode;
      const tip = U.$("#tooltip");
      tip.innerHTML = n.kind === "topic"
        ? `<strong>${U.esc(n.label)}</strong><div class="small muted" style="margin-top:4px">${n.ref.decisionCount} decisions · ${n.ref.flips} flips · score ${n.ref.score} (${n.ref.level})</div><div class="small faint">click to open</div>`
        : `<strong>${U.esc(n.label)}</strong><div class="small muted" style="margin-top:4px">${n.count} decisions chose this option</div>`;
      tip.classList.add("show");
      tip.style.left = U.clamp(e.clientX + 14, 8, innerWidth - tip.offsetWidth - 8) + "px";
      tip.style.top = U.clamp(e.clientY - tip.offsetHeight - 12, 8, innerHeight - 20) + "px";
    } else U.$("#tooltip").classList.remove("show");
  }, SIG);
  canvas.addEventListener("pointerup", e => {
    const wasDrag = dragNode;
    dragNode = null;
    canvas.style.cursor = "grab";
    if (wasDrag) {
      const r = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
      const moved = Math.hypot(e.clientX - r.left - wasDrag.x, e.clientY - r.top - wasDrag.y);
      if (moved < 6 && wasDrag.kind === "topic") router.go("/topics", { topic: wasDrag.ref.id });
    }
  }, SIG);
  canvas.addEventListener("pointerleave", () => { hoverNode = null; U.$("#tooltip").classList.remove("show"); }, SIG);

  return () => { ac.abort(); if (raf) cancelAnimationFrame(raf); };
};

/* ======================= HEATMAP ======================= */
pages.heatmap = function (el) {
  const revs = store.state.reversals;
  if (!revs.length) {
    el.innerHTML = `<div class="sec-head"><h2>Reversal heatmap</h2></div>`;
    el.appendChild(ui.empty("heatmap", "No reversal activity yet",
      "Once decisions start flipping, every day gets a cell — the brighter, the more reversals.",
      "Load demo data", () => pages.runDemo()));
    return;
  }

  const today = new Date(); today.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
  const end = today.getTime();
  const weeks = 26;
  // align the grid so the first column starts on a Monday
  const endDay = new Date(end);
  const shift = (endDay.getDay() + 6) % 7;
  const gridEnd = end + (6 - shift) * 86400e3;
  const gridStart = gridEnd - weeks * 7 * 86400e3 + 1;

  const byDay = store.reversalsByDay(gridStart, gridEnd);
  const dayCells = new Map(); // dateKey -> [cell elements]
  const cells = [];

  const host = document.createElement("div");
  host.className = "card";
  const days = ["Mon", "Tue", "Wed", "Thu", "Fri", "Sat", "Sun"];
  let grid = '<div class="heat-scroll"><div style="overflow:hidden"><span class="heat-days">' +
    days.map(d => `<span>${d}</span>`).join("") + "</span>";
  grid += '<div class="heat-grid" id="heat-grid">';
  const monthLabels = [];
  let lastMonth = -1;
  for (let wk = 0; wk < weeks; wk++) {
    for (let day = 0; day < 7; day++) {
      const ts = gridStart + (wk * 7 + day) * 86400e3;
      const d = new Date(ts);
      if (ts > end + 86400e3) { grid += '<span class="heat-cell" style="visibility:hidden"></span>'; continue; }
      const count = byDay.get(ts) || 0;
      const lvl = count === 0 ? "" : count === 1 ? "h1" : count === 2 ? "h2" : count <= 4 ? "h3" : "h4";
      grid += `<span class="heat-cell ${lvl}" data-ts="${ts}" data-count="${count}"
        style="transition-delay:${wk * 18}ms" tabindex="0" role="img"
        aria-label="${U.fmtDate(ts)}: ${count} reversals"></span>`;
      if (d.getMonth() !== lastMonth) { monthLabels.push({ wk, label: U.MONTHS[d.getMonth()] }); lastMonth = d.getMonth(); }
    }
  }
  grid += "</div>";
  let months = '<div class="heat-months">';
  let prevPos = 0;
  monthLabels.forEach(m => {
    const left = m.wk * 19;
    if (left - prevPos > 26) { months += `<span style="position:absolute;left:${left}px">${m.label}</span>`; prevPos = left; }
  });
  months += "</div>";
  grid += months + "</div></div>";
  host.innerHTML = `
    <div class="pulse-head" style="margin-bottom:12px">
      <h3 style="margin:0">Reversals, day by day</h3>
      <span class="small muted">${revs.length} reversals in the last ${weeks} weeks</span>
    </div>${grid}
    <div class="heat-legend"><span>calmer</span>
      <i style="background:var(--bg-3)"></i><i class="heat-cell h1" style="transform:none"></i><i class="heat-cell h2" style="transform:none"></i><i class="heat-cell h3" style="transform:none"></i><i class="heat-cell h4" style="transform:none"></i>
      <span>wilder</span></div>`;

  el.innerHTML = ui.sectionHead("Reversal heatmap", "GitHub-style, but for regret");
  el.appendChild(host);

  // staggered reveal once painted
  requestAnimationFrame(() => requestAnimationFrame(() => {
    host.querySelectorAll(".heat-cell").forEach(c => c.classList.add("in"));
  }));

  let tipTimer = null;
  host.querySelectorAll(".heat-cell[data-ts]").forEach(cell => {
    const show = () => {
      const count = +cell.dataset.count;
      const tip = U.$("#tooltip");
      const extra = count ? ` — ${count} reversal${count > 1 ? "s" : ""}` : " — quiet";
      tip.innerHTML = `<strong>${U.fmtDate(+cell.dataset.ts)}</strong>${extra}`;
      tip.classList.add("show");
      const r = cell.getBoundingClientRect();
      tip.style.left = U.clamp(r.left + r.width / 2 - tip.offsetWidth / 2, 8, innerWidth - tip.offsetWidth - 8) + "px";
      tip.style.top = (r.top - tip.offsetHeight - 10) + "px";
    };
    cell.addEventListener("mouseenter", show);
    cell.addEventListener("focus", show);
    cell.addEventListener("mouseleave", () => U.$("#tooltip").classList.remove("show"));
    cell.addEventListener("blur", () => U.$("#tooltip").classList.remove("show"));
    cell.addEventListener("click", () => {
      const ts = +cell.dataset.ts;
      const dayRevs = store.state.reversals.filter(r => r.ts >= ts && r.ts < ts + 86400e3);
      if (!dayRevs.length) return;
      ui.modal({
        title: `${U.fmtDate(ts)} — ${dayRevs.length} reversal${dayRevs.length > 1 ? "s" : ""}`,
        body: dayRevs.map(r => {
          const d = store.state.decisions.find(x => x.id === r.toId);
          const topic = store.topicById(r.topicId);
          return `<div class="decision-row">
            <div class="d-when">${U.fmtTime(r.ts)}</div>
            <div class="d-main">
              <div class="d-text">${d ? highlightMatches(d.text, d.matched) : U.esc(r.rule)}</div>
              <div class="d-meta">
                <span class="chip">${icon("user", 11)} ${U.esc(r.person)}</span>
                ${topic ? `<span class="chip">${icon("topics", 11)} ${U.esc(topic.name)}</span>` : ""}
                <span class="chip sev-critical">${icon("zap", 11)} ${U.esc(r.rule)}</span>
              </div></div></div>`;
        }).join(""),
        actions: [{ label: "Close", value: null }]
      });
    });
  });
};

/* ======================= PEOPLE ======================= */
pages.people = function (el, params) {
  const people = store.state.people;
  if (!people.length) {
    el.innerHTML = `<div class="sec-head"><h2>People</h2></div>`;
    el.appendChild(ui.empty("people", "No deciders on record yet",
      "Every person in your data gets a stability gauge, a radar of their quirks and behavior tags.",
      "Load demo data", () => pages.runDemo()));
    return;
  }
  const selected = params.person && people.some(p => p.person === params.person) ? params.person : null;

  if (!selected) {
    el.innerHTML = ui.sectionHead("People", "who flips, how fast, and when");
    const grid = document.createElement("div");
    grid.className = "people-grid";
    people.forEach(p => {
      const card = document.createElement("div");
      card.className = "card person-card tilt";
      card.setAttribute("data-reveal", "");
      card.setAttribute("role", "button");
      card.tabIndex = 0;
      card.innerHTML = `
        <div class="person-top">
          ${ui.avatar(p.person, 56)}
          <div style="flex:1;min-width:0">
            <div style="display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:center;flex-wrap:wrap"><strong style="font-size:1.05rem">${U.esc(p.person)}</strong>${ui.levelChip(p)}</div>
            <div class="small muted">${p.decisions} decisions · ${p.flips} flips · ${p.boomerangs} boomerangs</div>
          </div>
          <div class="tm-score" style="font-size:1.9rem">${p.score}</div>
        </div>
        <div class="leader-bar" style="height:6px"><i style="width:${p.score}%"></i></div>
        <div style="display:flex;gap:6px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-top:12px">
          ${p.tags.map(t => `<span class="tag-chip">${icon("spark", 11)} ${U.esc(t.label)}</span>`).join("")}
        </div>`;
      const open = () => router.go("/people", { person: p.person });
      card.addEventListener("click", open);
      card.addEventListener("keydown", e => { if (e.key === "Enter") open(); });
      grid.appendChild(card);
    });
    el.appendChild(grid);
    return;
  }

  // ---- profile ----
  const p = store.state.people.find(x => x.person === selected);
  el.innerHTML = `
    ${ui.sectionHead("People",
      `<button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" id="pp-back">${icon("chevronLeft", 13)} all people</button>`)}
    <div class="person-detail">
      <div class="card beam" data-reveal>
        <div class="person-top">
          ${ui.avatar(p.person, 64)}
          <div><strong style="font-size:1.3rem;font-family:var(--font-display)">${U.esc(p.person)}</strong>
            <div class="small muted">active ${U.timeAgo(p.firstTs)} → ${U.timeAgo(p.lastTs)}</div></div>
        </div>
        <canvas id="pp-gauge" style="width:100%;height:210px" aria-label="Decision stability gauge"></canvas>
        <div style="display:flex;gap:6px;flex-wrap:wrap;justify-content:center">
          ${p.tags.map(t => `<span class="tag-chip">${icon("spark", 11)} ${U.esc(t.label)}</span>`).join("")}
        </div>
        <div style="margin-top:16px">
          ${[["Decisions", p.decisions], ["Flips", p.flips], ["Boomerangs", p.boomerangs],
             ["Flip rate", Math.round(p.flipRate * 100) + "%"], ["Fastest flip", U.fmtGap(p.minGapMs)],
             ["Avg confidence", p.avgConf], ["Hedging", Math.round(p.hedgeRatio * 100) + "%"]]
            .map(([k, v]) => `<div class="switch-row"><span class="small muted">${k}</span><strong class="mono">${U.esc(String(v))}</strong></div>`).join("")}
        </div>
      </div>
      <div style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:16px">
        <div class="card" data-reveal>
          <h3 style="font-size:1.02rem">Decision DNA</h3>
          <canvas id="pp-radar" style="width:100%;height:270px" aria-label="Behavior radar chart"></canvas>
          <div class="radar-legend">
            <span>flip rate <b>${Math.round(p.flipRate * 100)}%</b></span>
            <span>boomerangs <b>${p.boomerangs}</b></span>
            <span>flip speed <b>${U.fmtGap(p.minGapMs)}</b></span>
            <span>confidence drift <b>${p.avgConf}</b></span>
            <span>hedging <b>${Math.round(p.hedgeRatio * 100)}%</b></span>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="card" data-reveal>
          <h3 style="font-size:1.02rem">When the flips happen</h3>
          <div style="display:flex;gap:3px;align-items:flex-end;height:64px" id="pp-hours"></div>
          <div class="small faint" style="margin-top:6px">hour of day (0–23)</div>
          <div style="display:flex;gap:6px;margin-top:14px" id="pp-days"></div>
        </div>
        <div class="card" data-reveal>
          <h3 style="font-size:1.02rem">Topics they touch</h3>
          ${p.topicShare.slice(0, 6).map(share => {
            const t = store.topicById(share.id);
            if (!t) return "";
            return `<div class="topic-mini" data-topic="${t.id}" role="button" tabindex="0">
              <div class="tm-score lvl-${t.levelIdx}">${t.score}</div>
              <div class="tm-body"><div class="tm-name">${U.esc(t.name)}</div>
                <div class="tm-bar"><i style="width:${Math.round(share.n / Math.max(...p.topicShare.map(s => s.n)) * 100)}%"></i></div>
              </div><span class="small faint">${share.n}×</span></div>`;
          }).join("") || `<p class="faint small">No topics yet.</p>`}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>`;

  el.querySelector("#pp-back").onclick = () => router.go("/people");
  el.querySelectorAll("[data-topic]").forEach(n => n.addEventListener("click", () => router.go("/topics", { topic: n.dataset.topic })));

  charts.gauge(el.querySelector("#pp-gauge"), p.score, { label: "flip-flop score", level: p.level });
  charts.radar(el.querySelector("#pp-radar"),
    ["flip rate", "boomerangs", "speed", "confidence", "hedging"], [
      U.clamp(p.flipRate / 0.6, 0, 1),
      U.clamp(p.boomerangs / 3, 0, 1),
      p.minGapMs != null ? U.clamp(1 - p.minGapMs / (30 * 86400e3), 0.05, 1) : 0.05,
      U.clamp(1 - p.avgConf, 0, 1),
      U.clamp(p.hedgeRatio * 2, 0, 1)
    ]);
  const maxH = Math.max(1, ...p.flipHours);
  el.querySelector("#pp-hours").innerHTML = p.flipHours.map((h, i) =>
    `<span data-tip="${i}:00 — ${h} flips" style="flex:1;height:${Math.max(6, h / maxH * 100)}%;border-radius:3px;background:${h ? "var(--grad)" : "var(--bg-3)"}"></span>`).join("");
  const dayNames = ["Sun", "Mon", "Tue", "Wed", "Thu", "Fri", "Sat"];
  const maxD = Math.max(1, ...p.flipDays);
  el.querySelector("#pp-days").innerHTML = p.flipDays.map((d, i) =>
    `<div style="flex:1;text-align:center" data-tip="${dayNames[i]} — ${d} flips">
      <div style="height:44px;display:flex;align-items:flex-end">
        <div style="width:100%;height:${Math.max(8, d / maxD * 100)}%;border-radius:4px;background:${d ? "var(--ac3)" : "var(--bg-3)"};box-shadow:${d ? "0 0 10px var(--ac3)" : "none"}"></div>
      </div><div class="small faint" style="margin-top:4px">${dayNames[i]}</div></div>`).join("");
};

/* ======================= IMPORT CENTER ======================= */
pages.import = function (el) {
  el.innerHTML = `
    ${ui.sectionHead("Import center", "chats, emails, task boards — format is auto-detected")}
    <div class="import-grid">
      <div class="drop-zone" id="dz-chat" data-kind="chat" tabindex="0" role="button" aria-label="Drop or pick a chat export">
        <canvas class="dz-sparks"></canvas>
        <div class="dz-ico">${icon("message", 24)}</div>
        <h4>Chats</h4>
        <p class="dz-hint">WhatsApp .txt · Slack JSON · "Name: message" lines</p>
        <div class="dz-actions"><button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm">Pick a file</button></div>
        <input type="file" accept=".txt,.json,.log" hidden>
      </div>
      <div class="drop-zone" id="dz-email" data-kind="email" tabindex="0" role="button" aria-label="Drop or pick an email export">
        <canvas class="dz-sparks"></canvas>
        <div class="dz-ico">${icon("mail", 24)}</div>
        <h4>Emails</h4>
        <p class="dz-hint">.eml / .mbox / pasted threads — quotes &amp; signatures stripped</p>
        <div class="dz-actions"><button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm">Pick a file</button></div>
        <input type="file" accept=".txt,.eml,.mbox" hidden>
      </div>
      <div class="drop-zone" id="dz-tasks" data-kind="tasks" tabindex="0" role="button" aria-label="Drop or pick a task board export">
        <canvas class="dz-sparks"></canvas>
        <div class="dz-ico">${icon("kanban", 24)}</div>
        <h4>Task boards</h4>
        <p class="dz-hint">CSV or JSON — status, due-date and history changes count as decisions</p>
        <div class="dz-actions"><button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm">Pick a file</button></div>
        <input type="file" accept=".csv,.json,.txt" hidden>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="card" style="margin-top:16px" data-reveal>
      <h3 style="font-size:1.02rem">…or paste anything</h3>
      <div class="field">
        <textarea class="input" id="paste-box" rows="6" placeholder="Paste messages, an email thread, or a CSV dump — the format lights up as it's detected."></textarea>
        <div class="underline"></div>
      </div>
      <div style="display:flex;gap:10px;align-items:center;flex-wrap:wrap">
        <span class="chip" id="paste-format"><span class="faint">format: —</span></span>
        <button class="btn btn-primary btn-sm" id="paste-go" style="margin-left:auto">
          <span class="sweep"></span>${icon("upload", 14)} Ingest through the pipeline</button>
        <button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" id="demo-go">${icon("spark", 14)} Load demo data</button>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fit,minmax(300px,1fr));gap:16px;margin-top:16px">
      <div class="card" data-reveal>
        <h3 style="font-size:1.02rem">Add a single decision</h3>
        <div class="field"><label>Who</label><input class="input" id="md-person" placeholder="Sam Rivera"></div>
        <div class="field"><label>The decision</label><textarea class="input" id="md-text" rows="2" style="min-height:60px;font-family:var(--font-body)" placeholder="Let's do React for the new site."></textarea></div>
        <div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:10px">
          <div class="field"><label>Option (optional)</label><input class="input" id="md-option" placeholder="react"></div>
          <div class="field"><label>Polarity</label><select class="input" id="md-polarity">
            <option value="commit">commit</option><option value="switch">switch</option>
            <option value="revert">revert</option><option value="reject">reject</option></select></div>
        </div>
        <button class="btn btn-primary btn-sm" id="md-go"><span class="sweep"></span>${icon("plus", 14)} Add decision</button>
      </div>
      <div class="card" data-reveal>
        <h3 style="font-size:1.02rem">Sources on file</h3>
        <div id="src-list"></div>
      </div>
    </div>`;

  const srcList = el.querySelector("#src-list");
  function drawSources() {
    const sources = [...store.state.sources].sort((a, b) => b.addedAt - a.addedAt);
    srcList.innerHTML = sources.map(s => {
      const ico = s.kind === "email" ? "mail" : s.kind === "tasks" ? "kanban" : "message";
      return `<div class="source-row">
        <span class="sr-ico">${icon(ico, 16)}</span>
        <div style="flex:1;min-width:0">
          <div style="font-weight:700;font-size:.88rem;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;white-space:nowrap">${U.esc(s.name)}</div>
          <div class="small faint">${s.messageCount} messages · ${s.format || s.kind} · added ${U.timeAgo(s.addedAt)}</div>
        </div>
        <button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" data-del="${s.id}" data-tip="Remove source and its decisions" aria-label="Delete ${U.esc(s.name)}">${icon("trash", 13)}</button>
      </div>`;
    }).join("") || `<p class="faint small">Nothing imported yet. The demo data makes a good first meal.</p>`;
    srcList.querySelectorAll("[data-del]").forEach(b => b.addEventListener("click", async () => {
      const ok = await ui.confirm({ title: "Remove source?", msg: "Its messages and decisions disappear, and everything is re-scored.", okLabel: "Remove", danger: true });
      if (!ok) return;
      try { await api.del("/sources/" + b.dataset.del); ui.toast({ msg: "Source removed", type: "ok" }); }
      catch (e) { ui.toastError(e.message); }
    }));
  }
  drawSources();
  router.sub("data:changed", drawSources);

  async function ingest(kindHint, raw, name) {
    if (!raw.trim()) { ui.toast({ msg: "There's nothing to ingest", type: "warn" }); return; }
    const pm = ui.progressModal("Ingesting “" + name + "”");
    pm.update({ pct: 4, label: "Reading input" });
    try {
      const res = await api.post("/ingest", {
        kind: kindHint, raw, name,
        onProgress: p => pm.update(p),
        onParsed: p => pm.update({ pct: 9, label: p.format + " — " + p.messages + " messages parsed" })
      });
      pm.close();
      ui.toast({
        title: "Ingested " + name,
        msg: `${res.messages} messages → ${res.decisions} new decisions · ${res.reversals} reversals total${res.alerts ? " · " + res.alerts + " new alert" + (res.alerts > 1 ? "s" : "") : ""}`,
        type: "ok", timeout: 6500
      });
    } catch (e) {
      pm.close();
      ui.toastError(e.message, "Import failed");
    }
  }

  // drop zones: file pick + drag/drop + cursor-chasing particles
  el.querySelectorAll(".drop-zone").forEach(zone => {
    const input = zone.querySelector("input");
    const btn = zone.querySelector("button");
    const openPicker = () => input.click();
    zone.addEventListener("click", e => { if (e.target === zone || e.target.closest(".dz-actions")) openPicker(); });
    zone.addEventListener("keydown", e => { if (e.key === "Enter" || e.key === " ") { e.preventDefault(); openPicker(); } });
    input.addEventListener("change", async () => {
      const file = input.files[0];
      if (file) ingest(zone.dataset.kind, await file.text(), file.name);
      input.value = "";
    });

    // particle sparks that chase the cursor while a file hovers over
    const cvs = zone.querySelector("canvas");
    let sparksRaf = null, target = null, parts = [];
    const startSparks = () => {
      if (sparksRaf || REDUCED || store.state.settings.calm) return;
      parts = Array.from({ length: 22 }, () => ({
        x: Math.random() * cvs.clientWidth, y: Math.random() * cvs.clientHeight,
        vx: 0, vy: 0, c: Math.random() > 0.5 ? "var(--ac2)" : "var(--ac1)"
      }));
      const loop = () => {
        const dpr = Math.min(2, devicePixelRatio || 1);
        if (cvs.width !== cvs.clientWidth * dpr) { cvs.width = cvs.clientWidth * dpr; cvs.height = cvs.clientHeight * dpr; }
        const ctx = cvs.getContext("2d");
        ctx.setTransform(dpr, 0, 0, dpr, 0, 0);
        ctx.clearRect(0, 0, cvs.clientWidth, cvs.clientHeight);
        for (const p of parts) {
          if (target) {
            const dx = target.x - p.x, dy = target.y - p.y;
            p.vx += dx * 0.0016; p.vy += dy * 0.0016;
          }
          p.vx *= 0.9; p.vy *= 0.9;
          p.x += p.vx; p.y += p.vy;
          ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(p.x, p.y, 1.6, 0, 7);
          ctx.fillStyle = p.c; ctx.globalAlpha = 0.75; ctx.fill();
        }
        ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
        sparksRaf = requestAnimationFrame(loop);
      };
      sparksRaf = requestAnimationFrame(loop);
    };
    const stopSparks = () => { if (sparksRaf) cancelAnimationFrame(sparksRaf); sparksRaf = null; target = null; };
    zone.addEventListener("dragenter", e => { e.preventDefault(); zone.classList.add("drag"); startSparks(); });
    zone.addEventListener("dragover", e => {
      e.preventDefault();
      const r = zone.getBoundingClientRect();
      target = { x: e.clientX - r.left, y: e.clientY - r.top };
    });
    zone.addEventListener("dragleave", () => { zone.classList.remove("drag"); stopSparks(); });
    zone.addEventListener("drop", async e => {
      e.preventDefault();
      zone.classList.remove("drag");
      stopSparks();
      const file = e.dataTransfer.files[0];
      if (file) ingest(zone.dataset.kind, await file.text(), file.name);
    });
  });

  // paste box with live format detection
  const pasteBox = el.querySelector("#paste-box");
  const fmtChip = el.querySelector("#paste-format");
  pasteBox.addEventListener("input", U.debounce(() => {
    const fmt = Engine.detectFormat(pasteBox.value);
    fmtChip.innerHTML = `<span class="${fmt === "unknown" ? "faint" : ""}">format: ${fmt}</span>`;
    fmtChip.classList.toggle("on", fmt !== "unknown");
  }, 220));
  el.querySelector("#paste-go").onclick = () => {
    const fmt = Engine.detectFormat(pasteBox.value);
    const kind = fmt.includes("email") ? "email" : fmt === "tasks" ? "tasks" : "chat";
    ingest(kind, pasteBox.value, "Pasted input " + new Date().toLocaleTimeString());
    pasteBox.value = "";
    fmtChip.innerHTML = '<span class="faint">format: —</span>';
  };
  el.querySelector("#demo-go").onclick = () => pages.runDemo();

  el.querySelector("#md-go").onclick = async () => {
    const person = el.querySelector("#md-person").value.trim();
    const text = el.querySelector("#md-text").value.trim();
    if (!text) { ui.toast({ msg: "Write the decision sentence first", type: "warn" }); return; }
    try {
      await api.post("/decisions", {
        person: person || "Anonymous",
        text, option: el.querySelector("#md-option").value.trim() || null,
        polarity: el.querySelector("#md-polarity").value, ts: Date.now()
      });
      el.querySelector("#md-text").value = ""; el.querySelector("#md-option").value = "";
      ui.toast({ msg: "Decision added — pipeline re-run", type: "ok" });
    } catch (e) { ui.toastError(e.message); }
  };
};

/* ======================= ALERTS ======================= */
pages.alerts = function (el, params) {
  let filter = params.filter || "all";
  const alerts = [...store.state.alerts].sort((a, b) => b.ts - a.ts);

  el.innerHTML = `
    ${ui.sectionHead("Alerts", "fired when a topic crosses its reversal threshold",
      `<button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" id="al-readall">${icon("check", 13)} Mark all read</button>
       <button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" id="al-clear">${icon("trash", 13)} Clear read</button>`)}
    <div class="tl-chips" id="al-filters">
      ${[["all", "All"], ["unread", "Unread"], ["warn", "Warnings"], ["critical", "Critical"]]
        .map(([id, label]) => `<button class="chip btn-chip ${filter === id ? "on" : ""}" data-f="${id}">${label}</button>`).join("")}
    </div>
    <div id="al-list" style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:12px"></div>`;

  function draw() {
    const list = el.querySelector("#al-list");
    const shown = alerts.filter(a =>
      filter === "all" ? true : filter === "unread" ? !a.read : a.severity === filter);
    if (!alerts.length) {
      list.innerHTML = "";
      list.appendChild(ui.empty("alert", "All quiet",
        "Alerts appear the moment a topic records its second reversal — the backend console shows the exact event."));
      return;
    }
    list.innerHTML = shown.map(a => {
      const topic = store.topicById(a.topicId);
      const rev = store.state.reversals.find(r => r.id === a.reversalId);
      const dec = rev && store.state.decisions.find(d => d.id === rev.toId);
      return `<div class="alert-card ${a.severity} ${a.read ? "" : "unread"}" data-id="${a.id}" role="button" tabindex="0">
        <div class="alert-ico">${icon("zap", 19)}</div>
        <div style="flex:1;min-width:0">
          <div style="display:flex;gap:10px;align-items:baseline;flex-wrap:wrap">
            <strong>${U.esc(a.title)}</strong>
            <span class="al-time">${U.fmtDateTime(a.ts)}</span>
            ${a.read ? "" : '<span class="chip sev-critical" style="padding:1px 8px;font-size:.62rem">new</span>'}
          </div>
          <div class="small muted" style="margin-top:3px">${U.esc(a.body)}</div>
          ${dec ? `<div class="small" style="margin-top:9px;padding:10px 12px;border-left:2.5px solid var(--${a.severity === "critical" ? "danger" : "warn"});background:var(--glass);border-radius:8px">
            “${highlightMatches(dec.text, dec.matched)}” — ${U.esc(dec.person)}</div>` : ""}
        </div>
        <div style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:8px;align-items:flex-end">
          ${topic ? `<button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" data-topic="${topic.id}">${icon("topics", 12)} Open topic</button>` : ""}
          ${a.read ? "" : `<button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" data-read="1">${icon("check", 12)} Mark read</button>`}
        </div>
      </div>`;
    }).join("") || `<p class="faint" style="padding:20px">No alerts match this filter.</p>`;

    list.querySelectorAll(".alert-card").forEach(card => {
      const readBtn = card.querySelector("[data-read]");
      if (readBtn) readBtn.addEventListener("click", async e => {
        e.stopPropagation();
        await api.put(`/alerts/${card.dataset.id}/read`);
      });
      const topicBtn = card.querySelector("[data-topic]");
      if (topicBtn) topicBtn.addEventListener("click", e => {
        e.stopPropagation();
        router.go("/topics", { topic: topicBtn.dataset.topic });
      });
      card.addEventListener("click", async () => {
        if (!card.classList.contains("unread")) return;
        await api.put(`/alerts/${card.dataset.id}/read`);
      });
      card.addEventListener("keydown", e => { if (e.key === "Enter") card.click(); });
    });
  }
  draw();
  router.sub("data:changed", draw);

  el.querySelector("#al-filters").querySelectorAll("[data-f]").forEach(c =>
    c.addEventListener("click", () => {
      filter = c.dataset.f;
      el.querySelectorAll("#al-filters .chip").forEach(x => x.classList.toggle("on", x === c));
      draw();
    }));
  el.querySelector("#al-readall").onclick = () => api.put("/alerts/read-all");
  el.querySelector("#al-clear").onclick = () => api.del("/alerts");
};

/* ======================= HOW IT WORKS ======================= */
pages.how = function (el) {
  el.innerHTML = `
    ${ui.sectionHead("How FlipTrace thinks", "plain algorithms, no black boxes — every step below runs on your own data in this file")}
    <div class="pipeline-flow" data-reveal>
      ${["Ingest|parse & normalise", "Extract|lexicon scoring", "Group|TF-IDF + similarity", "Detect|reversals & boomerangs", "Score|weighted blend", "Alert|threshold triggers"]
        .map((s, i, arr) => `<span class="pf-node">${s.split("|")[0]}<small>${s.split("|")[1]}</small></span>${i < arr.length - 1 ? '<span class="pf-link"><i></i></span>' : ""}`).join("")}
    </div>

    <div class="how-step" data-reveal>
      <div id="clip-1"></div>
      <div>
        <h3><span class="grad-text">01 — Messages become decisions</span></h3>
        <p class="muted">Every message is sentence-split, then each sentence is scored against a hand-tuned <strong>lexicon</strong>: commitment phrases (“let's go with”, “locked in”, “final answer”), reversal phrases (“actually”, “scratch that”, “back to”), and hedges (“maybe”, “probably”) that lower confidence.</p>
        <p class="muted">A pattern extractor pulls out the <strong>option</strong> being chosen — “React” from “we're going with React for the frontend” — and marks the polarity (commit / switch / reject / revert) plus a 0–1 confidence. You can edit every phrase in Settings, live.</p>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="how-step" data-reveal>
      <div id="clip-2"></div>
      <div>
        <h3><span class="grad-text">02 — Same topic, different days</span></h3>
        <p class="muted">Decisions are tokenised, stop-worded and lightly stemmed, then turned into <strong>TF-IDF vectors</strong>. New decisions join a topic when cosine similarity, Jaccard overlap of top keywords, and an <strong>option-vocabulary affinity</strong> (React/Vue/Svelte clearly belong together) clear a threshold you can tune.</p>
        <p class="muted">Wrong grouping? Merge or split topics by hand — with full undo.</p>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="how-step" data-reveal>
      <div id="clip-3"></div>
      <div>
        <h3><span class="grad-text">03 — The flip</span></h3>
        <p class="muted">Within a topic, decisions are ordered by time. A <strong>reversal</strong> fires when the chosen option changes — or a commit is followed by a reject — inside a configurable window (45 days by default). A <strong>boomerang</strong> is the loudest signal: A → B → A, returning to something already tried.</p>
        <p class="muted">Task-board progress (backlog → in progress → done) is deliberately ignored — that's work, not wobble.</p>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="how-step" data-reveal>
      <div id="clip-4"></div>
      <div>
        <h3><span class="grad-text">04 — The score</span></h3>
        <p class="muted">Each topic blends flip count (30%), boomerangs (25%), flip speed (15%), oscillation (15%), decision entropy (10%) and confidence drift (5%) into a 0–100 <strong>Flip-Flop Score</strong>:</p>
        <p><span class="lvl lvl-0">Stable</span> <span class="lvl lvl-1">Wobbly</span> <span class="lvl lvl-2">Restless</span> <span class="lvl lvl-3">Chaotic</span> <span class="lvl lvl-4">Full Pendulum</span></p>
        <p class="muted small">When a new decision lands and a topic crosses its second reversal, an alert fires with the exact sentence, who said it, when, and which rule matched.</p>
      </div>
    </div>`;

  clips.mount(el.querySelector("#clip-1"), clips.scenes.clip1);
  clips.mount(el.querySelector("#clip-2"), clips.scenes.clip2);
  clips.mount(el.querySelector("#clip-3"), clips.scenes.clip3);
  clips.mount(el.querySelector("#clip-4"), clips.scenes.clip4);
};

/* ======================= SETTINGS ======================= */
pages.settings = function (el) {
  const s = store.state.settings;
  el.innerHTML = `
    ${ui.sectionHead("Settings", "the knobs the detection engine actually reads")}
    <div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fit,minmax(330px,1fr));gap:16px">

      <div class="card" data-reveal>
        <h3 style="font-size:1.05rem">${icon("target", 16)} Detection</h3>
        <div class="field" style="margin-top:12px"><label>Reversal window — ${s.reversalWindowDays} days</label>
          <div style="display:flex;gap:12px;align-items:center">
            <input type="range" class="range" id="set-window" min="7" max="120" step="1" value="${s.reversalWindowDays}">
            <span class="range-val" id="set-window-v">${s.reversalWindowDays}d</span></div>
          <p class="small faint" style="margin:4px 0 0">A flip only counts if it happens inside this window.</p></div>
        <div class="field"><label>Topic similarity threshold</label>
          <div style="display:flex;gap:12px;align-items:center">
            <input type="range" class="range" id="set-sim" min="15" max="60" step="5" value="${Math.round(s.similarity * 100)}">
            <span class="range-val" id="set-sim-v">${Math.round(s.similarity * 100)}%</span></div>
          <p class="small faint" style="margin:4px 0 0">Higher = stricter grouping (fewer, tighter topics). Re-runs the full clustering.</p></div>
        <div class="field"><label>Alert after N reversals</label>
          <div style="display:flex;gap:12px;align-items:center">
            <input type="range" class="range" id="set-minrev" min="2" max="6" step="1" value="${s.alertMinReversals}">
            <span class="range-val" id="set-minrev-v">${s.alertMinReversals}</span></div></div>
        <div class="field"><label>Critical alert score</label>
          <div style="display:flex;gap:12px;align-items:center">
            <input type="range" class="range" id="set-crit" min="40" max="95" step="5" value="${s.alertCriticalScore}">
            <span class="range-val" id="set-crit-v">${s.alertCriticalScore}</span></div></div>
        <button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" id="set-rerun" data-tip="Re-cluster everything from scratch">${icon("refresh", 13)} Re-run full analysis</button>
      </div>

      <div class="card" data-reveal>
        <h3 style="font-size:1.05rem">${icon("edit", 16)} Lexicon editor</h3>
        <p class="small faint">Add or remove phrases — the engine re-scores on every change.</p>
        <div id="lex-host"></div>
        <button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" id="lex-reset" style="margin-top:10px">Restore defaults</button>
      </div>

      <div class="card" data-reveal>
        <h3 style="font-size:1.05rem">${icon("sun", 16)} Look &amp; feel</h3>
        <div style="display:flex;gap:10px;margin:12px 0 6px" id="theme-cards"></div>
        <div class="switch-row"><span class="small">Custom cursor <span class="faint">(desktop)</span></span><button class="switch ${s.cursor ? "on" : ""}" id="set-cursor" role="switch" aria-checked="${s.cursor}"></button></div>
        <div class="switch-row"><span class="small">Calm mode <span class="faint">(toned-down motion)</span></span><button class="switch ${s.calm ? "on" : ""}" id="set-calm" role="switch" aria-checked="${s.calm}"></button></div>
        <div class="switch-row"><span class="small">Live feed <span class="faint">(scripted messages through the real pipeline)</span></span><button class="switch ${feed.on() ? "on" : ""}" id="set-feed" role="switch" aria-checked="${feed.on()}"></button></div>
      </div>

      <div class="card" data-reveal>
        <h3 style="font-size:1.05rem">${icon("download", 16)} Your data</h3>
        <p class="small faint">Everything lives in ${U.esc(dbx.mode)} storage on this device. No network calls — ever.</p>
        <div style="display:flex;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin:12px 0">
          <button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" id="exp-json">${icon("download", 13)} JSON backup</button>
          <button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" id="exp-csv">${icon("download", 13)} Decisions CSV</button>
          <button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" id="exp-revcsv">${icon("download", 13)} Reversals CSV</button>
          <button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" id="exp-report">${icon("book", 13)} Print report</button>
        </div>
        <div style="display:flex;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap">
          <button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" id="imp-json">${icon("upload", 13)} Restore backup</button>
          <input type="file" id="imp-file" accept=".json" hidden>
          <button class="btn btn-danger btn-sm" id="reset-all">${icon("trash", 13)} Reset everything</button>
        </div>
        <p class="small faint" id="store-stats" style="margin-top:14px"></p>
      </div>
    </div>`;

  const st = store.stats();
  el.querySelector("#store-stats").textContent =
    `${st.messages} messages · ${st.decisions} decisions · ${st.topics} topics · ${st.reversals} reversals · ${st.alerts} alerts in ${dbx.mode}`;

  // sliders → settings (debounced save, engine rescores automatically)
  const bindSlider = (id, key, fmt, transform) => {
    const input = el.querySelector("#" + id), val = el.querySelector("#" + id + "-v");
    input.addEventListener("input", () => val.textContent = fmt(input.value));
    input.addEventListener("change", U.debounce(async () => {
      try { await api.put("/settings", { [key]: transform(+input.value) }); ui.toast({ msg: "Saved — engine re-scored", type: "ok", timeout: 1800 }); }
      catch (e) { ui.toastError(e.message); }
    }, 400));
  };
  bindSlider("set-window", "reversalWindowDays", v => v + "d", v => v);
  bindSlider("set-sim", "similarity", v => v + "%", v => v / 100);
  bindSlider("set-minrev", "alertMinReversals", v => v, v => v);
  bindSlider("set-crit", "alertCriticalScore", v => v, v => v);
  el.querySelector("#set-rerun").onclick = () => pages.rerunFull();

  // lexicon editor
  const lexHost = el.querySelector("#lex-host");
  function drawLex() {
    const groups = [["commit", "Commitment phrases", "commit"], ["reversal", "Reversal phrases", "reversal"], ["reject", "Rejection phrases", "reject"], ["hedge", "Hedges (lower confidence)", "hedge"]];
    lexHost.innerHTML = groups.map(([key, label, cls]) => `
      <div style="margin:13px 0 6px"><strong class="small">${label}</strong></div>
      <div class="lex-list" data-g="${key}">
        ${s.lexicon[key].map(p => `<span class="lex-chip ${cls}">"${U.esc(p)}"
          <button data-rm="${U.esc(p)}" data-g="${key}" aria-label="Remove ${U.esc(p)}">${icon("x", 10)}</button></span>`).join("")}
      </div>
      <div class="lex-add"><input data-add="${key}" placeholder="add a phrase…"><button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" data-go="${key}">${icon("plus", 12)}</button></div>`).join("");
    lexHost.querySelectorAll("[data-rm]").forEach(b => b.addEventListener("click", async () => {
      const g = b.dataset.g;
      s.lexicon[g] = s.lexicon[g].filter(p => p !== b.dataset.rm);
      await api.put("/settings", { lexicon: { [g]: s.lexicon[g] } });
      drawLex();
    }));
    lexHost.querySelectorAll("[data-go]").forEach(b => b.addEventListener("click", async () => {
      const input = lexHost.querySelector(`[data-add="${b.dataset.go}"]`);
      const phrase = input.value.trim().toLowerCase().replace(/[.]$/, "");
      if (!phrase) return;
      if (!s.lexicon[b.dataset.go].includes(phrase)) {
        s.lexicon[b.dataset.go].push(phrase);
        await api.put("/settings", { lexicon: { [b.dataset.go]: s.lexicon[b.dataset.go] } });
      }
      drawLex();
    }));
    lexHost.querySelectorAll("[data-add]").forEach(input => input.addEventListener("keydown", e => {
      if (e.key === "Enter") { e.preventDefault(); lexHost.querySelector(`[data-go="${input.dataset.add}"]`).click(); }
    }));
  }
  drawLex();
  el.querySelector("#lex-reset").onclick = async () => {
    const ok = await ui.confirm({ title: "Restore default lexicon?", msg: "Your custom phrases will be replaced.", okLabel: "Restore" });
    if (!ok) return;
    s.lexicon = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(Engine.DEFAULT_LEXICON));
    await api.put("/settings", { lexicon: s.lexicon });
    drawLex();
    ui.toast({ msg: "Default lexicon restored", type: "ok" });
  };

  // theme cards
  const themesHost = el.querySelector("#theme-cards");
  function drawThemes() {
    themesHost.innerHTML = theme.order.map(t => `
      <button class="card ${theme.current() === t ? "on" : ""}" data-th="${t}"
        style="flex:1;padding:10px;cursor:pointer;${theme.current() === t ? "border-color:var(--ac1);box-shadow:var(--glow-1)" : ""}">
        <div style="height:34px;border-radius:8px;background:${{
          midnight: "linear-gradient(120deg,#7c5cff,#22d3ee)",
          aurora: "linear-gradient(120deg,#00d4b4,#4cc2ff)",
          ember: "linear-gradient(120deg,#ff7a45,#ffb020)"
        }[t]}"></div>
        <div class="small" style="margin-top:7px;font-weight:700">${theme.labels[t]}</div>
      </button>`).join("");
    themesHost.querySelectorAll("[data-th]").forEach(b => b.addEventListener("click", async () => {
      theme.apply(b.dataset.th);
      await api.put("/settings", { theme: b.dataset.th });
      bus.emit("theme:changed", b.dataset.th);
      drawThemes();
    }));
  }
  drawThemes();

  const bindSwitch = (id, key, after) => {
    const sw = el.querySelector("#" + id);
