    sw.addEventListener("click", async () => {
      const on = !sw.classList.contains("on");
      sw.classList.toggle("on", on);
      sw.setAttribute("aria-checked", String(on));
      await api.put("/settings", { [key]: on });
      after && after(on);
    });
  };
  bindSwitch("set-cursor", "cursor", on => on ? cursor.enable() : cursor.disable());
  bindSwitch("set-calm", "calm", on => { document.body.classList.toggle("calm", on); bg.recalibrate(); });
  bindSwitch("set-feed", "liveFeed", on => feed.toggle(on));

  el.querySelector("#exp-json").onclick = () => pages.exportData("json");
  el.querySelector("#exp-csv").onclick = () => pages.exportData("csv");
  el.querySelector("#exp-revcsv").onclick = () => pages.exportData("revcsv");
  el.querySelector("#exp-report").onclick = () => pages.printReport();
  const impBtn = el.querySelector("#imp-json"), impFile = el.querySelector("#imp-file");
  impBtn.onclick = () => impFile.click();
  impFile.addEventListener("change", async () => {
    const file = impFile.files[0];
    if (!file) return;
    try {
      const dump = JSON.parse(await file.text());
      await api.post("/import", { dump });
      ui.toast({ msg: "Backup restored", type: "ok" });
    } catch (e) { ui.toastError(e.message, "Restore failed"); }
    impFile.value = "";
  });
  el.querySelector("#reset-all").onclick = () => pages.resetAll();
};

/* ======================= shared actions ======================= */
pages.runDemo = async function () {
  const pm = ui.progressModal("Loading demo data");
  pm.update({ pct: 2, label: "Staging four sources" });
  try {
    await api.post("/demo", { onProgress: p => pm.update(p) });
    pm.close();
    ui.toast({ title: "Demo data loaded", msg: "Three people · three months · four sources. Watch the console to see the pipeline chew through it.", type: "ok", timeout: 6500 });
    router.go("/dashboard");
  } catch (e) { pm.close(); ui.toastError(e.message, "Demo load failed"); }
};

pages.rerunFull = async function () {
  const pm = ui.progressModal("Re-running the full pipeline");
  try {
    await api.post("/analyze", { mode: "full", onProgress: p => pm.update(p) });
    pm.close();
    ui.toast({ msg: "Everything re-clustered from scratch", type: "ok" });
  } catch (e) { pm.close(); ui.toastError(e.message); }
};

pages.exportData = async function (kind) {
  try {
    if (kind === "json") {
      const dump = await api.get("/export/json");
      U.download("fliptrace-backup.json", JSON.stringify(dump, null, 2), "application/json");
    } else if (kind === "csv") {
      U.download("fliptrace-decisions.csv", await api.get("/export/csv?kind=decisions"), "text/csv");
    } else {
      U.download("fliptrace-reversals.csv", await api.get("/export/csv?kind=reversals"), "text/csv");
    }
    ui.toast({ msg: "Export downloaded", type: "ok" });
  } catch (e) { ui.toastError(e.message, "Export failed"); }
};

pages.printReport = async function () {
  const dump = await api.get("/export/json");
  const s = store.stats();
  const topicName = new Map(dump.topics.map(t => [t.id, t.name]));
  const view = U.$("#report-view");
  view.innerHTML = `
    <h1 style="font-size:26px">FlipTrace — stability report</h1>
    <p>Produced ${new Date().toLocaleString()} · ${s.decisions} decisions across ${s.topics} topics</p>
    <h2>Summary</h2>
    <p>Average Flip-Flop Score: <strong>${s.avgScore}</strong> (${Engine.levelOf(s.avgScore).label}) ·
      ${s.reversals} reversals (${s.boomerangs} boomerangs) · ${s.people} people tracked.</p>
    <h2>Topics</h2>
    <table><tr><th>Topic</th><th>Decisions</th><th>Flips</th><th>Boomerangs</th><th>Fastest flip</th><th>Score</th><th>Level</th></tr>
      ${dump.topics.filter(t => t.decisionCount).sort((a, b) => b.score - a.score).map(t =>
        `<tr><td>${U.esc(t.name)}</td><td>${t.decisionCount}</td><td>${t.flips}</td><td>${t.boomerangs}</td><td>${U.fmtGap(t.minGapMs)}</td><td>${t.score}</td><td>${t.level}</td></tr>`).join("")}</table>
    <h2>People</h2>
    <table><tr><th>Person</th><th>Decisions</th><th>Flips</th><th>Boomerangs</th><th>Flip rate</th><th>Score</th><th>Level</th></tr>
      ${store.state.people.map(p => `<tr><td>${U.esc(p.person)}</td><td>${p.decisions}</td><td>${p.flips}</td><td>${p.boomerangs}</td><td>${Math.round(p.flipRate * 100)}%</td><td>${p.score}</td><td>${p.level}</td></tr>`).join("")}</table>
    <h2>Recent reversals</h2>
    <table><tr><th>When</th><th>Topic</th><th>Who</th><th>What flipped</th></tr>
      ${dump.reversals.sort((a, b) => b.ts - a.ts).slice(0, 30).map(r =>
        `<tr><td>${U.isoDate(r.ts)}</td><td>${U.esc(topicName.get(r.topicId) || "")}</td><td>${U.esc(r.person)}</td><td>${U.esc(r.rule)}</td></tr>`).join("")}</table>`;
  window.print();
};

pages.resetAll = async function () {
  const ok = await ui.confirm({
    title: "Reset everything?",
    msg: "All sources, messages, decisions, topics, reversals and alerts will be deleted. Settings and theme stay.",
    okLabel: "Wipe it", danger: true
  });
  if (!ok) return;
  try {
    await api.post("/reset");
    ui.toast({ msg: "Fresh start. The demo data is one click away.", type: "ok" });
    router.go("/dashboard");
  } catch (e) { ui.toastError(e.message); }
};

pages.undoTopicOp = async function () {
  try {
    const type = await api.post("/topics/undo");
    ui.toast({ msg: type ? `Undid ${type}` : "Nothing to undo", type: type ? "ok" : "info", timeout: 1800 });
  } catch (e) { ui.toastError(e.message); }
};
pages.redoTopicOp = async function () {
  try {
    const type = await api.post("/topics/redo");
    ui.toast({ msg: type ? `Redid ${type}` : "Nothing to redo", type: type ? "ok" : "info", timeout: 1800 });
  } catch (e) { ui.toastError(e.message); }
};

/* ---------------------------------------------------------------
   boot
--------------------------------------------------------------- */
function buildNav() {
  const links = U.$("#nav-links");
  const mobile = U.$("#mobile-menu");
  links.innerHTML = router.pageList().map(p =>
    `<button class="nav-link" data-path="${p.path}" data-label="${p.title}">${p.title}</button>`).join("");
  mobile.innerHTML = router.pageList().map(p =>
    `<a href="#${p.path}" data-path="${p.path}">${p.title}</a>`).join("") +
    `<a href="#/settings" data-path="/settings">Settings</a>`;
  links.querySelectorAll(".nav-link").forEach(b => b.addEventListener("click", () => router.go(b.dataset.path)));
  U.$("#burger").addEventListener("click", () => {
    const m = U.$("#mobile-menu");
    m.classList.toggle("open");
    m.setAttribute("aria-hidden", String(!m.classList.contains("open")));
  });
  U.$("#theme-btn").addEventListener("click", () => theme.cycle());
  U.$("#palette-btn").addEventListener("click", () => palette.toggle());
}

function updateAlertBadge() {
  const badge = U.$("#alert-badge");
  const n = store.state.alerts.filter(a => !a.read).length;
  badge.hidden = !n;
  badge.textContent = n > 9 ? "9+" : n;
}

(async function boot() {
  try {
    conz.init();
    await dbx.open();
    registerRoutes();
    await store.init();

    theme.apply(store.state.settings.theme);
    if (store.state.settings.calm) document.body.classList.add("calm");

    charts.init();
    ui.init();
    cursor.init();
    bg.init();
    buildNav();
    palette.init();
    shortcuts.init();
    router.init();
    tour.init();

    // alert toasts + badge, wired once for the whole session
    bus.on("alert:new", a => {
      ui.toast({
        title: a.title, msg: a.body, alert: true,
        type: a.severity === "critical" ? "error" : "warn", timeout: 7500,
        onClick: () => router.go("/alerts")
      });
    });
    bus.on("data:changed", updateAlertBadge);
    updateAlertBadge();

    // scroll progress + shrinking nav
    const progress = U.$("#scroll-progress"), nav = U.$("#nav");
    const onScroll = () => {
      const max = document.documentElement.scrollHeight - innerHeight;
      progress.style.transform = `scaleX(${max > 0 ? U.clamp(scrollY / max, 0, 1) : 0})`;
      nav.classList.toggle("scrolled", scrollY > 24);
    };
    window.addEventListener("scroll", onScroll, { passive: true });
    onScroll();

    if (store.state.settings.liveFeed && store.stats().decisions) feed.toggle(true);
    conz.log("bus", "boot complete — FlipTrace is yours");
    conz.api("GET /health → 200 {ok:true, storage:" + dbx.mode + "}");

    // a handle for console tinkering: fliptrace.api.get("/state") and friends
    window.fliptrace = { api, store, router, pages, feed, palette, services, bus, conz, theme };
  } catch (err) {
    console.error(err);
    document.body.insertAdjacentHTML("beforeend",
      `<div style="position:fixed;inset:0;z-index:999;display:grid;place-items:center;background:#0a0c1a;color:#e9edff;font-family:system-ui;padding:30px;text-align:center">
        <div><h2 style="font-size:22px">FlipTrace failed to start</h2>
        <p style="color:#9aa5cc">${U.esc(err && err.message || err)}</p></div></div>`);
  }
})();

</script>
